.5)/4.1),near=seg.z<(mobile?lerp(58,85,budget.q):lerp(130,185,budget.q));
 const leftInner0=a.curb.left?V0.lo:V0.l,leftInner1=b.curb.left?V1.lo:V1.l,rightInner0=a.curb.right?V0.ro:V0.r,rightInner1=b.curb.right?V1.ro:V1.r;
 const cuts=(budget.pressure>=2&&!near)?[.34,.68]:(near?[.18,.36,.56,.76]:[.28,.56,.78]);
 const L=[V0.lx,...cuts.map(t=>camMix(V0.lx,leftInner0,t)),leftInner0],LB=[V1.lx,...cuts.map(t=>camMix(V1.lx,leftInner1,t)),leftInner1];
 const R=[rightInner0,...cuts.map(t=>camMix(rightInner0,V0.rx,t)),V0.rx],RB=[rightInner1,...cuts.map(t=>camMix(rightInner1,V1.rx,t)),V1.rx];
 const gravel=['rgba(198,180,138,.20)','rgba(150,133,99,.16)','rgba(111,98,74,.14)','rgba(211,193,151,.17)','rgba(132,116,86,.13)'];
 const asphalt=['rgba(255,255,255,.024)','rgba(0,0,0,.035)','rgba(255,255,255,.017)','rgba(0,0,0,.027)','rgba(255,255,255,.015)'];
 const grass=['rgba(60,101,51,.14)','rgba(126,151,91,.08)','rgba(42,82,40,.12)','rgba(154,173,109,.065)','rgba(48,91,44,.10)'];
 const cols=surf==='GRAVEL'?gravel:(surf==='ASPHALT'?asphalt:grass);
 for(let i=0;i<L.length-1;i++){
   const col=cols[(i+seed)%cols.length];
   webglPushCamQuad(L[i],L[i+1],LB[i+1],LB[i],pitch,col,.56,-.012-i*.0005);
   webglPushCamQuad(R[i],R[i+1],RB[i+1],RB[i],pitch,col,.56,-.012-i*.0005);
 }
 if(near&&surf==='GRAVEL'){
   // Shallow rake bands break up the large flat gravel polygons without textures.
   const t=.46,la0=camMix(V0.lx,leftInner0,t),la1=camMix(V1.lx,leftInner1,t),ra0=camMix(rightInner0,V0.rx,t),ra1=camMix(rightInner1,V1.rx,t);
   const w=.035+(seed%3)*.012;
   webglPushCamQuad(camMix(V0.lx,leftInner0,t-w),camMix(V0.lx,leftInner0,t+w),camMix(V1.lx,leftInner1,t+w),camMix(V1.lx,leftInner1,t-w),pitch,'rgba(75,66,51,.16)',.56,-.016);
   webglPushCamQuad(camMix(rightInner0,V0.rx,t-w),camMix(rightInner0,V0.rx,t+w),camMix(rightInner1,V1.rx,t+w),camMix(rightInner1,V1.rx,t-w),pitch,'rgba(75,66,51,.16)',.56,-.016);
 }
}

function webglPushRoadSegment(seg,cam,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,env=a.env,V0=a.v,V1=b.v;if(!V0||!V1)return;
 const seed=Math.floor(((a.s+b.s)*.5)/2.4),leftInner0=a.curb.left?V0.lo:V0.l,leftInner1=b.curb.left?V1.lo:V1.l,rightInner0=a.curb.right?V0.ro:V0.r,rightInner1=b.curb.right?V1.ro:V1.r;
 // Escapatorias / terreno inmediato: materiales genéricos; las superficies especiales proceden del SVG.
 if(apexInspectionActive())apexInspectorDrawRunoff(seg,cam,pitch,true);else{
 webglPushCamQuad(V0.lx,leftInner0,leftInner1,V1.lx,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,false));
 webglPushCamQuad(rightInner0,V0.rx,V1.rx,rightInner1,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,true));
 webglPushRunoffDetail(seg,pitch);
 }
 // Pianos alternos: paleta genérica o definida en JSON.
 const [curbA,curbB]=circuitCurbPair(seed);
 if(((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().curbMax)&&(a.curb.left||b.curb.left))webglPushCamQuad(V0.lo,V0.l,V1.l,V1.lo,pitch,curbA);
 if(((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().curbMax)&&(a.curb.right||b.curb.right))webglPushCamQuad(V0.r,V0.ro,V1.ro,V1.r,pitch,curbB);
 // Asfalto: a FPS normal conservamos las tres bandas. En móvil por debajo de ~14 FPS
 // el coste estaba en CONSTRUIR geometría JS, no en enviarla a WebGL; usamos un solo
 // quad en distancia media/lejana y mantenemos detalle completo junto al coche.
 const road=webglCircuitRoadColor(seg),renderP=mobileRenderPressure(),cheapRoad=mobilePerformanceMode()&&!(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())&&renderP<.34&&seg.z>42;
 if(cheapRoad){
   webglPushCamQuad(V0.l,V0.r,V1.r,V1.l,pitch,road);
 }else{
   const l30a=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.30),l30b=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.30),r30a=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.70),r30b=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.70);
   webglPushCamQuad(V0.l,l30a,l30b,V1.l,pitch,shadeColor(road,-3));
   webglPushCamQuad(l30a,r30a,r30b,l30b,pitch,shadeColor(road,2));
   webglPushCamQuad(r30a,V0.r,V1.r,r30b,pitch,shadeColor(road,-4));
 }
 const _wetRoad=apexWeatherWetFactor();if(_wetRoad>.06&&seg.z>5&&seg.z<220&&(!mobilePerformanceMode()||renderP>.22||seg.z<46)){const wa=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.16),wb=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.23),wc=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.23),wd=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.16);webglPushCamQuad(wa,wb,wc,wd,pitch,`rgba(205,225,238,${(.012+.052*_wetRoad).toFixed(3)})`,.56,-.012);}
 // Huella central y brillo lateral sólo cuando el presupuesto lo permite.
 if(seg.z>5&&seg.z<260&&(!mobilePerformanceMode()||renderP>.30||seg.z<38)){
   const g0a=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.43),g1a=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.57),g0b=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.43),g1b=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.57);
   webglPushCamQuad(g0a,g1a,g1b,g0b,pitch,"rgba(10,13,16,.07)");
   const h0a=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.18),h1a=camMix(V0.l,V0.r,.25),h0b=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.18),h1b=camMix(V1.l,V1.r,.25);
   webglPushCamQuad(h0a,h1a,h1b,h0b,pitch,"rgba(255,255,255,.025)");
 }
 // Líneas blancas de borde.
 if(seg.z>4&&((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())||seg.z<mobileControlProfile().edgeMax)){
   const e=.016;webglPushCamQuad(V0.l,camMix(V0.l,V0.r,e),camMix(V1.l,V1.r,e),V1.l,pitch,"rgba(248,249,247,.94)",.58,-.025);
   webglPushCamQuad(camMix(V0.r,V0.l,e),V0.r,V1.r,camMix(V1.r,V1.l,e),pitch,"rgba(248,249,247,.94)",.58,-.025);
 }
}
function webglPushNearPatch(cam,pitch){
 if(!player||!trackData)return;
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(player.s),env=envProfile(player.s),roadHalf=env.half,outerL=apexRoadFlatLimit(player.s,env,1),outerR=apexRoadFlatLimit(player.s,env,-1),bank=Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0;
 const make=(along,lat,yOff=0)=>worldToCamera(q.x+q.tx*along+q.ox*lat,q.y+Math.tan(bank)*clamp(lat,-roadHalf,roadHalf)+yOff,q.z+q.tz*along+q.oz*lat,cam);
 // Only fills the tiny near-camera hole. It sits slightly farther in the depth buffer,
 // so the authored road/terrain segments always win wherever they overlap.
 const a=-4.5,b=7.0,bias=.42;
 const lOa=make(a,outerL),lOb=make(b,outerL),lRa=make(a,roadHalf),lRb=make(b,roadHalf);
 const rRa=make(a,-roadHalf),rRb=make(b,-roadHalf),rOa=make(a,-outerR),rOb=make(b,-outerR);
 webglPushCamQuad(lOa,lRa,lRb,lOb,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,false),.34,bias);
 webglPushCamQuad(rRa,rOa,rOb,rRb,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,true),.34,bias);
 webglPushCamQuad(lRa,rRa,rRb,lRb,pitch,shadeColor(palette().road,-2),.34,bias);
}
function webglCollectCircuitSegments(cam,pitch){
 const near=.64,mp=mobileControlProfile(),mainSign=(player&&player.reverse)?-1:1,inspectionFull=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene()),fullD=inspectionFull&&trackData?trackData.km*1000*.515:0,branches=inspectionFull?[collectRoadBranch(1,cam,pitch,fullD,near),collectRoadBranch(-1,cam,pitch,fullD,near)]:mobilePerformanceMode()?[collectRoadBranch(mainSign,cam,pitch,mp.roadForward,near),collectRoadBranch(-mainSign,cam,pitch,mp.roadRear,near)]:[collectRoadBranch(1,cam,pitch,mp.roadForward,near),collectRoadBranch(-1,cam,pitch,mp.roadRear,near)],segments=[];
 for(let bi=0;bi<branches.length;bi++){const steps=branches[bi];for(let i=0;i<steps.length-1;i++){const a=steps[i],b=steps[i+1];if(!roadSegmentIsSane(a,b))continue;segments.push({a,b,z:roadSegmentDepth(a,b,near),branch:bi})}}
 return segments;
}
function webglVecNorm(v){const l=Math.hypot(v.x||0,v.y||0,v.z||0)||1;return{x:(v.x||0)/l,y:(v.y||0)/l,z:(v.z||0)/l};}
function webglVecCross(a,b){return{x:(a.y||0)*(b.z||0)-(a.z||0)*(b.y||0),y:(a.z||0)*(b.x||0)-(a.x||0)*(b.z||0),z:(a.x||0)*(b.y||0)-(a.y||0)*(b.x||0)};}
function webglRivalBasis(a){
 const q=a.q,side=a.r.lat>=0?1:-1,lat=Math.abs(a.r.lat||0),base=ribbonWorldPoint(q,lat,side,.014),rh=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz)+(a.r.yawKick||0);
 const F=webglVecNorm({x:Math.sin(rh),y:0,z:Math.cos(rh)});
 let R=webglVecNorm({x:q.ox,y:Math.tan(q.bank||0),z:q.oz});
 let U=webglVecNorm(webglVecCross(R,F));
 if(U.y<0)U={x:-U.x,y:-U.y,z:-U.z};
 R=webglVecNorm(webglVecCross(F,U));
 return{base,F,R,U};
}
function webglRivalLocal(b,lx,ly,lz){return{x:b.base.x+b.R.x*lx+b.U.x*ly+b.F.x*lz,y:b.base.y+b.R.y*lx+b.U.y*ly+b.F.y*lz,z:b.base.z+b.R.z*lx+b.U.z*ly+b.F.z*lz};}
function webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,A,B,C,D,color,near=.56,depthBias=0){
 return webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(A.x,A.y,A.z,cam),worldToCamera(B.x,B.y,B.z,cam),worldToCamera(C.x,C.y,C.z,cam),worldToCamera(D.x,D.y,D.z,cam),pitch,color,near,depthBias);
}
function webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,pts,color,near=.56,depthBias=0){return webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,pts[0],pts[1],pts[2],pts[3],color,near,depthBias);}
function webglPushRivalLocalBox(b,cam,pitch,cx,cy,cz,sx,sy,sz,cols={}){
 const x0=cx-sx*.5,x1=cx+sx*.5,y0=cy-sy*.5,y1=cy+sy*.5,z0=cz-sz*.5,z1=cz+sz*.5;
 const p=(x,y,z)=>webglRivalLocal(b,x,y,z);
 const main=cols.main||'#d0d4d8',top=cols.top||main,bottom=cols.bottom||shadeColor(main,-35),left=cols.left||shadeColor(main,-16),right=cols.right||shadeColor(main,-12),front=cols.front||shadeColor(main,10),back=cols.back||shadeColor(main,-22);
 let tris=0;
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,[p(x0,y1,z0),p(x1,y1,z0),p(x1,y1,z1),p(x0,y1,z1)],top,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,[p(x0,y0,z1),p(x1,y0,z1),p(x1,y0,z0),p(x0,y0,z0)],bottom,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,[p(x0,y0,z0),p(x0,y1,z0),p(x0,y1,z1),p(x0,y0,z1)],left,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,[p(x1,y0,z1),p(x1,y1,z1),p(x1,y1,z0),p(x1,y0,z0)],right,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,[p(x0,y0,z1),p(x0,y1,z1),p(x1,y1,z1),p(x1,y0,z1)],front,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalQuad(b,cam,pitch,[p(x1,y0,z0),p(x1,y1,z0),p(x0,y1,z0),p(x0,y0,z0)],back,.56,-.010);
 return tris;
}
function webglRivalVariant(teamKey){
 const key=String(teamKey||'base');let h=0;for(let i=0;i<key.length;i++)h=(h*33+key.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;
 const n=v=>((h>>v)&255)/255;
 return{
  noseLen:1.54+n(0)*.20,
  frontWing:1.78+n(8)*.18,
  rearWing:1.42+n(16)*.18,
  sidepodWidth:.34+n(4)*.08,
  engineHeight:.22+n(12)*.08,
  wheelbase:2.84+n(20)*.16,
  colorMix:n(24)
 };
}
function webglPushRivalShadow(b,cam,pitch,len=3.48,width=1.56,alpha=.22){
 const y=.004,zF=len*.5,zR=-len*.5,xL=-width*.5,xR=width*.5,p=(x,z)=>webglRivalLocal(b,x,y,z),aa=alpha*apexWeatherShadowStrength();
 return webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,p(xL,zR),p(xR,zR),p(xR,zF),p(xL,zF),'rgba(0,0,0,'+aa.toFixed(3)+')',.56,.020);
}

function webglPushRivalFrustum3D(b,cam,pitch,cx,cy,cz,len,wFront,wRear,hFront,hRear,cols={}){
 const zF=cz+len*.5,zR=cz-len*.5;
 const p=(x,y,z)=>webglRivalLocal(b,x,y,z);
 const A0=p(cx-wFront*.5,cy-hFront*.5,zF),A1=p(cx+wFront*.5,cy-hFront*.5,zF),A2=p(cx+wFront*.5,cy+hFront*.5,zF),A3=p(cx-wFront*.5,cy+hFront*.5,zF);
 const B0=p(cx-wRear*.5,cy-hRear*.5,zR),B1=p(cx+wRear*.5,cy-hRear*.5,zR),B2=p(cx+wRear*.5,cy+hRear*.5,zR),B3=p(cx-wRear*.5,cy+hRear*.5,zR);
 const main=cols.main||'#d0d4d8',top=cols.top||shadeColor(main,12),bottom=cols.bottom||shadeColor(main,-40),left=cols.left||shadeColor(main,-18),right=cols.right||shadeColor(main,-14),front=cols.front||shadeColor(main,10),back=cols.back||shadeColor(main,-24);
 let tris=0;
 tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,A3,A2,B2,B3,top,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,B0,B1,A1,A0,bottom,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,A0,A3,B3,B0,left,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,B1,B2,A2,A1,right,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,A0,A1,A2,A3,front,.56,-.010);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,B1,B0,B3,B2,back,.56,-.010);
 return tris;
}
function webglPushRivalRod3D(b,cam,pitch,x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,thick,color){
 const mx=(x0+x1)*.5,my=(y0+y1)*.5,mz=(z0+z1)*.5;
 const dx=x1-x0,dy=y1-y0,dz=z1-z0;
 // Axis-aligned local box approximation is enough for suspension rods at this scale.
 return webglPushRivalLocalBox(b,cam,pitch,mx,my,mz,Math.max(thick,Math.abs(dx)+thick),Math.max(thick,Math.abs(dy)+thick),Math.max(thick,Math.abs(dz)+thick),{main:color,top:shadeColor(color,18)});
}
function webglRivalLod(a){
 if(!a)return 0;
 if(Number.isFinite(a._webglLod))return clamp(Math.round(a._webglLod),0,2);
 return Number.isFinite(a.visualLod)?clamp(Math.round(a.visualLod),0,2):apexRivalVisualLod(a.z,a.p&&a.p.scale);
}

function webglPushRivalWheel3D(b,cam,pitch,cx,cy,cz,halfW,radius,teamKey,spin=0,lod=1){
 const brand=teamBranding(teamKey)||{},tyre='#0c0f13',rim=lod>=2?'#303842':'#252b32',hub=lod>=2?'#9ca5af':'#69727c',stripe=brand.tyreColor||'#f0cf34',N=lod>=2?14:(lod===1?10:6);
 const left=[],right=[];
 for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
   const ang=spin+i*Math.PI*2/N,s=Math.sin(ang),c=Math.cos(ang);
   left.push(webglRivalLocal(b,cx-halfW,cy+s*radius,cz+c*radius));
   right.push(webglRivalLocal(b,cx+halfW,cy+s*radius,cz+c*radius));
 }
 let tris=0;
 for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const j=(i+1)%N;tris+=webglPushRivalWorldQuad(cam,pitch,left[i],right[i],right[j],left[j],tyre,.56,-.004);}
 const cL=webglRivalLocal(b,cx-halfW,cy,cz),cR=webglRivalLocal(b,cx+halfW,cy,cz);
 const r2=radius*(lod>=2?.67:.72),ring=radius*.92;
 for(const sidePack of [[cL,left,-1],[cR,right,1]]){
   const [cc,edge,sg]=sidePack,disc=[],outer=[];
   for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
     const ang=spin+i*Math.PI*2/N,s=Math.sin(ang),c=Math.cos(ang);
     disc.push(webglRivalLocal(b,cx+sg*(halfW+.002),cy+s*r2,cz+c*r2));
     outer.push(webglRivalLocal(b,cx+sg*(halfW+.003),cy+s*ring,cz+c*ring));
   }
   for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
     const j=(i+1)%N;
     tris+=webglPushCamPoly([worldToCamera(cc.x,cc.y,cc.z,cam),worldToCamera(disc[i].x,disc[i].y,disc[i].z,cam),worldToCamera(disc[j].x,disc[j].y,disc[j].z,cam)],pitch,rim,.56,-.006);
     if(lod>=1)tris+=webglPushCamPoly([worldToCamera(cc.x,cc.y,cc.z,cam),worldToCamera(outer[i].x,outer[i].y,outer[i].z,cam),worldToCamera(outer[j].x,outer[j].y,outer[j].z,cam)],pitch,stripe,.56,-.008);
   }
   if(lod>=2){
     const hubR=radius*.20,hubPts=[];
     for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const ang=i*Math.PI*2/N;hubPts.push(webglRivalLocal(b,cx+sg*(halfW+.004),cy+Math.sin(ang)*hubR,cz+Math.cos(ang)*hubR));}
     for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const j=(i+1)%N;tris+=webglPushCamPoly([worldToCamera(cc.x,cc.y,cc.z,cam),worldToCamera(hubPts[i].x,hubPts[i].y,hubPts[i].z,cam),worldToCamera(hubPts[j].x,hubPts[j].y,hubPts[j].z,cam)],pitch,hub,.56,-.010);}
   }
 }
 return tris;
}
function webglRivalUse3D(a){return !!(a&&a.z>2.2&&a.p&&a.p.scale>.35);}
function webglPrepareRivalVisuals(items){
 const cfg=apexRivalLodSettings();let fullCount=0,midCount=0;
 // collectRivalSprites viene ordenado lejos -> cerca. Recorremos al revés para que
 // los coches realmente próximos reciban primero el presupuesto de detalle.
 for(let i=items.length-1;i>=0;i--){
   const a=items[i],wanted=Number.isFinite(a.visualLod)?clamp(Math.round(a.visualLod),0,2):apexRivalVisualLod(a.z,a.p&&a.p.scale);let lod=wanted;
   if(lod>=2){if(fullCount<cfg.fullBudget){fullCount++;}else lod=1;}
   if(lod===1){if(midCount<cfg.midBudget){midCount++;}else lod=0;}
   a._webglLod=lod;a._webgl3d=webglRivalUse3D(a);
   if(a._webgl3d){apexRivalLodFrameStats.gpu3d++;if(lod>=2)apexRivalLodFrameStats.gpuFull++;else if(lod===1)apexRivalLodFrameStats.gpuMid++;else apexRivalLodFrameStats.gpuLow++;}
   else apexRivalLodFrameStats.canvasFallback++;
 }
 return items;
}
function webglPushRivalCar3D(a,cam,pitch){
 const team=teamInfo(a.r.team),primary=team.color||'#d43b42',accent=team.accent||shadeColor(primary,28),light=shadeColor(primary,18),dark=shadeColor(primary,-30),carbon='#090c10',carbon2='#161c22',metal='#7d8793',basis=webglRivalBasis(a),speed=Number(a.r.speed)||0,spin=raceT*(speed*.018+2.4),v=webglRivalVariant(a.r.team),lod=webglRivalLod(a);
 const wb=v.wheelbase,frontZ=wb*.52,rearZ=-wb*.48;
 let tris=0;

 // Grounding
 tris+=webglPushRivalShadow(basis,cam,pitch,wb+1.22,lod>=2?1.76:1.66,lod>=2?.24:.20);

 // LOD 0: silueta 3D barata y estable. Mantiene el rival visible sin construir
 // toda la suspensión, cockpit, halo y elementos aerodinámicos lejanos.
 if(lod===0){
   tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.25,-.10,2.25,.62,.84,.18,.28,{main:primary,top:light,left:dark,right:dark,front:accent,back:shadeColor(primary,-22)});
   tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.16,1.25,1.05,.18,.42,.07,.16,{main:primary,top:light,front:accent,back:dark});
   tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.095,frontZ+.55,v.frontWing,.035,.18,{main:accent,top:shadeColor(accent,14)});
   tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.70,rearZ-.60,v.rearWing,.05,.20,{main:accent,top:shadeColor(accent,12),back:dark});
   tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.92,.31,frontZ,.16,.33,a.r.team,spin,0);
   tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,.92,.31,frontZ,.16,.33,a.r.team,spin,0);
   tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.88,.33,rearZ,.18,.39,a.r.team,spin,0);
   tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,.88,.33,rearZ,.18,.39,a.r.team,spin,0);
   return tris;
 }

 // Floor / plank
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.055,-.02,wb+1.04,1.20,1.44,.05,.055,{main:carbon,top:'#1d2329',left:'#0e1318',right:'#0e1318',front:'#161c21',back:'#05070a'});
 if(lod>=1)tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.020,-.02,.22,.018,wb+1.30,{main:'#8a6336',top:'#a57942'});

 // Sidepods with taper: narrower front, wider rear
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.48,.27,-.12,1.88,.28,.42,.18,.27,{main:primary,top:light,left:dark,front:accent,back:shadeColor(primary,-16)});
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,.48,.27,-.12,1.88,.28,.42,.18,.27,{main:primary,top:light,right:dark,front:accent,back:shadeColor(primary,-16)});
 // Coke-bottle rear taper
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.29,-.98,.96,.58,.36,.24,.22,{main:shadeColor(primary,-5),top:light,back:dark});

 // Nose: real F1-style long taper
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.22,.72,1.28,.31,.48,.10,.18,{main:primary,top:light,front:accent,back:dark});
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.16,1.62,.78,.16,.31,.07,.10,{main:primary,top:light,front:accent,back:dark});
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.11,2.17,.34,.10,.16,.05,.07,{main:accent,top:shadeColor(accent,18),front:shadeColor(accent,10)});

 // Cockpit and halo
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.38,-.24,.76,.46,.38,.16,.20,{main:carbon2,top:'#262e36',front:'#20272e',back:'#0e1318'});
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.59,-.08,.055,.28,.31,{main:carbon2,top:'#38414a'});
 tris+=webglPushRivalRod3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.16,.51,-.34,-.16,.59,.07,.025,carbon2);
 tris+=webglPushRivalRod3D(basis,cam,pitch,.16,.51,-.34,.16,.59,.07,.025,carbon2);
 tris+=webglPushRivalRod3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.16,.59,.06,.16,.59,.06,.025,carbon2);

 // Airbox / engine cover
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.49,-.88,1.10,.31,.44,.20,.34,{main:primary,top:light,back:dark});
 tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.70,-.72,.30,.14,.22,.12,.20,{main:carbon2,top:'#36404a'});

 // Front wing: multi-element according to distance
 const fw=v.frontWing,fwZ=frontZ+.57;
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.095,fwZ,fw,.036,.18,{main:accent,top:shadeColor(accent,18),front:shadeColor(accent,8)});
 if(lod>=1)tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.067,fwZ+.16,fw*1.03,.028,.14,{main:shadeColor(accent,8),top:shadeColor(accent,20)});
 if(lod>=2)tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.050,fwZ+.29,fw*.92,.020,.10,{main:carbon2,top:'#242b31'});
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,-fw*.49,.16,fwZ+.04,.045,.18,.20,{main:carbon});
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,fw*.49,.16,fwZ+.04,.045,.18,.20,{main:carbon});

 // Rear wing / beam wing / diffuser
 const rw=v.rearWing,rwZ=rearZ-.62;
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.72,rwZ,rw,.055,.22,{main:shadeColor(accent,6),top:shadeColor(accent,16),back:shadeColor(accent,-18)});
 if(lod>=1)tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.86,rwZ-.05,rw*.89,.045,.16,{main:carbon2,top:'#38424c',back:'#0c1116'});
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,-rw*.45,.76,rwZ,.045,.34,.14,{main:carbon});
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,rw*.45,.76,rwZ,.045,.34,.14,{main:carbon});
 if(lod>=1)tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.24,rearZ-.31,.58,.07,.30,{main:carbon,back:'#040609'});
 if(lod>=2){
   tris+=webglPushRivalFrustum3D(basis,cam,pitch,0,.12,rearZ-.48,.50,.74,.50,.04,.09,{main:'#10151a',top:'#1b2229',back:'#05070a'});
   for(const sx of [-.24,-.12,0,.12,.24])tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,sx,.13,rearZ-.48,.025,.16,.42,{main:'#11171d'});
 }

 // Mirrors
 if(lod>=1){
   tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,-.43,.45,.49,.13,.055,.10,{main:accent,top:light});
   tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,.43,.45,.49,.13,.055,.10,{main:accent,top:light});
 }

 // Team stripe / livery geometry (not image)
 tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.31,-.01,.085,.025,2.28,{main:accent,top:shadeColor(accent,18)});

 // Wheels
 tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.92,.31,frontZ,.16,.33,a.r.team,spin,lod);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,.92,.31,frontZ,.16,.33,a.r.team,spin,lod);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,-.88,.33,rearZ,.18,.39,a.r.team,spin,lod);
 tris+=webglPushRivalWheel3D(basis,cam,pitch,.88,.33,rearZ,.18,.39,a.r.team,spin,lod);

 // Suspension only when it will actually be visible
 if(lod>=1){
   for(const sg of [-1,1]){
     tris+=webglPushRivalRod3D(basis,cam,pitch,sg*.30,.20,frontZ-.18,sg*.76,.29,frontZ,.018,metal);
     tris+=webglPushRivalRod3D(basis,cam,pitch,sg*.30,.29,frontZ+.05,sg*.76,.29,frontZ,.018,metal);
     tris+=webglPushRivalRod3D(basis,cam,pitch,sg*.34,.22,rearZ+.18,sg*.72,.31,rearZ,.020,metal);
   }
 }
 if(lod>=2){
   // Brake ducts / wheel fairing hints and rear rain light body
   for(const sg of [-1,1]){
     tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,sg*.70,.28,frontZ-.06,.10,.16,.12,{main:'#11161b',top:'#262d34'});
     tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,sg*.67,.30,rearZ+.02,.11,.18,.12,{main:'#11161b',top:'#262d34'});
   }
   const flash=apexWeatherIsWet()?(Math.sin(raceT*24+a.r.id*.43)>-.05):true;
   tris+=webglPushRivalLocalBox(basis,cam,pitch,0,.20,rearZ-.56,.12,.12,.06,{main:flash?'#ff263f':'#741625',top:flash?'#ff7180':'#8c2230',back:'#5f111d'});
 }

 return tris;
}
function webglPushRivalField3D(items,cam,pitch){
 let tris=0;for(const a of items||[]){if(a._webgl3d)tris+=webglPushRivalCar3D(a,cam,pitch);}return tris;
}
function webglDrawCircuitCanvasOverlay(cam,pitch,segments,rivalItems=null){
 // v5.230.62.4: en backend WebGL los rivales nunca se dibujan como overlay 2D.
 // TODOS pasan por el z-buffer 3D, de modo que terreno, muros, boxes, gradas,
 // edificios y puentes pueden ocultarlos correctamente. Canvas sólo conserva guías DEV.
 if(renderLimitGuides)for(const seg of segments)drawPhysicalLimitGuidesOnSegment(seg.a,seg.b,cam,pitch);
 return 0;
}
function webglSubmit(){
 const gl=webglState.gl;if(!gl||!webglState.ready)return false;const t0=performance.now();gl.viewport(0,0,webglCv.width,webglCv.height);gl.clearColor(.02,.03,.05,1);gl.clearDepth(1.0);gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT|gl.DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);gl.useProgram(webglState.program);gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,webglState.buffer);
 const data=webglVertexData.subarray(0,webglFloatCount),needBytes=data.byteLength;if(!webglState.bufferBytes||needBytes>webglState.bufferBytes){let cap=Math.max(65536,webglState.bufferBytes||0);while(cap<needBytes)cap*=2;gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,cap,gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);webglState.bufferBytes=cap;apexStaticBatchPerf.bufferGrows++;}if(needBytes)gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,0,data);const stride=28;gl.enableVertexAttribArray(webglState.aPos);gl.vertexAttribPointer(webglState.aPos,3,gl.FLOAT,false,stride,0);gl.enableVertexAttribArray(webglState.aColor);gl.vertexAttribPointer(webglState.aColor,4,gl.FLOAT,false,stride,12);if(webglFloatCount)gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES,0,webglFloatCount/7);
 webglState.triangles=Math.floor(webglFloatCount/21);telemetryPerf.webglTriangles=webglState.triangles;renderProfileAdd("webglSubmitMs",performance.now()-t0);return true;
}
function webgl2RenderFrame(){
 renderFrameId++;renderProfileReset();
 if(!webglInit()){setRendererBackend("canvas2d",{notify:false,persist:false});return canvas2dRenderFrame()}
 webglClearCanvasOverlay();webglFloatCount=0;webglScreenRoll=0;
 const pitch=trackData&&player?driverCamera().pitch:0;
 if(!trackData||!player){renderProfileMeasure("environmentMs",()=>webglPushBackground(null,0));webglSubmit();renderProfileCommit();return}
 const cam=driverCamera(),viewRoll=impactFX.roll+finite(cam.roadRoll,0),visual=webglCircuitVisualAvailable(),authoredBackdrop=webglUsesAuthoredWorldBackdrop(),trackT0=performance.now();
 renderProfileMeasure("environmentMs",()=>apexInspectionActive()?webglPushBackground(cam,cam.pitch):authoredBackdrop?webglPushCircuitBackground(cam,cam.pitch):webglPushBackground(cam,cam.pitch));
 let segments=null,rivalVisuals=null;
 if(visual){
   webglScreenRoll=viewRoll;
   segments=webglCollectCircuitSegments(cam,cam.pitch);
   renderProfileMeasure("environmentMs",()=>{webglPushCircuitLandmarks(cam,cam.pitch);if(trackData.gradeCrossing)webglPushGradeCrossingEmbankments(cam,cam.pitch)});
   if(apexPlateTerrainGrid())webglPushTerrainPlateMesh(cam,cam.pitch);
   const terrainSegments=segments.slice().sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);for(const seg of terrainSegments)if(!apexInspectionActive()||!apexPlateTerrainGrid())webglPushTerrainSegment(seg,cam,cam.pitch);apexWebglFallbackStructureBanks(terrainSegments,cam,cam.pitch);
   if(!apexInspectionActive())webglPushNearPatch(cam,cam.pitch);const roadSegments=segments.slice().sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);for(const seg of roadSegments)webglPushRoadSegment(seg,cam,cam.pitch);
   renderProfileMeasure("environmentMs",()=>{webglPushSurfaceMarkings(roadSegments,cam,cam.pitch);webglPushCircuitWalls(segments,cam,cam.pitch);webglPushExactWallFences(cam,cam.pitch);webglPushTunnelDetails(segments,cam,cam.pitch);apexWebglGenericPortalFaces(cam,cam.pitch);webglPushBrakeBoards(cam,cam.pitch);if(trackData.gradeCrossing)webglPushGradeCrossingOverpass(cam,cam.pitch)});
   rivalVisuals=webglPrepareRivalVisuals(collectRivalSprites(cam,cam.pitch));
   renderProfileMeasure("carsMs",()=>webglPushRivalField3D(rivalVisuals,cam,cam.pitch));
   webglScreenRoll=0;
 }
 webglSubmit();
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(W*.5,H*.5);ctx.rotate(viewRoll);ctx.translate(-W*.5,-H*.5);
 if(visual){webglDrawCircuitCanvasOverlay(cam,cam.pitch,segments,rivalVisuals);renderProfileAdd("trackMs",Math.max(0,performance.now()-trackT0));}
 renderProfileMeasure("effectsMs",()=>{if(!mobilePerformanceMode()||impactParticles.length<18)drawImpactParticles(cam,cam.pitch);drawRain()});ctx.restore();
 
 if(apexInspectionActive())renderProfileMeasure("cockpitGrossMs",()=>apexDrawInspectionOverlays(cam,cam.pitch));else renderProfileMeasure("cockpitGrossMs",()=>drawResponsiveCockpitLayer());renderProfileCommit();
}
function webgl2DrawMiniMap(){return canvas2dDrawMiniMap()}
registerRenderer({id:"webgl2",label:"WebGL2 VISUAL",available:()=>webgl2Available(),activate:()=>webglActivate(),deactivate:()=>webglDeactivate(),renderFrame:()=>webgl2RenderFrame(),drawMiniMap:()=>webgl2DrawMiniMap()});
const TELEMETRY_RECORD_KEY="apex26.telemetry.recordMode.v1";
let telemetryRecordMode=null,sessionTelemetryEventNext=0;
function telemetryDefaultRecordMode(){return mobilePerformanceMode()?"EVENTS":"FULL"}
function getTelemetryRecordMode(){
 if(telemetryRecordMode)return telemetryRecordMode;
 try{const v=String(appStorage.getItem(TELEMETRY_RECORD_KEY)||"").toUpperCase();if(["OFF","EVENTS","FULL"].includes(v))telemetryRecordMode=v;}catch(e){}
 if(!telemetryRecordMode)telemetryRecordMode=telemetryDefaultRecordMode();
 return telemetryRecordMode;
}
function telemetryFullSampleHz(){return mobilePerformanceMode()?5:10}
function telemetryEventCheckHz(){return mobilePerformanceMode()?10:20}
function telemetryRecordingEnabled(){return getTelemetryRecordMode()!=="OFF"}
function telemetryFullRecording(){return getTelemetryRecordMode()==="FULL"}
function telemetryEventAllowed(type,data={}){
 if(getTelemetryRecordMode()!=="EVENTS")return true;
 if(type==="RIVAL_INTENT")return false;
 if(type==="RIVAL_RIVAL_CONTACT")return (Number(data.severity)||0)>=.04||(Number(data.closingKph)||0)>=14;
 if(type==="RIVAL_INCIDENT")return data.mode==="SPIN"||(Number(data.severity)||0)>=.035;
 if(type==="RIVAL_RECOVER")return (Number(data.damage)||100)<94;
 return true;
}
function telemetryRecordLabel(mode=getTelemetryRecordMode()){
 return mode==="FULL"?`COMPLETA · ${telemetryFullSampleHz()} Hz`:mode==="EVENTS"?"EVENTOS":"OFF";
}
function updateTelemetryRecordingUI(){
 const mode=getTelemetryRecordMode(),b=$("telemetryRecordBtn"),hint=$("telemetryHint"),tel=$("telemetryBtn");
 if(b){b.textContent="GRABAR · "+telemetryRecordLabel(mode);b.classList.remove("off","events","full");b.classList.add(mode.toLowerCase());b.title=mode==="FULL"?"Graba muestras completas y eventos":mode==="EVENTS"?"Graba sólo eventos importantes, con menor consumo":"No guarda telemetría";}
 if(hint)hint.textContent=mode==="FULL"?`Grabación completa ${telemetryFullSampleHz()} Hz · TEL sólo muestra/oculta.`:mode==="EVENTS"?"Sólo eventos importantes · recomendado en móvil · TEL sólo muestra/oculta.":"Grabación desactivada · TEL puede seguir mostrando datos en directo.";
 if(tel)tel.title=`Mostrar/ocultar telemetría · Grabación ${telemetryRecordLabel(mode)}`;
}
function setTelemetryRecordMode(mode,notify=true){
 mode=String(mode||"").toUpperCase();if(!["OFF","EVENTS","FULL"].includes(mode))return;
 const old=getTelemetryRecordMode();telemetryRecordMode=mode;
 try{appStorage.setItem(TELEMETRY_RECORD_KEY,mode)}catch(e){}
 sessionTelemetryNext=raceT||0;sessionTelemetryEventNext=raceT||0;
 if(sessionTelemetry){sessionTelemetry.recording=sessionTelemetry.recording||{};sessionTelemetry.recording.currentMode=mode;sessionTelemetry.recording.fullSampleHz=telemetryFullSampleHz();sessionTelemetry.recording.eventCheckHz=telemetryEventCheckHz();sessionTelemetry.recording.changes=sessionTelemetry.recording.changes||[];if(old!==mode)sessionTelemetry.recording.changes.push({t:telemetryRound(raceT||0,3),from:old,to:mode});}
 updateTelemetryRecordingUI();
 if(notify){const a=$("raceAlert");if(a){a.textContent=`TEL · GRABACIÓN ${telemetryRecordLabel(mode)}`;a.style.color=mode==="OFF"?"#ff9cab":mode==="EVENTS"?"#ffe19a":"#9feeff";setTimeout(()=>{if(a.textContent.startsWith("TEL · GRABACIÓN"))a.textContent=""},1500)}}
}
function cycleTelemetryRecordMode(){const mode=getTelemetryRecordMode(),next=mode==="EVENTS"?"FULL":mode==="FULL"?"OFF":"EVENTS";setTelemetryRecordMode(next,true)}
function telemetryRound(v,n=4){return Number.isFinite(v)?+v.toFixed(n):null}
function initSessionTelemetry(spec,gm){
 const recMode=getTelemetryRecordMode();sessionTelemetryNext=0;sessionTelemetryEventNext=0;sessionTelemetryPrev={zone:null,recovery:null,recoveryStage:null,lap:0,wallHit:-99,wallEventAt:-99,rivalContactAt:-99,recoveryEpisodeAt:null,emergency:false,targetReason:null};
 sessionTelemetry={
   schema:"ApexFormula26.SessionTelemetry.v22",appVersion:APP_VERSION,build:APP_BUILD,
   createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),session:{mode:gm,type:currentSession,weather:raceWeather,weatherPreset:sessionSettings&&sessionSettings.weather||null,weatherRuntime:apexWeatherRuntimeSnapshot(),difficulty:sessionSettings?sessionSettings.diff:null,noRivals:!!(sessionSettings&&sessionSettings.noRivals),gridPosition:player?player.pos:null,rivalCount:rivals?rivals.length:0},
   circuit:{round:spec&&spec.r,country:spec&&spec.country,name:spec&&spec.name,dir:spec&&spec.dir,km:trackData&&trackData.km},
   circuitCompile:trackData&&trackData._compileStats?clonePlain(trackData._compileStats):null,
   car:{team:player&&player.teamName,teamKey:player&&player.teamKey,chassis:player&&player.carModel},
   trafficModel:{lookBehindM:42,lookAheadM:70,nearCountLongitudinalM:24,sideLongitudinalM:4.4,sideLateralM:2.45,followLongitudinalM:12,followLateralM:2.15,launchProtectSec:14,rearThreatEscape:true,doubleTrafficLimiter:false,rivalLaunchGapGuard:true,rivalLaunchGapGuardSec:10,rivalLaunchSameLaneGapM:7.0,packSafeGridCorridors:true,gridCorridorReleaseAfterSec:6.6,gridCorridorMaxHoldSec:14.5,substepPlayerRivalCollisions:true,rivalRivalCollisions:true,personalitySafetyMargins:true,postContactCaution:true,softRubFiltering:true,firstLapCornerPassGuard:true,firstComplexLaneLock:true,packContactContainment:true,smoothFrenetTangent:true,geometryKinkGuard:true,continuousDriverReference:true,pathCurvatureSpeedEnvelope:true,purePursuitCleanAir:false,legacyPlayerAutV5149:true,firstChicaneRelease:true,geometryCommit:true,cityLaneSwingGuard:true,rivalKinkCaution:true},
   aiModels:{playerAuto:"FULL_PHYSICS_V5149_RESTORED",rivals:"HYBRID_SHARED_FRENET_LOD_PARITY",sharedCornerPlanner:true,sharedSpeedEnvelope:true,kinematicParityGovernor:true,parityCornerReference:"legacy149CenterlineCurvature",nearDynamicM:150,midHybridM:500,farKinematic:true,mobileNearDynamicM:120,mobileMidHybridM:360,racePaceFloor:.995,racePaceCeil:1.055,incidentAwarePace:true,launchCorridorHold:true,cleanRiskDecay:true,localRiskPenaltyV3:true,riskLearningIncidentOnly:true,racePaceReleaseV2:true,raceSurvivalGovernor:true,firstLapSurvivalBias:true,postImpactLaneHold:true,playerSoftRubFiltering:true,firstComplexLaneLock:true,technicalRiskLinePrep:true,technicalDirectionGuardV2:true,cleanAirSurvivalRelease:true,packContactContainment:true,smoothFrenetTangent:true,geometryKinkGuard:true,continuousDriverReference:true,pathCurvatureSpeedEnvelope:true,purePursuitCleanAir:false,legacyPlayerAutV5149:true,firstChicaneRelease:true,geometryCommit:true,cityLaneSwingGuard:true,rivalKinkCaution:true},
   recoveryModel:{lockedEmergencyPlan:true,lockedExitSide:true,edgeHoldAlignment:true,alignmentGateDeg:15,normalRealignGateDeg:24,lowSpeedTractionAssist:true,hardPullAfterSec:6.5,lateralDivergenceGuard:true,longRecoveryLocalAssist:true},
   renderer:{backend:rendererBackendId(),modularFacade:true,webgl2Prototype:true,hybridCanvasOverlay:true,changes:["v62.4 adaptive mirrors: cached projection, independent refresh budget, distance LOD and landmark occlusion","v62.3.1 rival LOD: 3D full near / 3D medium mid / 3D low-poly far; depth-buffer occlusion"]},telemetryFeatures:{engineerTabs:true,autDecisionChain:true,nearRivalSnapshots:true,incidentWindows:true,performanceStats:true,circuitCompiler:true,persistentCircuitCompileCache:true,rendererWarmup:true,runtimeProfilerV2:true,rivalVisualLod:true,rival3DOnly:true,rivalDepthOcclusion:true,adaptiveMirrors:true,mirrorProjectionCache:true,mirrorLandmarkOcclusion:true,terrainTunnelBreakdown:true,trafficMap:true,relativeMapDriverView:true,launchCorridorDiagnostics:true,substepCarCollisions:true,rivalRivalContacts:true,persistentRivalImpactDynamics:true,rivalImpactStates:true,raceSurvivalDiagnostics:true,racePaceReleaseDiagnostics:true,firstComplexLaneLockDiagnostics:true,packContactContainmentDiagnostics:true,technicalRiskPrepDiagnostics:true,smoothFrenetDiagnostics:true,geometryKinkDiagnostics:true,continuousDriverReference:true,pathCurvatureSpeedEnvelope:true,purePursuitCleanAir:false,legacyPlayerAutV5149:true,geometryCommitDiagnostics:true,laneSwingDiagnostics:true,rivalKinkDiagnostics:true},
   rivalProfiles:(rivals||[]).map(r=>({uid:r.uid||raceIdentity(r.id),name:r.driverName||raceName(r.id),team:r.team,skill:telemetryRound(r.skill,3),aggression:telemetryRound(r.aggression,3),consistency:telemetryRound(r.consistency,3),defense:telemetryRound(r.defense,3),awareness:telemetryRound(r.awareness,3),bravery:telemetryRound(r.bravery,3),reaction:telemetryRound(r.reaction,3),brakeSkill:telemetryRound(r.brakeSkill,3),tractionSkill:telemetryRound(r.tractionSkill,3),cornerSkill:telemetryRound(r.cornerSkill,3),paceProfile:telemetryRound(r.racePaceProfile,4)})),
   recording:{modeAtStart:recMode,currentMode:recMode,adaptiveMobile:true,fullSampleHz:telemetryFullSampleHz(),eventCheckHz:telemetryEventCheckHz(),changes:[]},
   sampleHz:telemetryFullSampleHz(),samples:[],events:[]
 };
 updateTelemetryRecordingUI();
}
function pushTelemetryEvent(type,data={}){
 if(!sessionTelemetry||!telemetryRecordingEnabled()||!telemetryEventAllowed(type,data))return;
 const ev=sessionTelemetry.events||(sessionTelemetry.events=[]);ev.push({t:telemetryRound(raceT,3),lap:player?player.lap+1:null,s:player?telemetryRound(player.s,2):null,type,...data});
 const max=mobilePerformanceMode()?8000:20000;if(ev.length>max)ev.splice(0,Math.min(500,ev.length-max+200));
}
function captureTelemetryEventState(){
 if(!sessionTelemetry||!player||mode!=="race"||!telemetryRecordingEnabled()||raceT+1e-6<sessionTelemetryEventNext)return;
 sessionTelemetryEventNext=raceT+1/telemetryEventCheckHz();
 const prev=sessionTelemetryPrev||{},ps=playerSurfaceState(),rec=player.autoRecovery?player.autoRecovery.mode:null,deg=180/Math.PI;
 let recoveryEpisodeAt=prev.recoveryEpisodeAt;
 if(prev.zone!==ps.zone)pushTelemetryEvent('SURFACE',{from:prev.zone,to:ps.zone});
 if(prev.recovery!==rec){
   if(!prev.recovery&&rec){recoveryEpisodeAt=raceT;pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_START',{mode:rec});}
   else if(prev.recovery&&rec)pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_CHANGE',{from:prev.recovery,to:rec});
   else if(prev.recovery&&!rec){pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_END',{mode:prev.recovery,duration:recoveryEpisodeAt!=null?telemetryRound(raceT-recoveryEpisodeAt,2):null});recoveryEpisodeAt=null;}
 }
 const emergencyNow=!!(player.autoRecovery&&player.autoRecovery.emergency),recoveryStageNow=player.autoRecovery?(player.autoRecovery.emergencyStage||player.autoRecovery.mode):null;
 let headingErr=null;
 if(prev.recoveryStage!==recoveryStageNow&&recoveryStageNow){const tr=player.track||poseAt(player.s);headingErr=angleDiff(Math.atan2(tr.tx,tr.tz),player.heading);pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_STAGE_CHANGE',{from:prev.recoveryStage,to:recoveryStageNow,lat:telemetryRound(player.lat,2),headingErrDeg:telemetryRound(headingErr*deg,1),speedKph:telemetryRound(player.speed*3.6,1)});}
 if(emergencyNow&&!prev.emergency)pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE',{stage:player.autoRecovery.emergencyStage||null,attempts:player.autoRecovery.attempts||0});
 let wallEventAt=prev.wallEventAt||-99;
 if((player.autoLastWallHit||-99)>prev.wallHit+.001&&raceT-(player.autoLastWallHit||-99)<.25&&raceT-wallEventAt>.32){pushTelemetryEvent('WALL_HIT',{side:player.autoLastWallSide||0});wallEventAt=raceT;}
 if(prev.lap!==player.lap)pushTelemetryEvent('LAP_COMPLETE',{completed:player.lap,lastLap:Number.isFinite(player.lastLapTime)?telemetryRound(player.lastLapTime,3):null});
 if(Math.abs(player.yawRate)>.45){if(headingErr==null){const tr=player.track||poseAt(player.s);headingErr=angleDiff(Math.atan2(tr.tx,tr.tz),player.heading);}if(Math.abs(headingErr)>Math.PI*.55&&(!prev.spinAt||raceT-prev.spinAt>2)){pushTelemetryEvent('SPIN',{headingErrDeg:telemetryRound(headingErr*deg,1),speedKph:telemetryRound(player.speed*3.6,1),recentRivalContact:raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<2.5});prev.spinAt=raceT;}}
 const targetReason=player.autoTargetSpeedReason||"TRACK";
 if(prev.targetReason!==targetReason)pushTelemetryEvent('TARGET_LIMIT_CHANGE',{from:prev.targetReason,to:targetReason,detail:player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail||null,baseTrackKph:telemetryRound((player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),targetKph:telemetryRound((player.autoTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),reductionKph:telemetryRound(Math.max(0,(player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)-(player.autoTargetSpeed||0))*3.6,1),preTrafficKph:telemetryRound((player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),trafficLimitKph:Number.isFinite(player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit)?telemetryRound(player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit*3.6,1):null,aheadM:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.d,2):null,aheadSpeedKph:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead?telemetryRound((player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.speed||0)*3.6,1):null,rearThreat:telemetryRound(player.autoRearThreat||0,3),escapeLane:!!player.autoTrafficEscapeLane});
 sessionTelemetryPrev={zone:ps.zone,recovery:rec,recoveryStage:recoveryStageNow,lap:player.lap,wallHit:player.autoLastWallHit||-99,wallEventAt,spinAt:prev.spinAt||0,recoveryEpisodeAt,emergency:emergencyNow,targetReason};
}
function captureSessionTelemetry(){
 if(!sessionTelemetry||!player||mode!=="race")return;
 captureTelemetryEventState();
 if(!telemetryFullRecording()||raceT+1e-6<sessionTelemetryNext)return;
 sessionTelemetryNext=raceT+1/telemetryFullSampleHz();
 const tr=player.track||poseAt(player.s),env=envProfile(player.s),wallCtx=apexWallContextAtS(player.s,env),ps=playerSurfaceState(),deg=180/Math.PI,surface=trackSurfacePoseAt(player.s,player.lat);
 const trackHeading=Math.atan2(tr.tx,tr.tz),headingErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading);
 const autoCtlMode=demoMode?"FULL":assistMode?"SPEED_ASSIST":"OFF";
 const rec=player.autoRecovery?player.autoRecovery.mode:null;
 const sample={
  t:telemetryRound(raceT,3),lap:player.lap+1,lapTime:telemetryRound(Math.max(0,raceT-lapStart),3),
  s:telemetryRound(player.s,2),lapFrac:telemetryRound(mod(player.s,trackData.km*1000)/(trackData.km*1000),5),x:telemetryRound(player.x,3),z:telemetryRound(player.z,3),lat:telemetryRound(player.lat,3),
  timingSector:null,centerElevationM:telemetryRound(surface.centerY,3),surfaceElevationM:telemetryRound(surface.y,3),bankDeg:telemetryRound(surface.bank*deg,3),bankCrossfallPct:telemetryRound(Math.tan(surface.bank)*100,2),
  speedKph:telemetryRound(player.speed*3.6,2),vx:telemetryRound(player.vx,3),vz:telemetryRound(player.vz,3),
  throttle:telemetryRound(player.throttle,3),brake:telemetryRound(player.brakeInput,3),steer:telemetryRound(player.steer,3),steerDeg:telemetryRound((player.delta||0)*deg,2),driveProfile:apexControls.driveProfile,inputDevice:apexInputDevice,tcAssist:!!player.tcAssist,absAssist:!!player.absAssist,
  headingDeg:telemetryRound(player.heading*deg,2),trackHeadingDeg:telemetryRound(trackHeading*deg,2),rawTrackHeadingDeg:telemetryRound(rawTrackHeadingAt(player.s)*deg,2),headingErrDeg:telemetryRound(headingErr*deg,2),trackDirDot:telemetryRound(player.trackDirDot,4),yawRate:telemetryRound(player.yawRate,4),
  slipAngleDeg:telemetryRound((player.slipAngle||0)*deg,3),slipFrontDeg:telemetryRound((player.slipF||0)*deg,3),slipRearDeg:telemetryRound((player.slipR||0)*deg,3),
  latG:telemetryRound(player.latG,3),longG:telemetryRound(player.longG,3),frontUse:telemetryRound(player.frontUse,3),rearUse:telemetryRound(player.rearUse,3),
  downforceN:telemetryRound(player.downforce,1),frontLoadN:telemetryRound(player.frontLoad,1),rearLoadN:telemetryRound(player.rearLoad,1),terrainDrag:telemetryRound(player.terrainDrag,3),
  surfaceZone:ps.zone,surfaceKind:ps.kind,halfWidth:telemetryRound(env.half,2),runoff:telemetryRound(env.runoff,2),wall:!!env.wall,wallAware:!!wallCtx.wallish,nearestSvgWallM:Number.isFinite(wallCtx.nearest)?telemetryRound(wallCtx.nearest,2):null,svgWallPositiveM:Number.isFinite(wallCtx.pos)?telemetryRound(wallCtx.pos,2):null,svgWallNegativeM:Number.isFinite(wallCtx.neg)?telemetryRound(wallCtx.neg,2):null,wallCorridorWidthM:Number.isFinite(wallCtx.corridor)?telemetryRound(wallCtx.corridor,2):null,driveNetwork:player.driveNetwork||'TRACK',pitS:Number.isFinite(player.pitS)?telemetryRound(player.pitS,2):null,
  elevationM:telemetryRound(elevationAt(player.s),2),gradePct:telemetryRound((elevationAt(player.s+5)-elevationAt(player.s-5))*10,2),
  curvature0:telemetryRound(curvatureAt(player.s),6),curvature15:telemetryRound(curvatureAt(player.s+15),6),curvature30:telemetryRound(curvatureAt(player.s+30),6),pathCurvature:telemetryRound(driverReferencePathCurvatureAt(player.s),6),pathLaneM:telemetryRound(driverReferenceLaneAt(player.s),3),pathLocalSafeKph:telemetryRound(driverReferenceSafeSpeedAt(player.s)*3.6,1),pathBrakeTargetKph:telemetryRound(driverReferenceTargetSpeedAt(player.s)*3.6,1),safeSpeedKph:telemetryRound(cornerSafeSpeed(player.s)*3.6,1),autoPhysicalSpeedKph:telemetryRound((player.autoPhysicalNow||autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(player.s))*3.6,1),
  baseTrackTargetKph:telemetryRound((player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),preTrafficTargetKph:telemetryRound((player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||player.autoTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),trafficSpeedLimitKph:Number.isFinite(player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit)?telemetryRound(player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit*3.6,1):null,targetSpeedKph:telemetryRound((player.autoTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),committedTargetKph:Number.isFinite(player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed)?telemetryRound(player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed*3.6,1):null,targetSpeedReason:player.autoTargetSpeedReason||"TRACK",targetSpeedReasonDetail:player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail||null,

  autoMode:autoCtlMode,autoPhase:player.autoPhase||null,autoPace:telemetryRound(player.autoPace,4),autoTargetLat:telemetryRound(player.autoTargetLat,3),autoTechnical:!!player.autoTechnicalActive,autoTechnicalAhead:!!player.autoTechnicalAhead,stabilityRisk:telemetryRound(player.autoStabilityRisk,3),rearCritical:telemetryRound(player.autoRearCritical,3),brakeRelease:telemetryRound(player.autoBrakeRelease,3),counterSteerActive:!!player.autoCounterSteerActive,counterSteerRequest:!!player.autoCounterSteerRequest,renderPressure:telemetryRound(mobileRenderPressure(),3),recovery:rec,

  recoveryEmergency:!!(player.autoRecovery&&player.autoRecovery.emergency),recoveryStage:player.autoRecovery?(player.autoRecovery.emergencyStage||player.autoRecovery.mode):null,

  rivalLOD:rivalLODCounts(),rivalLaunchGapGuards:(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.launchGapGuard?1:0),0),rivalLaunchCorridorHolds:(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.launchCorridorHold?1:0),0),rivalKinkGuards:(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.kinkGuard?1:0),0),playerLaunchCorridorHold:!!player.autoLaunchCorridorHold,firstComplexLaneLock:!!player.autoFirstComplexLaneLock,firstChicaneRelease:telemetryRound(player.autoFirstChicaneRelease||0,3),technicalRiskPrep:telemetryRound(player.autoTechnicalRiskPrep||0,3),geometryKinkGuard:!!player.autoGeometryKinkGuard,geometryKinkAngleDeg:telemetryRound((player.autoGeometryKinkAngle||0)*deg,1),geometryKinkJerkDeg:telemetryRound((player.autoGeometryKinkJerk||0)*deg,1),geometryKinkDistanceM:Number.isFinite(player.autoGeometryKinkD)?telemetryRound(player.autoGeometryKinkD,1):null,rawGeometryKinkAngleDeg:telemetryRound((player.autoRawGeometryKinkAngle||0)*deg,1),rawGeometryKinkDistanceM:Number.isFinite(player.autoRawGeometryKinkD)?telemetryRound(player.autoRawGeometryKinkD,1):null,lineFreeze:!!player.autoLineFreeze,laneDivergenceGuard:!!player.autoLaneDivergenceGuard,kinkCommitActive:!!player.autoKinkCommitActive,kinkCommitRemaining:telemetryRound(player.autoKinkCommitRemaining||0,2),kinkCommitBlend:telemetryRound(player.autoKinkCommitBlend||0,3),laneSwingGuard:!!player.autoLaneSwingGuard,laneSwingLimitM:Number.isFinite(player.autoLaneSwingLimit)?telemetryRound(player.autoLaneSwingLimit,2):null,pitMergeGuard:!!player.autoPitMergeGuard,
  rivalImpacting:(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+((r.impactMode||'NONE')!=='NONE'?1:0),0),rivalCautious:(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+((r.cautionTimer||0)>0?1:0),0),
  raceSurvivalActive:!!player.autoRaceSurvivalActive,raceSurvivalTargetKph:Number.isFinite(player.autoRaceSurvivalTarget)?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceSurvivalTarget*3.6,1):null,raceSurvivalCornerM:Number.isFinite(player.autoRaceSurvivalCornerD)?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceSurvivalCornerD,1):null,raceSurvivalSafeKph:Number.isFinite(player.autoRaceSurvivalSafe)?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceSurvivalSafe*3.6,1):null,raceSurvivalCleanRelease:!!player.autoRaceSurvivalCleanRelease,raceSurvivalRiskAhead:telemetryRound(player.autoRaceSurvivalRiskAhead||0,3),contactGuardActive:(player.autoContactGuardUntil||-99)>raceT,contactGuardRemaining:telemetryRound(Math.max(0,(player.autoContactGuardUntil||-99)-raceT),2),
  recoveryAssist:!!player.autoRecoveryAssist,recoveryForcePull:!!player.autoRecoveryForcePull,
  recoveryLockedSide:player.autoRecovery&&player.autoRecovery.emergency?(player.autoRecovery.emergencySide||null):null,
  recoveryEdgeLane:player.autoRecovery&&Number.isFinite(player.autoRecovery.emergencyEdgeLane)?telemetryRound(player.autoRecovery.emergencyEdgeLane,3):null,
  recoveryEdgeErrorM:player.autoRecovery&&Number.isFinite(player.autoRecovery.emergencyEdgeLane)?telemetryRound(player.lat-player.autoRecovery.emergencyEdgeLane,3):null,
  recoveryAlignErrorDeg:player.autoRecovery?telemetryRound(headingErr*deg,2):null,
  recoveryNoLatProgressSec:player.autoRecovery&&player.autoRecovery.emergency?telemetryRound(raceT-(player.autoRecovery.emergencyLastImprove||raceT),2):null,
  recoveryLateralGuardTrips:player.autoRecovery&&player.autoRecovery.emergency?(player.autoRecovery.lateralGuardTrips||0):0,
  trafficNearCount:player.autoRaceTraffic?player.autoRaceTraffic.nearCount||0:0,
  nearestAheadM:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.d,2):null,
  nearestAheadLatM:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.latAbs??Math.abs((player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.lat||0)-player.lat),2):null,
  nearestAheadId:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.rv?player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.rv.id:null,
  nearestAheadUid:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.rv?raceIdentity(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.rv.id):null,
  nearestAheadSpeedKph:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead?telemetryRound((player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.speed||0)*3.6,1):null,
  nearestAheadRelKph:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead?telemetryRound((player.speed-(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.speed||0))*3.6,1):null,
  timeGapAheadSec:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead&&player.speed>.5?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.d/player.speed,2):null,
  closingTimeSec:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead&&(player.speed-(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.speed||0))>.25?telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.d/(player.speed-(player.autoRaceTraffic.ahead.speed||0)),2):null,
  nearestBehindM:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind?telemetryRound(-player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.d,2):null,
  nearestBehindId:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.rv?player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.rv.id:null,
  nearestBehindUid:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.rv?raceIdentity(player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.rv.id):null,
  nearestBehindSpeedKph:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind?telemetryRound((player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.speed||0)*3.6,1):null,
  nearestBehindRelKph:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind?telemetryRound(((player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.speed||0)-player.speed)*3.6,1):null,
  closingTimeBehindSec:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.behind&&((player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.speed||0)-player.speed)>.25?telemetryRound((-player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.d)/((player.autoRaceTraffic.behind.speed||0)-player.speed),2):null,
  sideRival:player.autoRaceTraffic&&player.autoRaceTraffic.side?{id:player.autoRaceTraffic.side.rv.id,uid:raceIdentity(player.autoRaceTraffic.side.rv.id),d:telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.side.d,2),latDelta:telemetryRound(player.autoRaceTraffic.side.latDelta,2),speedKph:telemetryRound((player.autoRaceTraffic.side.speed||0)*3.6,1),relKph:telemetryRound(((player.autoRaceTraffic.side.speed||0)-player.speed)*3.6,1)}:null,
  lastRivalContactAgo:raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<20?telemetryRound(raceT-player.autoLastRivalContact,2):null,
  autDecision:{baseKph:telemetryRound((player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),afterGeometryKph:telemetryRound((player.autoDecisionAfterGeometry||player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),beforeStabilityKph:telemetryRound((player.autoDecisionBeforeStability||player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),afterStabilityKph:telemetryRound((player.autoDecisionAfterStability||player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),finalKph:telemetryRound((player.autoTargetSpeed||0)*3.6,1),rawTargetLat:telemetryRound(player.autoRawTargetLat,3),targetLat:telemetryRound(player.autoTargetLat,3),laneErrorM:telemetryRound((player.lat||0)-(player.autoTargetLat||0),3),laneRateMps:telemetryRound(player.autoLaneRate,3),desiredHeadingDeg:telemetryRound((player.autoDesiredHeading||player.heading)*deg,2)},

  localRisk:telemetryRound(autoRiskAt(player.s),3),reverse:!!player.reverse,ers:telemetryRound(player.ers,2),hp:telemetryRound(player.hp,2),balance:player.balanceState||null
 };
 sessionTelemetry.samples.push(sample);
 // En móvil la grabación COMPLETA funciona a 5 Hz; EVENTOS no crea estas muestras pesadas.
 const maxSamples=mobilePerformanceMode()?27000:54000;if(sessionTelemetry.samples.length>maxSamples)sessionTelemetry.samples.splice(0,Math.min(1000,sessionTelemetry.samples.length-maxSamples+250));
}
function exportSessionTelemetry(){
 try{
 if(!sessionTelemetry){const a=$("raceAlert");if(a){a.textContent="TEL · AÚN NO HAY DATOS DE SESIÓN";setTimeout(()=>{if(a.textContent.startsWith("TEL ·"))a.textContent=""},1200)}return;}
 const ev=sessionTelemetry.events||[],recoveryEnds=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RECOVERY_END'&&Number.isFinite(e.duration));
 const ss=sessionTelemetry.samples||[],reasonCounts={},impact={};
 for(let i=0;i<ss.length;i++){
   const s=ss[i],r=s.targetSpeedReason||"UNKNOWN",dt=i+1<ss.length?clamp((ss[i+1].t||s.t)-(s.t||0),0,.5):.1;
   reasonCounts[r]=(reasonCounts[r]||0)+1;
   const base=Math.max(0,s.baseTrackTargetKph||0),tar=Math.max(0,s.targetSpeedKph||0),red=Math.max(0,base-tar);
   const x=impact[r]||(impact[r]={samples:0,seconds:0,kphSeconds:0,maxReductionKph:0,estimatedTimeLossSec:0});
   x.samples++;x.seconds+=dt;x.kphSeconds+=red*dt;x.maxReductionKph=Math.max(x.maxReductionKph,red);
   // Estimación orientativa: tiempo adicional necesario para cubrir la distancia de
   // esta muestra al objetivo limitado frente al objetivo puro de pista.
   if(base>8&&tar>4&&red>.5){const vk=Math.max(0,s.speedKph||0)/3.6,dist=vk*dt;x.estimatedTimeLossSec+=Math.max(0,dist/(tar/3.6)-dist/(base/3.6));}
 }
 for(const [r,x] of Object.entries(impact)){x.seconds=telemetryRound(x.seconds,2);x.avgReductionKph=telemetryRound(x.kphSeconds/Math.max(.001,x.seconds),1);x.kphSeconds=telemetryRound(x.kphSeconds,1);x.maxReductionKph=telemetryRound(x.maxReductionKph,1);x.estimatedTimeLossSec=telemetryRound(x.estimatedTimeLossSec,2);}
 const contacts=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RIVAL_CONTACT'),containedContacts=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RIVAL_CONTACT_CONTAINED'),contactEpisodes=[];
 for(const c of contacts){const last=contactEpisodes[contactEpisodes.length-1];if(last&&last.rivalUid===c.rivalUid&&c.t-last.lastT<=.8){last.lastT=c.t;last.contacts++;last.maxSeverity=Math.max(last.maxSeverity,c.severity||0);last.maxClosingKph=Math.max(last.maxClosingKph,c.closingKph||0);}else contactEpisodes.push({rivalUid:c.rivalUid,rivalName:c.rivalName,team:c.team,firstT:c.t,lastT:c.t,contacts:1,maxSeverity:c.severity||0,maxClosingKph:c.closingKph||0});}
 for(const e of contactEpisodes){e.duration=telemetryRound(e.lastT-e.firstT,2);e.maxSeverity=telemetryRound(e.maxSeverity,3);e.maxClosingKph=telemetryRound(e.maxClosingKph,1);}
 const closedRecoveryTime=recoveryEnds.reduce((a,e)=>a+e.duration,0),ongoingRecoverySec=(player&&player.autoRecovery&&sessionTelemetryPrev&&sessionTelemetryPrev.recoveryEpisodeAt!=null)?Math.max(0,raceT-sessionTelemetryPrev.recoveryEpisodeAt):0;
 const recoveryTime=telemetryRound(closedRecoveryTime+ongoingRecoverySec,2),hardPullEvents=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RECOVERY_TRACTION_ASSIST').length;
 const recoveryDurations=recoveryEnds.map(e=>e.duration);if(ongoingRecoverySec>0)recoveryDurations.push(ongoingRecoverySec);
 const rivalBestLaps=(rivals||[]).filter(r=>Number.isFinite(r.bestLap)&&r.bestLap<1e8).map(r=>({uid:r.uid||raceIdentity(r.id),name:r.driverName||raceName(r.id),team:r.team,bestLap:telemetryRound(r.bestLap,3),skill:telemetryRound(r.skill,3),aggression:telemetryRound(r.aggression,3),consistency:telemetryRound(r.consistency,3),brakeSkill:telemetryRound(r.brakeSkill,3),tractionSkill:telemetryRound(r.tractionSkill,3),cornerSkill:telemetryRound(r.cornerSkill,3),paceProfile:telemetryRound(r.racePaceProfile,4)})).sort((a,b)=>a.bestLap-b.bestLap);
 const rivalTimes=rivalBestLaps.map(r=>r.bestLap).filter(Number.isFinite).sort((a,b)=>a-b),rivalMedian=rivalTimes.length?rivalTimes[Math.floor(rivalTimes.length/2)]:null;
 const rivalPaceStats={fastest:rivalTimes.length?telemetryRound(rivalTimes[0],3):null,median:Number.isFinite(rivalMedian)?telemetryRound(rivalMedian,3):null,slowest:rivalTimes.length?telemetryRound(rivalTimes[rivalTimes.length-1],3):null};
 const earlyContacts=contacts.filter(c=>(c.t||0)<10),earlySevereContacts=earlyContacts.filter(c=>(c.severity||0)>=.05),launchGuardSamples=ss.filter(s=>(s.rivalLaunchGapGuards||0)>0).length,maxLaunchGapGuards=ss.reduce((m,s)=>Math.max(m,s.rivalLaunchGapGuards||0),0);
 const rivalRivalContacts=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RIVAL_RIVAL_CONTACT'),rivalIncidents=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RIVAL_INCIDENT'),rivalSpinIncidents=rivalIncidents.filter(e=>e.mode==='SPIN'),rivalOfftracks=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RIVAL_OFFTRACK'),rivalRecovers=ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RIVAL_RECOVER');
 const rrContactEpisodes=[];for(const c of rivalRivalContacts){const pair=[c.rivalAUid||c.rivalA,c.rivalBUid||c.rivalB].sort().join('|'),last=rrContactEpisodes[rrContactEpisodes.length-1];if(last&&last.pair===pair&&c.t-last.lastT<=.8){last.lastT=c.t;last.contacts++;last.maxSeverity=Math.max(last.maxSeverity,c.severity||0);last.maxClosingKph=Math.max(last.maxClosingKph,c.closingKph||0);}else rrContactEpisodes.push({kind:'RIVAL_RIVAL',pair,rivalAUid:c.rivalAUid,rivalAName:c.rivalAName,rivalBUid:c.rivalBUid,rivalBName:c.rivalBName,firstT:c.t,lastT:c.t,contacts:1,maxSeverity:c.severity||0,maxClosingKph:c.closingKph||0});}
 for(const e of rrContactEpisodes){e.duration=telemetryRound(e.lastT-e.firstT,2);e.maxSeverity=telemetryRound(e.maxSeverity,3);e.maxClosingKph=telemetryRound(e.maxClosingKph,1);}
 const stateEpisodes=rivalIncidents.filter(e=>e.mode==='SPIN'||e.mode==='WOBBLE').map(e=>({kind:'RIVAL_STATE',rivalUid:e.rivalUid,rivalName:e.rivalName,mode:e.mode,source:e.source,firstT:e.t,lastT:e.t,contacts:1,maxSeverity:e.severity||0,maxClosingKph:e.closingKph||0}));
 const incidentWindows=telemetryIncidentWindows(ss,[...contactEpisodes,...rrContactEpisodes,...stateEpisodes].sort((a,b)=>a.firstT-b.firstT));
 const perfRows=ss.map(s=>s.performance).filter(Boolean),avg=(key)=>perfRows.length?perfRows.reduce((a,p)=>a+(Number(p[key])||0),0)/perfRows.length:null,maxv=(key)=>perfRows.length?Math.max(...perfRows.map(p=>Number(p[key])||0)):null;
 const performanceStats={avgFps:telemetryRound(avg('fps'),1),avgFrameMs:telemetryRound(avg('frameMs'),2),maxFrameMs:telemetryRound(maxv('lastFrameMs'),2),avgRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('renderMs'),2),avgPhysicsMs:telemetryRound(avg('physicsMs'),2),avgAiMs:telemetryRound(avg('aiMs'),2),maxAiMs:telemetryRound(maxv('aiMs'),2),avgCollisionMs:telemetryRound(avg('collisionMs'),2),avgTelemetryMs:telemetryRound(avg('telemetryMs'),2),avgHudMs:telemetryRound(avg('hudMs'),2),avgTrackRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('trackRenderMs'),2),avgTerrainRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('terrainRenderMs'),2),avgTunnelRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('tunnelRenderMs'),2),avgEnvironmentRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('environmentRenderMs'),2),avgCarsRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('carsRenderMs'),2),avgMirrorsRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('mirrorsRenderMs'),2),avgCockpitRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('cockpitRenderMs'),2),avgEffectsRenderMs:telemetryRound(avg('effectsRenderMs'),2),avgWebglSubmitMs:telemetryRound(avg('webglSubmitMs'),2)};
 if(sessionTelemetry.recording){sessionTelemetry.recording.currentMode=getTelemetryRecordMode();sessionTelemetry.recording.fullSampleHz=telemetryFullSampleHz();sessionTelemetry.recording.eventCheckHz=telemetryEventCheckHz();}
 const payload={...sessionTelemetry,incidentWindows,exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),summary:{recordingMode:getTelemetryRecordMode(),fullSampleHz:telemetryFullSampleHz(),eventCheckHz:telemetryEventCheckHz(),duration:telemetryRound(raceT,3),completedLaps:player?player.lap:null,bestLap:best<1e8?telemetryRound(best,3):null,sampleCount:ss.length,eventCount:ev.length,rivalContacts:contacts.length,containedRivalContacts:containedContacts.length,rivalContactEpisodes:contactEpisodes.length,earlyRivalContacts:earlyContacts.length,earlySevereRivalContacts:earlySevereContacts.length,rivalRivalContacts:rivalRivalContacts.length,rivalRivalContactEpisodes:rrContactEpisodes.length,rivalIncidents:rivalIncidents.length,rivalSpinIncidents:rivalSpinIncidents.length,rivalOfftracksFromImpact:rivalOfftracks.length,rivalRecovers:rivalRecovers.length,launchGapGuardSamples:launchGuardSamples,maxLaunchGapGuards,contactEpisodes,performanceStats,runtimeProfiler:apexRuntimePerfSummary(),circuitCompile:trackData&&trackData._compileStats?clonePlain(trackData._compileStats):null,wallHits:ev.filter(e=>e.type==='WALL_HIT').length,spins:ev.filter(e=>e.type==='SPIN').length,recoveryEpisodes:ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RECOVERY_START').length,emergencyRecoveries:ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RECOVERY_EMERGENCY').length,hardRecoveryAssists:hardPullEvents,recoveryActiveAtExport:ongoingRecoverySec>0,recoveryTimeSec:recoveryTime,maxRecoverySec:recoveryDurations.length?telemetryRound(Math.max(...recoveryDurations),2):null,lateralRecoveryGuardTrips:ev.filter(e=>e.type==='RECOVERY_LATERAL_GUARD').length,rivalSimulation:{model:"HYBRID_SHARED_FRENET_LOD_PARITY",lodAtExport:rivalLODCounts(),nearDynamicM:mobilePerformanceMode()?120:150,midHybridM:mobilePerformanceMode()?360:500},rivalBestLaps,rivalPaceStats,targetSpeedReasonSamples:reasonCounts,targetLimiterImpact:impact}};
 const safe=(trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"circuito").replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi,'-');
 const stamp=new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-');
 const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');
 a.href=url;a.download=`Apex26_${safe}_${currentSession}_${stamp}.json`;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1500);
 const msg=$("raceAlert");if(msg){msg.textContent="TEL · SESIÓN EXPORTADA · "+telemetryRecordLabel();msg.style.color="#9feeff";setTimeout(()=>{if(msg.textContent.startsWith("TEL ·"))msg.textContent=""},1300)}
 }catch(err){console.error("Apex26 telemetry export",err);const msg=$("raceAlert");if(msg){msg.textContent="TEL · ERROR AL EXPORTAR · "+(err&&err.message?err.message:"DESCONOCIDO");msg.style.color="#ff8b9a";setTimeout(()=>{if(msg.textContent.startsWith("TEL · ERROR"))msg.textContent=""},3500)}}
}

function telemetryHtml(v){return String(v??'—').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]))}
function telemetryNearbyRivals(limit=8){
 if(!player||!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000;
 return (rivals||[]).filter(r=>!r.finished).map(rv=>{let d=(rv.total||0)-(player.total||0);if(d>L*.5)d-=L;else if(d<-L*.5)d+=L;const latDelta=(rv.lat||0)-(player.lat||0);return{rv,id:rv.id,uid:rv.uid||raceIdentity(rv.id),name:rv.driverName||raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,teamName:rv.teamName||teamInfo(rv.team).name,d,absD:Math.abs(d),lat:rv.lat||0,latDelta,speedKph:(rv.speed||0)*3.6,relKph:((rv.speed||0)-(player.speed||0))*3.6,targetKph:Number.isFinite(rv.liveTargetSpeed)?rv.liveTargetSpeed*3.6:null,targetLat:Number.isFinite(rv.liveTargetLat)?rv.liveTargetLat:rv.targetLat,intent:rv.intent||'RACE',note:rv.racecraftNote||rv.intent||'RACE',mistake:rv.mistakeTimer>0?rv.mistakeType:'',guard:!!rv.launchGapGuard,impactMode:rv.impactMode||'NONE',impactTimer:rv.impactTimer||0,cautionTimer:rv.cautionTimer||0,contactMemory:rv.contactRiskMemory||0,damage:rv.damage??100,yawKick:rv.yawKick||0,lod:rv.simModel||rivalLODName(rv.simLOD||0),skill:rv.skill,aggression:rv.aggression,consistency:rv.consistency,defense:rv.defense,planD:rv.livePlanD,draft:!!rv.liveDraft,color:(teamInfo(rv.team).color||'#9fb4c7')}}).sort((a,b)=>a.absD-b.absD).slice(0,limit);
}
function telemetryCornerSnapshot(){
 if(!player)return null;const p=player.autoPlan||driverCornerPlan(player.s,300,10);if(!p)return null;return{distanceM:Number.isFinite(p.d)?p.d:null,curvature:Number.isFinite(p.k)?p.k:null,safeKph:Number.isFinite(p.safe)?p.safe*3.6:null,sequence:!!p.sequence,signChanges:p.signChanges||0,firstM:p.first&&Number.isFinite(p.first.d)?p.first.d:null,lastM:p.last&&Number.isFinite(p.last.d)?p.last.d:null,direction:Math.abs(p.k)<.0002?'RECTA':p.k>0?'DERECHA':'IZQUIERDA'};
}
function telemetryCompactRivals(limit=4){return telemetryNearbyRivals(limit).map(x=>({id:x.id,uid:x.uid,name:x.name,team:x.team,dM:telemetryRound(x.d,2),latM:telemetryRound(x.latDelta,2),speedKph:telemetryRound(x.speedKph,1),relKph:telemetryRound(x.relKph,1),targetKph:telemetryRound(x.targetKph,1),targetLat:telemetryRound(x.targetLat,2),intent:x.intent,note:x.note,mistake:x.mistake||null,launchGuard:x.guard,launchCorridorHold:!!x.rv.launchCorridorHold,launchCorridorMix:telemetryRound(x.rv.launchCorridorMix,3),impactMode:x.impactMode,impactTimer:telemetryRound(x.impactTimer,2),cautionTimer:telemetryRound(x.cautionTimer,2),contactMemory:telemetryRound(x.contactMemory,3),damage:telemetryRound(x.damage,1),yawKickDeg:telemetryRound(x.yawKick*180/Math.PI,1),lod:x.lod}))}
function telemetryIncidentWindows(samples,episodes){
 const out=[];for(const ep of (episodes||[]).slice(-60)){const from=ep.firstT-3,to=ep.lastT+2,frames=[];let lastT=-1e9;for(const s of samples){if(s.t<from)continue;if(s.t>to)break;if(s.t-lastT<.19)continue;lastT=s.t;frames.push({t:s.t,lap:s.lap,s:s.s,speedKph:s.speedKph,lat:s.lat,headingErrDeg:s.headingErrDeg,targetSpeedKph:s.targetSpeedKph,targetReason:s.targetSpeedReason,autoPhase:s.autoPhase,racePackRisk:s.racePackRisk,rearThreat:s.rearThreat,trafficNearCount:s.trafficNearCount,nearestAheadM:s.nearestAheadM,nearestBehindM:s.nearestBehindM,sideRival:s.sideRival,rivalLaunchGapGuards:s.rivalLaunchGapGuards,rivalLaunchCorridorHolds:s.rivalLaunchCorridorHolds,playerLaunchCorridorHold:s.playerLaunchCorridorHold,firstComplexLaneLock:s.firstComplexLaneLock,technicalRiskPrep:s.technicalRiskPrep,geometryKinkGuard:s.geometryKinkGuard,kinkCommitActive:s.kinkCommitActive,kinkCommitBlend:s.kinkCommitBlend,laneSwingGuard:s.laneSwingGuard,rivalKinkGuards:s.rivalKinkGuards,rivalImpacting:s.rivalImpacting,rivalCautious:s.rivalCautious,raceSurvivalActive:s.raceSurvivalActive,raceSurvivalTargetKph:s.raceSurvivalTargetKph,raceSurvivalCornerM:s.raceSurvivalCornerM,raceSurvivalCleanRelease:s.raceSurvivalCleanRelease,contactGuardActive:s.contactGuardActive,nearRivals:s.nearRivals||[],performance:s.performance||null});}out.push({...ep,windowFrom:telemetryRound(from,2),windowTo:telemetryRound(to,2),frames});}return out;
}
let telemetryPopoutWindow=null,telemetryPopoutHistory=[],telemetryPopoutLastUpdate=0;
function desktopTelemetryWindowAllowed(){
 const ua=navigator.userAgent||"",touchTablet=/(Android|iPad|iPhone|iPod|Mobile|Tablet)/i.test(ua)||(navigator.platform==="MacIntel"&&(navigator.maxTouchPoints||0)>1);
 const fine=!!(window.matchMedia&&matchMedia("(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine)").matches);
 return !touchTablet&&fine;
}
function telemetryPopoutNotify(text){
 const a=$("raceAlert");if(!a)return;a.textContent=text;a.style.color="#9feeff";setTimeout(()=>{if(a.textContent===text)a.textContent=""},1800);
}

function telemetryPopoutSet(id,value){const w=telemetryPopoutWindow;if(!w||w.closed)return;const el=w.document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=value;}
function drawTelemetryPopoutChart(){
 const w=telemetryPopoutWindow;if(!w||w.closed)return;const c=w.document.getElementById("speedChart");if(!c)return;const ctx=c.getContext("2d"),W=c.width,H=c.height,h=telemetryPopoutHistory;if(!h.length)return;
 ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.fillStyle="#071019";ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.strokeStyle="#173040";ctx.lineWidth=1;for(let i=1;i<4;i++){const y=H*i/4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(W,y);ctx.stroke()}
 let max=120;for(const p of h)max=Math.max(max,p.speed||0,p.target||0);max=Math.ceil(max/50)*50;const x=i=>h.length<2?0:i*(W/(h.length-1)),y=v=>H-6-(Math.max(0,v)/max)*(H-14);
 const line=(key,color,width)=>{ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.beginPath();h.forEach((p,i)=>{const X=x(i),Y=y(p[key]||0);i?ctx.lineTo(X,Y):ctx.moveTo(X,Y)});ctx.stroke()};line("target","#59788c",1.4);line("speed","#7fe7ff",2.2);
 ctx.fillStyle="#8397aa";ctx.font="9px system-ui";ctx.fillText(max+" km/h",5,10);ctx.fillStyle="#7fe7ff";ctx.fillText("REAL",W-65,11);ctx.fillStyle="#91a9b8";ctx.fillText("OBJ",W-31,11);
}
function drawTelemetryTrafficCanvas(near){
 const w=telemetryPopoutWindow;if(!w||w.closed)return;const c=w.document.getElementById('trafficChart');if(!c)return;const ctx=c.getContext('2d'),W=c.width,H=c.height;ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.fillStyle='#071019';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 // Vista relativa desde el asiento del piloto: +lat es la izquierda física de la
 // pista, por lo que en pantalla debe desplazarse hacia la IZQUIERDA, no a la derecha.
 // ΔS positivo sigue siendo coche DELANTE y se dibuja arriba.
 const x=lat=>W*.5-clamp(lat/4.3,-1,1)*W*.42,y=d=>H*.60-clamp(d/45,-.67,1)*H*.50;
 ctx.strokeStyle='#183243';ctx.lineWidth=1;for(const lat of [-4,-2,0,2,4]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x(lat),8);ctx.lineTo(x(lat),H-8);ctx.stroke()}for(const d of [-30,-15,0,15,30,45]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(8,y(d));ctx.lineTo(W-8,y(d));ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='#61798b';ctx.font='10px system-ui';ctx.fillText((d>0?'+':'')+d+'m',10,y(d)-3)}
 ctx.fillStyle='#7891a3';ctx.font='900 9px system-ui';ctx.fillText('DELANTE',W-58,12);ctx.fillText('DETRÁS',W-50,H-9);ctx.fillText('IZQ.',8,H-9);ctx.fillText('DER.',W-25,H-9);
 ctx.fillStyle='#7fe7ff';ctx.fillRect(x(0)-8,y(0)-12,16,24);ctx.fillStyle='#dff8ff';ctx.font='900 10px system-ui';ctx.fillText('TÚ',x(0)+12,y(0)+4);
 (near||[]).filter(r=>r.d>=-30&&r.d<=45&&Math.abs(r.latDelta)<=4.6).forEach(r=>{ctx.beginPath();ctx.fillStyle=r.color||'#ffb36b';ctx.arc(x(r.latDelta),y(r.d),7,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle=r.guard?'#ffd66b':'#ffffff55';ctx.lineWidth=r.guard?3:1;ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='#dfeaf2';ctx.font='900 9px system-ui';ctx.fillText((r.name||'R').split(' ').pop().slice(0,6),x(r.latDelta)+10,y(r.d)+3)});
}
function updateTelemetryPopout(force=false){
 const w=telemetryPopoutWindow;if(!w||w.closed){telemetryPopoutWindow=null;return;}const now=performance.now();if(!force&&now-telemetryPopoutLastUpdate<95)return;telemetryPopoutLastUpdate=now;
 if(!player||!trackData){telemetryPopoutSet("meta","Sin sesión activa");telemetryPopoutSet("xReason","SIN SESIÓN");const live=w.document.getElementById("live");if(live)live.style.color="#8397aa";return;}
 const deg=180/Math.PI,tr=player.track||poseAt(player.s),trackHeading=Math.atan2(tr.tx,tr.tz),headingErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading)*deg,rt=player.autoRaceTraffic||{},ahead=rt.ahead,behind=rt.behind,side=rt.side,rec=player.autoRecovery,corner=telemetryCornerSnapshot(),near=telemetryNearbyRivals(12),lod=rivalLODCounts();
 const speed=Math.max(0,player.speed*3.6),target=Math.max(0,(player.autoTargetSpeed||0)*3.6),base=Math.max(0,(player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)*3.6),pre=Math.max(0,(player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||player.autoTargetSpeed||0)*3.6),trafficRed=Math.max(0,pre-target);
 telemetryPopoutSet("meta",`${trackData.spec?.country||""} · ${trackData.spec?.name||"Circuito"} · ${currentSession.toUpperCase()} · ${APP_VERSION}`);telemetryPopoutSet("xSpeed",Math.round(speed));telemetryPopoutSet("xTarget",Math.round(target)+" km/h");telemetryPopoutSet("xBase",Math.round(base)+" km/h");telemetryPopoutSet("xReason",(player.autoTargetSpeedReason||"TRACK")+(player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail?" · "+player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail:""));telemetryPopoutSet("xGear",gear());telemetryPopoutSet("xRpm",Math.round(engineRPM()/100)*100);telemetryPopoutSet("xRace","P"+(player.pos||"—")+" · V"+(player.lap+1));
 telemetryPopoutSet("xAhead",ahead?`${ahead.d.toFixed(1)} m · ${Math.round((ahead.speed||0)*3.6)} km/h · Δ ${Math.round((player.speed-(ahead.speed||0))*3.6)}`:"—");telemetryPopoutSet("xBehind",behind?`${(-behind.d).toFixed(1)} m · ${Math.round((behind.speed||0)*3.6)} km/h · Δ ${Math.round(((behind.speed||0)-player.speed)*3.6)}`:"—");telemetryPopoutSet("xSide",side?`${side.d>=0?"+":""}${side.d.toFixed(1)} m · lat ${side.latDelta.toFixed(1)} m · ${Math.round((side.speed||0)*3.6)} km/h`:"—");telemetryPopoutSet("xNear",String(rt.nearCount||0));telemetryPopoutSet("xTrafficRed",trafficRed.toFixed(1)+" km/h");
 telemetryPopoutSet("xThrottle",Math.round((player.throttle||0)*100)+"%");telemetryPopoutSet("xBrake",Math.round((player.brakeInput||0)*100)+"%");const tb=w.document.getElementById("xThrottleBar"),bb=w.document.getElementById("xBrakeBar");if(tb)tb.style.width=clamp((player.throttle||0)*100,0,100)+"%";if(bb)bb.style.width=clamp((player.brakeInput||0)*100,0,100)+"%";telemetryPopoutSet("xSteer",((player.delta||0)*deg).toFixed(1)+"°");telemetryPopoutSet("xLatHead",(player.lat||0).toFixed(2)+" m · "+headingErr.toFixed(1)+"°");
 // AUT / decisión
 const geom=Math.max(0,(player.autoDecisionAfterGeometry||player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed||0)*3.6),preStab=Math.max(0,(player.autoDecisionBeforeStability||player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||0)*3.6),stab=Math.max(0,(player.autoDecisionAfterStability||player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed||0)*3.6),mx=Math.max(80,base,geom,preStab,stab,target);const chain=[['xAutBase','barBase',base],['xAutGeometry','barGeometry',geom],['xAutPreStab','barPreStab',preStab],['xAutStab','barStab',stab],['xAutFinal','barFinal',target]];for(const [id,bar,v] of chain){telemetryPopoutSet(id,Math.round(v)+' km/h');const el=w.document.getElementById(bar);if(el)el.style.width=clamp(v/mx*100,0,100)+'%'}
 telemetryPopoutSet('xAutReason',(player.autoTargetSpeedReason||'TRACK')+(player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail?' · '+player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail:''));telemetryPopoutSet('xAutPhase',player.autoPhase||'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xRawLane',Number.isFinite(player.autoRawTargetLat)?player.autoRawTargetLat.toFixed(2)+' m':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xTargetLane',Number.isFinite(player.autoTargetLat)?player.autoTargetLat.toFixed(2)+' m':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xLaneErr',Number.isFinite(player.autoTargetLat)?((player.lat||0)-player.autoTargetLat).toFixed(2)+' m':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xDesiredHeading',Number.isFinite(player.autoDesiredHeading)?(player.autoDesiredHeading*deg).toFixed(1)+'°':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xAutoPace',Number.isFinite(player.autoPace)?player.autoPace.toFixed(4):'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xSurvivalMode',player.autoRaceSurvivalActive?(player.lap<1?'1ª VUELTA':'ACTIVO'):'OFF');telemetryPopoutSet('xSurvivalTarget',Number.isFinite(player.autoRaceSurvivalTarget)&&player.autoRaceSurvivalActive?Math.round(player.autoRaceSurvivalTarget*3.6)+' km/h':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xSurvivalCorner',Number.isFinite(player.autoRaceSurvivalCornerD)?Math.round(player.autoRaceSurvivalCornerD)+' m':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xContactGuard',(player.autoContactGuardUntil||-99)>raceT?Math.max(0,(player.autoContactGuardUntil-raceT)).toFixed(1)+' s':'OFF');telemetryPopoutSet('xCornerDist',corner&&Number.isFinite(corner.distanceM)?Math.round(corner.distanceM)+' m':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xCornerDir',corner?corner.direction:'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xCornerSafe',corner&&Number.isFinite(corner.safeKph)?Math.round(corner.safeKph)+' km/h':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xCornerK',corner&&Number.isFinite(corner.curvature)?'k '+corner.curvature.toFixed(5):'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xAutStability',Math.round((player.autoStabilityRisk||0)*100)+'%');telemetryPopoutSet('xRearCritical',Math.round((player.autoRearCritical||0)*100)+'%');telemetryPopoutSet('xAutLocalRisk',Math.round(autoRiskAt(player.s)*100)+'%');telemetryPopoutSet('xCornerSequence',corner?(corner.sequence?`SÍ · ${corner.signChanges} cambio(s)`:'NO'):'—');
 // Tráfico + rivales
 telemetryPopoutSet('xPackRisk',Math.round((player.autoRacePackRisk||0)*100)+'%');telemetryPopoutSet('xRearThreat',Math.round((player.autoRearThreat||0)*100)+'%');telemetryPopoutSet('xLaunchProtect',player.autoRaceStartProtect?'SÍ':'NO');telemetryPopoutSet('xEscapeLane',player.autoTrafficEscapeLane?'SÍ':'NO');const guards=(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.launchGapGuard?1:0),0);const corridorHolds=(rivals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.launchCorridorHold?1:0),0);telemetryPopoutSet('xGapGuards',String(guards)+' · COR '+corridorHolds+(player.autoLaunchCorridorHold?' · TÚ':''));telemetryPopoutSet('xLastContact',raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<99?(raceT-player.autoLastRivalContact).toFixed(1)+' s':'—');telemetryPopoutSet('xLodNear',lod.nearDynamic||0);telemetryPopoutSet('xLodMid',lod.midHybrid||0);telemetryPopoutSet('xLodFar',lod.farKinematic||0);drawTelemetryTrafficCanvas(near);
 const rowHtml=r=>`<tr><td>${telemetryHtml((r.d>=0?'+':'')+r.d.toFixed(1))}</td><td>${telemetryHtml(r.name)}</td><td>${telemetryHtml(r.team)}</td><td>${r.latDelta.toFixed(2)}</td><td>${Math.round(r.speedKph)}</td><td>${Number.isFinite(r.targetKph)?Math.round(r.targetKph):'—'}</td><td>${telemetryHtml(r.intent)}</td><td>${r.guard?'<span class="pill guard">GUARD</span>':''}${r.impactMode&&r.impactMode!=='NONE'?'<span class="pill '+r.impactMode.toLowerCase()+'">'+telemetryHtml(r.impactMode)+'</span>':''}${r.cautionTimer>.05?'<span class="pill caution">CAUT '+r.cautionTimer.toFixed(1)+'</span>':''}${r.mistake?'<span class="pill mistake">'+telemetryHtml(r.mistake)+'</span>':''}</td><td>${telemetryHtml(r.lod)}</td></tr>`;

 // Coche
 telemetryPopoutSet("xSurface",terrainName(player.surfaceKind,player.surface));telemetryPopoutSet("xBalance",player.balanceState||"ESTABLE");telemetryPopoutSet("xStability",Math.round((player.autoStabilityRisk||0)*100)+"%");telemetryPopoutSet("xLocalRisk",Math.round(autoRiskAt(player.s)*100)+"%");telemetryPopoutSet("xHealth",Math.round(player.ers||0)+"% · "+Math.round(player.hp||0)+"%");telemetryPopoutSet('xTerrainDrag',(player.terrainDrag||0).toFixed(2)+' m/s²');telemetryPopoutSet('xCarSlip',((player.slipAngle||0)*deg).toFixed(2)+'°');telemetryPopoutSet('xCarAxleSlip',((player.slipF||0)*deg).toFixed(2)+'° / '+((player.slipR||0)*deg).toFixed(2)+'°');telemetryPopoutSet('xCarG',(player.latG||0).toFixed(2)+' / '+(player.longG||0).toFixed(2)+' G');telemetryPopoutSet('xCarGrip',Math.round((player.frontUse||0)*100)+'% / '+Math.round((player.rearUse||0)*100)+'%');telemetryPopoutSet('xCarDownforce',Math.round((player.downforce||0)/9.81)+' kg');telemetryPopoutSet('xCarLoads',Math.round((player.frontLoad||0)/9.81)+' / '+Math.round((player.rearLoad||0)/9.81)+' kg');
