}

function toggleTelemetry(){
 updateTelemetryRecordingUI();
 const panel=$("telemetryPanel"),open=panel.classList.contains("hidden");
 panel.classList.toggle("hidden",!open);
 $("telemetryBtn").classList.toggle("on",open);
 if(open)updateTelemetry();
}
function updateTelemetry(){
 updateTelemetryPopout();
 if(!player||$("telemetryPanel").classList.contains("hidden"))return;
 const deg=180/Math.PI;
 $("telSteer").textContent=((player.delta||0)*deg).toFixed(1)+"°";
 $("telSlip").textContent=((player.slipAngle||0)*deg).toFixed(1)+"°";
 $("telAxleSlip").textContent=((player.slipF||0)*deg).toFixed(1)+"° / "+((player.slipR||0)*deg).toFixed(1)+"°";
 $("telG").textContent=(player.latG||0).toFixed(2)+" / "+(player.longG||0).toFixed(2);
 const fu=clamp((player.frontUse||0)*100,0,140),ru=clamp((player.rearUse||0)*100,0,140);
 $("telFront").textContent=Math.round(fu)+"%";$("telRear").textContent=Math.round(ru)+"%";
 $("telFrontBar").style.width=Math.min(100,fu)+"%";$("telRearBar").style.width=Math.min(100,ru)+"%";
 $("telDownforce").textContent=Math.round((player.downforce||0)/9.81)+" kg";
 $("telLoads").textContent=Math.round((player.frontLoad||0)/9.81)+" / "+Math.round((player.rearLoad||0)/9.81)+" kg";
 $("telSurface").textContent=terrainName(player.surfaceKind,player.surface);$("telTerrainDrag").textContent=(player.terrainDrag||0).toFixed(1)+" m/s²";$("telPedals").textContent=Math.round((player.throttle||0)*100)+" / "+Math.round((player.brakeInput||0)*100)+"%";$("telFaults").textContent=player.physicsFaults||0;
 const st=player.balanceState||"ESTABLE";$("telState").textContent=st;
 $("telState").style.color=st==="SUBVIRA"?"#ffd66b":st==="SOBREVIRA"?"#ff7988":st==="AL LÍMITE"?"#ffad66":st==="BLOQUEO"?"#ff6678":st==="TRACCIÓN"?"#73dfff":"#7dffc1";
}
let hudLastUpdate=0;
function updateHud(force=false){
 apexUpdateControlHud();
 const now=performance.now(),minGap=mobileControlProfile().hudGap;
 if(!force&&now-hudLastUpdate<minGap)return;
 hudLastUpdate=now;
 $("speed").textContent=Math.round(finite(player.speed,0)*3.6);$("gear").textContent=gear();if($("rpm"))$("rpm").textContent=Math.round(engineRPM()/100)*100+" RPM";
 const temp=ambientTemp(trackData.spec,raceWeather),weather=weatherHudLabel(raceWeather,trackData.spec),gpLabel=(trackData.spec.country||'GP')+' · '+trackData.spec.name,gpAbbr=(roundFlagCode[(trackData.spec.r||1)-1]||'GP');

 const lapElapsed=Math.max(0,raceT-lapStart),bestTxt=(best<1e8?fmt(best):"--:--.---"),currentTxt=fmt(lapElapsed),sessionBestTxt=(currentSession==='race'&&Number.isFinite(sessionFastestLap.time)?fmt(sessionFastestLap.time):bestTxt),soloPractice=currentSession==='freepractice'&&!!sessionSettings.noRivals;

 }

 if(player.damage){

   const worst=Object.entries(player.damage).sort((a,b)=>a[1]-b[1])[0];

 const corner=upcomingCorner(),kmh=player.speed*3.6,safe=corner.safe*3.6;
 updatePitButtonUI();
 let alert=pitStopHudStatus()||(player.trackDirDot<-.25?"SENTIDO CONTRARIO":"");
 if(!alert&&player.trackDirDot>.25&&corner.d<170&&kmh>safe+28)alert="FRENA · "+Math.round(corner.d)+" m";
 else if(!alert&&player.slipstream)alert="REBUFO";
 else if(!alert&&player.surface==="KERB")alert="PIANO";
 else if(!alert&&player.surfaceKind==="GRAVEL")alert="GRAVA";
 else if(!alert&&player.surfaceKind==="SAND")alert="ARENA";
 else if(!alert&&player.surfaceKind==="GRASS")alert="CÉSPED";

 else if(!alert&&player.surface==="RUNOFF")alert="ESCAPATORIA";
 $("raceAlert").textContent=alert;$("raceAlert").style.color=alert==="SENTIDO CONTRARIO"?"#ff6678":alert.startsWith("FRENA")?"#ffdf58":alert.startsWith("BOX")?"#8dffb6":alert.startsWith("PIT")?"#69f5ff":alert==="REBUFO"?"#65ffc0":"#fff";
 const isMobileHud=document.body.classList.contains("mobile-landscape");
 let all=apexLiveRaceOrder(),pi=all.findIndex(a=>a.id===0);
 const tyreMark=apexWeatherIsWet()?"W":"S",leader=all[0],towerDen=Math.max(58,player.speed*3.6*.72,trackData.km*1.8),driverCode=name=>((name||"DRV").replace(/[^A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚÜÑ]/g,"").toUpperCase().slice(0,3)||"DRV"),gapFor=entry=>entry.id===leader.id?(currentSession==="race"?"LÍDER":((best<1e8&&entry.id===0)?bestTxt:currentTxt)):"+"+((((leader.s-entry.s)/towerDen)<10)?((leader.s-entry.s)/towerDen).toFixed(1):Math.round((leader.s-entry.s)/towerDen)),lapHead=currentSession==="race"?`${Math.min(player.lap+1,player.laps)}/${player.laps}`:`${player.lap+1}`;
 const place=hudPlaceInfo(trackData.spec),gpCity=place.city,circuitName=place.circuit,countryName=stripCountryEmoji(trackData.spec.country||'Gran Premio'),gpMetaLb=`${weatherHudMarkup(raceWeather,trackData.spec)} ${temp}°C`,flag=flagMarkup(trackData.spec),lb=$("lb");
 const circuitLine=circuitName?`<div class="gpCircuit">${circuitName}</div>`:'';
 if(soloPractice){
   const d=practiceDeltaMarkup(practiceLiveDelta());
   const sessionClock=String(Math.max(0,Math.floor(raceT/60))).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(Math.max(0,Math.floor(raceT%60))).padStart(2,'0');
   const bestEntry=sessionLapLog.reduce((m,r)=>Number.isFinite(r.time)&&(!m||r.time<m.time)?r:m,null);
   const bestLapNo=bestEntry?bestEntry.lap:0,bestLapTxt=bestEntry?fmt(bestEntry.time):'—:--.---';
   const currentLapNo=player.crossedStart?Math.max(1,(player.lap|0)+1):1;
   lb.classList.remove('positionTower');
   lb.classList.add('practiceSolo');
   lb.innerHTML=`<div class="tvgp"><div class="gpCountryStack"><span class="gpAbbr">${gpAbbr}</span><span class="gpFlag">${flag}</span></div><div class="gpTextStack"><div class="gpTitle">GP ${countryName}</div>${circuitLine}<div class="gpCityWx">${gpCity} · ${gpMetaLb}</div></div></div><div class="soloSessionClock"><span>TIEMPO SESIÓN</span><b>${sessionClock}</b></div><div class="tvt">ENTRENAMIENTOS LIBRES</div><div class="soloPracticeRows"><div class="soloPracticeCard"><span class="soloWho">TÚ</span><div class="soloLapStack"><div class="soloLapLine"><span class="soloLapNo">MEJOR · V${bestLapNo||'—'}</span><b>${bestLapTxt}</b></div><div class="soloLapLine current"><span class="soloLapNo">ACTUAL · V${currentLapNo}</span><b class="soloDelta ${d.cls}">${d.txt}</b></div></div></div></div>`;
   lb.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',apexBroadcastPanel());
   activeRenderer.drawMiniMap();updateTelemetry();return;
 }
 lb.classList.remove('practiceSolo');
 lb.classList.add('positionTower');
 const towerHead=`<div class="tvgp"><div class="gpCountryStack"><span class="gpAbbr">${gpAbbr}</span><span class="gpFlag">${flag}</span></div><div class="gpTextStack"><div class="gpTitle">GP ${countryName}</div>${circuitLine}<div class="gpCityWx">${gpCity} · ${gpMetaLb}</div></div></div><div class="tvh"><div><div class="small">VUELTA</div><div class="big">${lapHead}</div></div><div style="text-align:right"><div class="small">POS</div><div class="big">${player.pos}</div></div></div><div class="tvt">POSICIONES</div><div class="towerRows"></div>`;
 lb.innerHTML=towerHead+apexBroadcastPanel();
 const rowsBox=lb.querySelector('.towerRows'),lbStyle=getComputedStyle(lb),minRowH=Math.max(1,parseFloat(lbStyle.getPropertyValue('--tower-min-row-h'))||(isMobileHud?16:21));
 // v5.128: el tower clásico NO depende de clientHeight de un flex vacío.
 // En algunos Chrome Android, tras haber usado el HUD compacto de libres, ese valor puede ser 0
 // y el cálculo deja sólo una fila. Calculamos el hueco real a partir del panel y sus cabeceras.
 let rowsHeight=0;
 if(rowsBox){
   const panelH=Math.max(0,lb.clientHeight||lb.getBoundingClientRect().height||0);
   let fixedH=0;
   Array.from(lb.children).forEach(el=>{if(el!==rowsBox)fixedH+=Math.max(0,el.getBoundingClientRect().height||el.offsetHeight||0)});
   rowsHeight=Math.max(minRowH,panelH-fixedH);
 }
 const towerCount=Math.max(1,Math.min(all.length,Math.floor(rowsHeight/minRowH)));
 const visible=selectTowerEntries(all,pi,towerCount),fastId=currentSession==='race'?sessionFastestLap.id:null,fastFresh=fastId!==null&&raceT<sessionFastestLap.flashUntil;
 const toast=fastFresh?`<div class="fastLapToast"><span>VUELTA RÁPIDA · ${driverCode(raceName(fastId))}</span><b>${fmt(sessionFastestLap.time)}</b></div>`:'';
 if(rowsBox)rowsBox.innerHTML=toast+visible.map((a)=>{const me=a.id===0,teamKey=me?(player.teamKey||selectedTeamKey()):rivalTeamById(a.id),code=driverCode(raceName(a.id)),pos=all.findIndex(x=>x.id===a.id)+1,isFast=a.id===fastId;return `<div class="lbr ${me?"you ":""}${isFast?"fastestLap ":""}${isFast&&fastFresh?"freshFastest":""}">${apexSectorBars(apexTimingCar(a.id))}<span class="lpos">${pos}</span><span class="ldriver"><span class="ldc">${code}</span></span><span class="tvlogo">${teamLogoMarkup(teamKey,"tvLogoSvg")}</span><span class="tvtyre" title="${tyreMark==='S'?'Neumático blando':'Neumático de lluvia'}">${tyreMark}</span><span class="tvgap ${me?"self":""}">${apexTowerTimingValue(apexTimingCar(a.id),gapFor(a))}</span>${fastestLapIconMarkup()}</div>`;}).join("");
 activeRenderer.drawMiniMap();updateTelemetry();
}

// v5.230.62.2 · WARMUP DE RENDER: fuerza JIT, shader, crecimiento de buffers y
// rutas visuales de túneles antes de iniciar la cuenta atrás. No avanza física ni IA.
async function apexWarmRendererBeforeStart(btn=null){
 if(!trackData||!player||!activeRenderer||typeof activeRenderer.renderFrame!=='function')return null;
 // v5.230.62.4.1: el warmup es temporal. Nunca puede dejar deshabilitado el
 // botón que lanzó la sesión; de lo contrario al volver al menú ese mismo botón
 // quedaba disabled y parecía que APEX no permitía iniciar otra carrera.
 const btnState=btn?{disabled:!!btn.disabled,text:btn.textContent}:null;
 const t0=performance.now(),saved={x:player.x,z:player.z,s:player.s,total:player.total,lat:player.lat,heading:player.heading,trackIndex:player.trackIndex,track:player.track,progressS:player.progressS},L=trackData.km*1000,samples=[];
 const add=s=>{s=mod(+s||0,L);if(!samples.some(v=>Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(v,s,L))<18))samples.push(s)};add(saved.s);add(L*.25);add(L*.5);add(L*.75);
 const cc=trackData.compiledCircuit;if(cc&&Array.isArray(cc.portals))for(const p of cc.portals)if(samples.length<10)add(p.s);
 if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='CALENTANDO RENDER…'}
 try{
   resize();
   for(let i=0;i<samples.length;i++){
     const ss=samples[i],q=poseAt(ss);player.s=ss;player.total=ss;player.progressS=ss;player.lat=0;player.x=q.x;player.z=q.z;player.heading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);player.track=q;player.trackIndex=q.i;
     activeRenderer.renderFrame();if(i===0&&typeof activeRenderer.drawMiniMap==='function')activeRenderer.drawMiniMap();apexRaceLoadingUpdate('CALENTANDO RENDER…',.91+.065*((i+1)/Math.max(1,samples.length)));
     if((i&1)===1)await apexCompilerYield();
   }
   // Un segundo pase en el punto inicial fuerza buffers que crecieron en el primer recorrido.
   const q=poseAt(saved.s);player.s=saved.s;player.total=saved.total;player.progressS=saved.progressS;player.lat=saved.lat;player.x=saved.x;player.z=saved.z;player.heading=saved.heading;player.track=saved.track||q;player.trackIndex=saved.trackIndex;activeRenderer.renderFrame();
 }catch(e){console.warn('APEX: warmup de render incompleto',e)}finally{
   if(player)Object.assign(player,saved);
   if(btn&&btnState){btn.disabled=btnState.disabled;btn.textContent=btnState.text;}
 }
 const out={backend:rendererBackendId(),samples:samples.length,ms:Number((performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2))};if(trackData&&trackData._compileStats)trackData._compileStats.rendererWarmup=out;return out;
}

async function startRace(spec,laps,gm,btn){
 currentMode=gm;
 const weatherPreset=sessionSettings&&sessionSettings.weather?sessionSettings.weather:(gm==="quick"?$("weather").value:'VARIABLE');
 apexWeatherInit(weatherPreset,spec);
 // v62.6: al pulsar salir a pista el menú desaparece INMEDIATAMENTE. Todo el trabajo
 // posterior queda cubierto por una barra de progreso hasta que el simulador está listo.
 apexRaceLoadingShow(spec,'INICIANDO SESIÓN…',.015);

 // Abrir AudioContext dentro del gesto del usuario, antes del await de red.
 initAudio();
 if(audio.ctx&&audio.ctx.state==="suspended"){try{audio.ctx.resume()}catch(e){}}
 // El AudioContext ya se abrió dentro del gesto del usuario; cedemos ahora un frame para
 // que la pantalla de carga se pinte antes de cualquier trabajo pesado.
 await new Promise(r=>requestAnimationFrame(r));

 if(gm==="season"){
   if(!save.seasonTeam){
     save.seasonTeam=selectedTeamKey();
     const seat=driverRoster.find(d=>d.team===save.seasonTeam);
     save.seasonSeatUid=seat?seat.uid:null;
     persist();
   }
   if($("teamSelect")){
     $("teamSelect").value=save.seasonTeam;
     syncTeamPreset(false);
   }
 }

 const old=btn.textContent;
 btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent="CARGANDO TRAZADO…";apexRaceLoadingUpdate('CARGANDO TRAZADO…',.055);
 try{
   let td=await loadTrack(spec);apexRaceLoadingUpdate('INTERPRETANDO CIRCUITO…',.12);
   trackData={...td,spec};prepareTrackModel();apexRaceLoadingUpdate('PREPARANDO GEOMETRÍA…',.17);
   await apexCompileCircuitStatic((label,ratio)=>{if(btn)btn.textContent=`PREPARANDO CIRCUITO · ${Math.round(ratio*100)}% · ${label}`;apexRaceLoadingUpdate(label,.17+clamp(ratio,0,1)*.65);});
 }catch(e){
   btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=old;apexRaceLoadingRestore();
   if((gm==='season'||gm==='grandprix')&&weekendContext){if($('menu'))$('menu').classList.add('hidden');if($('weekendPanel'))$('weekendPanel').classList.remove('hidden');}
   alert("No he podido cargar la geometría real del circuito.\n\n"+e.message+"\n\nComprueba que tienes conexión la primera vez.");
   return;
 }
 btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=old;
 await startPreparedRace(spec,laps,gm,btn);
}

 let quickGridSlot=null;
 if(isRace&&gm==='quick'&&!grid){

   steer:0,longG:0,latG:0,drs:false,slipstream:false,lastSpeed:0,surface:"TRACK",surfaceKind:"ASPHALT",driveNetwork:"TRACK",pitS:0,pitLat:0,pitIndex:null,pitState:null,
   wallCooldown:0,trackIndex:q0.i,track:q0,trackDirDot:1,delta:0,slipAngle:0,slipF:0,slipR:0,
   frontUse:0,rearUse:0,frontLoad:0,rearLoad:0,downforce:0,balanceState:"ESTABLE",wheelspin:false,
   lockF:false,lockR:false,throttle:0,brakeInput:0,terrainDrag:0,lastGood:null,physicsFaults:0,
   carModel:team.chassis,carColor,carAccent,carSpec:car,teamKey,teamName:team.name,
   checkpoint:0,crossedStart:false,raceFirstLapPending:false,timingLapStart:0,timingSectorIndex:0,timingSectorSplits:[],timingSectorTimes:[],timingSpeedTrapKph:null,progressOriginS:progressOrigin,progressS:playerS,
   damage:freshDamage(),lastDamageZone:"",contactCooldown:0,
   reverse:false,reverseHold:0,cameraLook:0,cameraGradePitch:0,fovBoost:0,tyreSet:1,tyreAge:0,pitStops:0,pitStop:null,autoLapSeconds:0,autoUsedThisLap:false,lastLapAutoInvalid:false,
   autoPlan:null,autoPlanStamp:-99,autoLineStamp:0,autoTargetLat:playerLane,autoPassSide:0,autoPassUntil:0,autoPhase:"RECTA",
   autoPace:(currentSession==='race'?1.00:1.000),autoStableTime:0,autoRiskTime:0,autoLastRisk:0,autoLowSpeedSince:0,autoLaunchAssistUntil:0,

 last=performance.now();

function carAxes(){
 const h=player?player.heading:0;
 const fx=Math.sin(h),fz=Math.cos(h);
 return {fx,fz,rx:fz,rz:-fx,lx:-fz,lz:fx};
}
function wrapDeltaS(a,b,L){
 let d=a-b;
 if(d>L*.5)d-=L;
 if(d<-L*.5)d+=L;
 return d;
}
function nearestTrackState(x,z,hint,opts=null){
 const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,L=trackData.km*1000,step=L/n;
 let bestD=Infinity,bestI=0,bestT=0,bx=0,bz=0;
 const test=i0=>{
   const i=mod(i0,n),a=P[i],b=P[(i+1)%n],dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],den=dx*dx+dz*dz||1;
   const t=clamp(((x-a[0])*dx+(z-a[1])*dz)/den,0,1),px=a[0]+dx*t,pz=a[1]+dz*t;
   const dd=(x-px)*(x-px)+(z-pz)*(z-pz);
   if(dd<bestD){bestD=dd;bestI=i;bestT=t;bx=px;bz=pz}
 };
 if(Number.isFinite(hint)){
   // En AUT la proyección queda anclada al tramo local. Así una horquilla, un puente
   // o dos rectas próximas no pueden hacer que 's' salte a otra parte del circuito.
   const radius=opts&&Number.isFinite(opts.radius)?Math.max(4,Math.floor(opts.radius)):75;
   const allowGlobal=!(opts&&opts.allowGlobal===false),fallback=opts&&Number.isFinite(opts.fallbackDist)?opts.fallbackDist:55;
   for(let k=-radius;k<=radius;k++)test(hint+k);
   if(allowGlobal&&bestD>fallback*fallback)for(let i=0;i<n;i+=2)test(i);
 }else for(let i=0;i<n;i++)test(i);
 const s=mod((bestI+bestT)*step,L),q=poseAt(s),lat=(x-q.x)*q.nx+(z-q.z)*q.nz;
 return {...q,s,index:bestI,lat,dist:Math.sqrt(bestD),px:bx,pz:bz};
}
function syncPlayerTrack(){
 // El jugador manual conserva la búsqueda amplia para recuperarse de accidentes.
 // AUT usa una ventana pequeña y nunca salta globalmente a un tramo cercano distinto.
 const trackOpts=(demoMode||assistMode)?{radius:18,allowGlobal:false}:null;
 const t=nearestTrackState(player.x,player.z,player.trackIndex,trackOpts);
 player.trackIndex=t.index;player.track=t;player.s=t.s;player.lat=t.lat;
 const a=carAxes();player.trackDirDot=a.fx*t.tx+a.fz*t.tz;
 const pitState=syncPlayerDriveNetwork(t),surface=pitState?playerDriveSurfacePose():trackSurfacePoseAt(t.s,t.lat);
 player.surfaceY=surface.y;player.surfaceBank=surface.bank;
 return t;
}
function rivalWorld(r){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(r.s),surface=trackSurfacePoseAt(r.s,r.lat,.014);
 return {x:surface.x,z:surface.z,y:surface.y,q,surface};
}
function playerSurfaceState(){
 const env=envProfile(player&&Number.isFinite(player.s)?player.s:0),a=Math.abs(player.lat),track=env.half,kerb=track+env.curb,run=kerb+env.runoff;
 if(player&&player.driveNetwork==='PIT'&&player.pitState&&player.pitState.dist<=player.pitState.width*.58+.35)return {zone:"PIT",kind:"ASPHALT",grip:.96,steerGrip:.96,driveGrip:.97};
 const pit=pitLaneAtS(player&&Number.isFinite(player.s)?player.s:0);
 // La pista principal manda en la zona de mezcla salvo cuando el coche YA está en la red PIT exacta.
 if(a<=track)return {zone:"TRACK",kind:"ASPHALT",grip:1.00,steerGrip:1.00,driveGrip:1.00};
 if(pit&&Math.abs(player.lat-pit.centerLat)<=pit.width*.56)return {zone:"PIT",kind:"ASPHALT",grip:.96,steerGrip:.96,driveGrip:.97};
 if(a<=kerb)return {zone:"KERB",kind:"KERB",grip:.91,steerGrip:.88,driveGrip:.91};
 const drivable=apexDrivableAreaContains(player.x,player.z);if(drivable===false)return {zone:'OFF',kind:'BOUNDARY',grip:.08,steerGrip:.06,driveGrip:.08};
 const svgZone=apexSurfaceZoneAtWorld(player.x,player.z);
 if(svgZone){
   const k=String(svgZone.kind||'ASPHALT').toUpperCase();
   if(k==='WATER')return {zone:'WATER',kind:'WATER',grip:.06,steerGrip:.04,driveGrip:.05};
   if(k==='GRAVEL')return {zone:'RUNOFF',kind:'GRAVEL',grip:.34,steerGrip:.25,driveGrip:.42};
   if(k==='SAND')return {zone:'RUNOFF',kind:'SAND',grip:.28,steerGrip:.20,driveGrip:.36};
   if(k==='GRASS')return {zone:'RUNOFF',kind:'GRASS',grip:.38,steerGrip:.28,driveGrip:.48};
   return {zone:'RUNOFF',kind:'ASPHALT',grip:.78,steerGrip:.75,driveGrip:.82};
 }
 if(drivable===true)return {zone:'RUNOFF',kind:'ASPHALT',grip:.78,steerGrip:.75,driveGrip:.82};
 if(a<=run){
   if(env.runoffSurface==="ASPHALT")return {zone:"RUNOFF",kind:"ASPHALT",grip:.78,steerGrip:.75,driveGrip:.82};
   if(env.runoffSurface==="GRAVEL")return {zone:"RUNOFF",kind:"GRAVEL",grip:.34,steerGrip:.25,driveGrip:.42};
   if(env.runoffSurface==="GRASS")return {zone:"RUNOFF",kind:"GRASS",grip:.38,steerGrip:.28,driveGrip:.48};
 }
 if(env.wall&&!(trackData&&trackData.svgAuthoritative))return {zone:"WALL",kind:"WALL",grip:.20,steerGrip:.18,driveGrip:.25};
 if(env.offSurface==="GRAVEL")return {zone:"OFF",kind:"GRAVEL",grip:.31,steerGrip:.22,driveGrip:.40};
 if(env.offSurface==="SAND")return {zone:"OFF",kind:"SAND",grip:.28,steerGrip:.20,driveGrip:.36};
 if(env.offSurface==="GRASS")return {zone:"OFF",kind:"GRASS",grip:.35,steerGrip:.25,driveGrip:.46};
 return {zone:"OFF",kind:"ASPHALT",grip:.68,steerGrip:.63,driveGrip:.72};
}

function terrainDragAccel(ps,speed){
 speed=Math.max(0,finite(speed,0));
 if(ps.kind==="ASPHALT"&&ps.zone==="TRACK")return 0;
 if(ps.kind==="KERB")return .18+.006*speed;
 if(ps.kind==="ASPHALT")return .35+.018*speed+.00035*speed*speed;
 if(ps.kind==="GRASS")return .55+.070*speed+.00125*speed*speed;
 if(ps.kind==="GRAVEL")return .80+.145*speed+.0035*speed*speed;
 if(ps.kind==="SAND")return 1.00+.125*speed+.0031*speed*speed;
 if(ps.kind==="WATER")return 5.5+.22*speed+.0045*speed*speed;
 if(ps.kind==="WALL")return 8+speed*.18;if(ps.kind==="BOUNDARY")return 10+speed*.24;
 return .50+.025*speed;
}
function terrainName(kind,zone){
 if(zone==="TRACK")return "ASFALTO";
 if(zone==="PIT")return "PIT LANE";
 if(kind==="KERB")return "PIANO";
 if(kind==="WATER")return "AGUA";
 if(kind==="GRASS")return "CÉSPED";
 if(kind==="GRAVEL")return "GRAVA";
 if(kind==="SAND")return "ARENA";
 if(kind==="WALL")return "MURO";
 if(kind==="BOUNDARY")return "LÍMITE";
 if(kind==="ASPHALT")return "ASFALTO ESC."; return kind||zone;
}
function physicsSurface(){
 return playerSurfaceState();
}

function staticSafeFront(){return 800*9.81*((3.60-1.90)/3.60)}
function staticSafeRear(){return 800*9.81*(1.90/3.60)}

function snapshotPhysics(){
 if(!player)return null;
 return {x:player.x,z:player.z,vx:player.vx,vz:player.vz,heading:player.heading,yawRate:player.yawRate,s:player.s,total:player.total,lat:player.lat,speed:player.speed,longG:player.longG,latG:player.latG};
}
function physicsFinite(){
 if(!player)return false;
 return [player.x,player.z,player.vx,player.vz,player.heading,player.yawRate,player.s,player.total,player.lat,player.speed].every(Number.isFinite);
}
function recoverPhysics(reason="NUM"){
 if(!player)return;
 const g=player.lastGood;
 if(g){
   Object.assign(player,g);
   player.vx*=.45;player.vz*=.45;player.yawRate=0;player.longG=0;player.latG=0;
 }else{
   const q=poseAt(Number.isFinite(player.s)?player.s:0);
   player.x=q.x;player.z=q.z;player.vx=0;player.vz=0;player.heading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);player.yawRate=0;player.speed=0;player.longG=0;player.latG=0;
 }
 player.slipF=0;player.slipR=0;player.slipAngle=0;player.frontUse=0;player.rearUse=0;
 player.physicsFaults=(player.physicsFaults||0)+1;
 player.balanceState="RECUPERADO";
 try{syncPlayerTrack()}catch(e){}
}

function movePlayerWithWalls(dx,dz){
 const dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
 const steps=Math.max(1,Math.min(18,Math.ceil(dist/.42)));
 const sx=dx/steps,sz=dz/steps;
 let x=player.x,z=player.z,hint=player.trackIndex,hit=false,contact=null;

 for(let i=0;i<steps;i++){
   x+=sx;z+=sz;
   const t=nearestTrackState(x,z,hint);hint=t.index;
   const c=wallContactAtPose(x,z,player.heading,hint);
   if(c){
     x-=c.nx*(c.penetration+.045);
     z-=c.nz*(c.penetration+.045);
     hit=true;contact=c;break;
   }
 }
 player.x=finite(x,player.lastGood?player.lastGood.x:0);
 player.z=finite(z,player.lastGood?player.lastGood.z:0);
 player.trackIndex=hint;

 if(hit&&contact){
   const nx=contact.nx,nz=contact.nz;
   const vn=player.vx*nx+player.vz*nz;
   if(vn>0){
     const kind=contact.wall&&contact.wall.barrier?contact.wall.barrier.kind:"CONCRETE";
     const profile=barrierImpactProfile(kind);
     const restitution=vn>18?profile.restitution:Math.max(.02,profile.restitution*.65);
     player.vx-=(1+restitution)*vn*nx;
     player.vz-=(1+restitution)*vn*nz;
     const tx=-nz,tz=nx,vt=player.vx*tx+player.vz*tz;
     const tangentKeep=clamp(profile.tangentKeep-Math.min(.16,vn*.0028),.46,.92);
     const newVt=vt*tangentKeep;
     const newVn=player.vx*nx+player.vz*nz;
     player.vx=tx*newVt+nx*newVn;
     player.vz=tz*newVt+nz*newVn;

     const impulse=CAR_MASS*vn*(1+restitution);
     const rpx=contact.x-player.x,rpz=contact.z-player.z;
     const torque=rpx*(-impulse*nz)-rpz*(-impulse*nx);
     player.yawRate=clamp(player.yawRate+torque/CAR_IZ*.30,-2.7,2.7);

     const severity=clamp((vn-1.0)/25,0,1.7);
     if(severity>.02&&(player.wallCooldown||0)<=0){
       if(vn<5.4){
         const amount=clamp((vn-1.0)*.14*profile.damage,.12,1.1);
         applyPlayerDamage(contact.zone,amount,severity*.42*profile.damage);
         player.wallCooldown=.09;frameShake=Math.max(frameShake,(1.05+vn*.15)*profile.shake);
         soundScrape(clamp(vn/5.4,.22,1)*profile.scrape);
         if(vn>2.8)spawnImpactParticles(contact.x,contact.t.y+.10,contact.z,nx,nz,severity*.22*profile.scrape);
       }else{
         const amount=clamp((vn-1.0)*.50*profile.damage,.8,28);
         applyPlayerDamage(contact.zone,amount,severity*profile.damage);player.wallCooldown=.15;
         soundImpact((.42+severity*.38)*profile.scrape);impactCamera(nx,nz,severity*profile.shake);
         if(severity>.12)spawnImpactParticles(contact.x,contact.t.y+.12,contact.z,nx,nz,severity*.82*profile.scrape);
       }
     }
   }
 }
 if(hit&&contact&&(demoMode||assistMode)){player.autoLastWallSide=contact.side||0;player.autoLastWallNx=contact.nx||0;player.autoLastWallNz=contact.nz||0;}
 return hit;
}

function forwardDistanceS(from,to,L){return mod(to-from,L)}
function crossedTimingLine(prevS,currentS,lineS,L){
 if(!Number.isFinite(prevS)||!Number.isFinite(currentS)||!Number.isFinite(lineS)||!(L>0))return false;
 const ds=wrapDeltaS(currentS,prevS,L);if(ds<=1e-5||ds>Math.max(120,L*.18))return false;
 const d=forwardDistanceS(prevS,lineS,L);return d>1e-5&&d<=ds+1e-7;
}
function crossedForward(prevS,newS,target,L){return crossedTimingLine(prevS,newS,target,L)}

function apexSectorText(t){return Number.isFinite(t)?t.toFixed(3):'—';}
function apexTimingCar(id){return id===0?player:rivals.find(r=>r.id===id);}
function apexRecordBroadcastSector(car,i,t){
 if(!Number.isFinite(t))return;
 car.bestSectorTimes=car.bestSectorTimes||[];car.timingSectorColors=car.timingSectorColors||[];car.timingSectorDeltas=car.timingSectorDeltas||[];
 const pb=car.bestSectorTimes[i],cars=[player,...rivals].filter(Boolean),sb=cars.reduce((v,c)=>Math.min(v,Number.isFinite((c.bestSectorTimes||[])[i])?c.bestSectorTimes[i]:Infinity),Infinity);
 const color=t<=sb+.0005?'purple':(!Number.isFinite(pb)||t<pb-.0005?'green':'yellow');
 car.timingSectorColors[i]=color;car.timingSectorDeltas[i]=Number.isFinite(pb)?t-pb:null;
 car.bestSectorTimes[i]=Math.min(Number.isFinite(pb)?pb:Infinity,t);
 car.lastTimingEvent={sector:i+1,time:t,delta:car.timingSectorDeltas[i],color,at:raceT};
}
function apexCompleteBroadcastLap(car,times,elapsed){
 if(Number.isFinite(times[2]))apexRecordBroadcastSector(car,2,times[2]);
 car.lastSectorTimes=times.slice();car.lastSectorColors=(car.timingSectorColors||[]).slice();car.lastSectorDeltas=(car.timingSectorDeltas||[]).slice();car.lastTimingLapAt=raceT;
 if(!car.raceFirstLapPending&&times.every(Number.isFinite)&&(!Number.isFinite(car.broadcastBestLap)||elapsed<car.broadcastBestLap)){
  car.broadcastBestLap=elapsed;car.bestLapSectorTimes=times.slice();
 }
}
function apexBroadcastState(car){
 const frozen=car&&Number.isFinite(car.lastTimingLapAt)&&raceT-car.lastTimingLapAt<6;
 return {times:frozen?(car.lastSectorTimes||[]):(car&&car.timingSectorTimes||[]),colors:frozen?(car.lastSectorColors||[]):(car&&car.timingSectorColors||[]),frozen};
}
function apexTowerTimingValue(car,fallback){
 const e=car&&car.lastTimingEvent;
 if(e&&raceT-e.at<6)return `<span class="towerSectorTime ${e.color}">S${e.sector} ${apexSectorText(e.time)}</span>`;
 return fallback;
}
function apexSectorBars(car){
 const state=apexBroadcastState(car);return `<span class="sectorBars" aria-label="S1 S2 S3">${[0,1,2].map(i=>`<i class="${state.colors[i]||'pending'}" title="S${i+1}: ${apexSectorText(state.times[i])}"></i>`).join('')}</span>`;
}
function apexBroadcastPanel(){
 if(!player)return '';const state=apexBroadcastState(player),idx=Math.min(2,player.timingSectorIndex||0),prior=idx?((player.timingSectorSplits||[])[idx-1]||0):0;
 const live=player.crossedStart?Math.max(0,raceT-player.timingLapStart-prior):null,event=player.lastTimingEvent,fresh=event&&raceT-event.at<6;
 const delta=fresh&&Number.isFinite(event.delta)?(event.delta>=0?'+':'')+event.delta.toFixed(3):'—';
 return `<div class="sectorPanel"><div class="sectorPanelHead"><b>TÚ · ${state.frozen?'ÚLTIMA VUELTA':'PARCIALES'}</b><span>${state.frozen?'VUELTA COMPLETA':player.crossedStart?'S'+(idx+1):'ESPERANDO META'}</span></div><div class="sectorTimes">${[0,1,2].map(i=>{const active=!state.frozen&&player.crossedStart&&i===idx,v=Number.isFinite(state.times[i])?state.times[i]:active?live:null;return `<div class="sectorCell ${state.colors[i]||'pending'}"><span>S${i+1}${active?' · EN CURSO':''}</span><b>${apexSectorText(v)}</b></div>`}).join('')}</div><div class="sectorSplit ${fresh?event.color:''}">${fresh?'S'+event.sector+' · '+apexSectorText(event.time)+' s':'S1 → S2 → META'}<span>${fresh?'Δ personal '+delta:'S3 cierra la vuelta'}</span></div><div class="sectorLegend"><span class="purple">Sesión</span><span class="green">Personal</span><span class="yellow">Más lento</span></div></div>`;
}
function apexResultSectors(r){
 if(Array.isArray(r.sectors))return r.sectors;
 const car=r.uid==='PLAYER'?player:rivals.find(c=>c.id===r._raceId||c.uid===r.uid);
 return car&&car.bestLapSectorTimes||[];
}
function apexResultSectorCells(r){return [0,1,2].map(i=>`<td class="metric sectorResult">${apexSectorText(apexResultSectors(r)[i])}</td>`).join('');}

function apexArmRaceGridTimingCar(car,isPlayer=false){
 if(!car||currentSession!=='race'||!trackData)return false;const g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(trackData.spec,car.laps||(player&&player.laps)||1);
 car.crossedStart=true;car.raceFirstLapPending=true;car.raceFirstLapControlCrossings=0;car.raceFirstLapControlCrossingsRequired=g.firstLapControlCrossings||1;car.raceStartControlRelation=g.controlRelation||'UNKNOWN';car.raceStartSignalTime=raceT;car.completedLaps=car.completedLaps||0;
 apexResetDriverTimingState(car,raceT);if(isPlayer){lapStart=raceT;car.autoLapSeconds=0;car.autoUsedThisLap=false;car.lastLapAutoInvalid=false;car.currentLapTrace=[];car.lapTraceLastBin=-1;car.lapTraceLastTime=0;}
 return true;
}
function apexArmRaceGridTiming(){
 if(currentSession!=='race'||!player)return;apexArmRaceGridTimingCar(player,true);for(const rv of rivals)apexArmRaceGridTimingCar(rv,false);
}
function apexFirstLapControlCrossingAccepted(car,g){
 if(!car||!car.raceFirstLapPending)return true;car.raceFirstLapControlCrossings=(car.raceFirstLapControlCrossings||0)+1;const need=Math.max(1,car.raceFirstLapControlCrossingsRequired||g.firstLapControlCrossings||1);
 car.timingGateEvents=car.timingGateEvents||[];car.timingGateEvents.push({id:'finish-line',role:'finish-line',firstLap:true,crossing:car.raceFirstLapControlCrossings,required:need,accepted:car.raceFirstLapControlCrossings>=need,s:Number(g.finish.toFixed(3)),t:Math.max(0,raceT-finite(car.timingLapStart,raceT))});
 return car.raceFirstLapControlCrossings>=need;
}
function apexResetDriverTimingState(car,atTime){if(!car)return;car.timingLapStart=Number.isFinite(atTime)?atTime:raceT;car.timingSectorIndex=0;car.timingSectorSplits=[];car.timingSectorTimes=[];car.timingSectorColors=[];car.timingSectorDeltas=[];car.timingSpeedTrapKph=null;car.timingGateEvents=[];car.checkpoint=0;}
function apexUpdateIntermediateTiming(car,prevS,newS,L,speedMps){
 if(!car||!(L>0))return;const sectors=trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.sectorControls)?trackData.sectorControls:apexTimingSectorControls(trackData&&trackData.spec);let idx=Math.max(0,car.timingSectorIndex|0);
 // S1 y S2 son líneas de control físicas. El “sector” es sólo el intervalo ENTRE líneas; nunca dispara el crono por pertenencia.
 while(idx<sectors.length){const c=sectors[idx];if(!crossedTimingLine(prevS,newS,c.s,L))break;const split=Math.max(0,raceT-finite(car.timingLapStart,raceT)),prior=idx?finite(car.timingSectorSplits[idx-1],0):0;car.timingSectorSplits[idx]=split;car.timingSectorTimes[idx]=Math.max(0,split-prior);apexRecordBroadcastSector(car,idx,car.timingSectorTimes[idx]);car.timingGateEvents.push({id:c.id||`sector-${idx+1}-control`,role:'sector-line',sector:c.sector??idx+1,s:Number(c.s.toFixed(3)),t:split});idx++;car.timingSectorIndex=idx;car.checkpoint=idx;}
 for(const c of apexTimingSpeedTrapControls(trackData&&trackData.spec)){if(crossedTimingLine(prevS,newS,c.s,L)){car.timingSpeedTrapKph=Math.max(0,finite(speedMps,0)*3.6);car.timingGateEvents.push({id:c.id||'speed-trap',role:'speed-trap',s:Number(c.s.toFixed(3)),t:Math.max(0,raceT-finite(car.timingLapStart,raceT)),kph:car.timingSpeedTrapKph});}}
}
function apexCompletedSectorTimes(car,lapElapsed){
 const times=car&&car.timingSectorTimes||[],splits=car&&car.timingSectorSplits||[];
 // Una lectura ausente no se convierte en un sector ficticio ni desplaza S3 a S1.
 return [Number.isFinite(times[0])?times[0]:null,Number.isFinite(times[1])?times[1]:null,Number.isFinite(splits[1])?Math.max(0,finite(lapElapsed,0)-splits[1]):null];
}
window.crossedTimingLine=crossedTimingLine;
function apexTimingDiagnostics(){
 if(!trackData||!player)return null;const g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(trackData.spec,player.laps);return {circuit:trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir,startS:g.start,finishS:g.finish,offsetM:g.offset,playerS:player.s,playerTotalForClassification:player.total,crossedStart:player.crossedStart,firstLapPending:player.raceFirstLapPending,firstLapControlCrossings:player.raceFirstLapControlCrossings||0,firstLapControlCrossingsRequired:player.raceFirstLapControlCrossingsRequired||g.firstLapControlCrossings,controlRelation:g.controlRelation,lap:player.lap,sectorIndex:player.timingSectorIndex||0,sectorSplits:player.timingSectorSplits||[],speedTrapKph:player.timingSpeedTrapKph,gateEvents:player.timingGateEvents||[],controls:apexTimingPointControls(trackData.spec).map(c=>({id:c.id||c.role,role:c.role,sector:c.sector??null,s:Number(c.s.toFixed(3)),quality:c.quality||c.status||null})),timingGate:'AUTHORED_POINT_LINE_CROSSING_ONLY',straightMode:trackData.straightMode||null,operationalControls:trackData.operationalControls||[]};
}
window.apexTimingDiagnostics=apexTimingDiagnostics;
function apexTimingSelfTest(){
 const rows=[];for(const spec of calendar.slice(0,F1_SEASON_COUNT)){const j=apexBaseCircuitJson(spec.dir),L=+(j&&j.track&&j.track.lengthMeters||0),td=j&&j.timing||{},controls=Array.isArray(td.controls)?td.controls:[],points=controls.filter(c=>c&&['start-line','finish-line','sector-line','speed-trap'].includes(String(c.role||'').toLowerCase())&&Number.isFinite(+c.s));if(!(L>0)||points.length<4){rows.push({id:spec.dir,ok:false,reason:'TIMING_POINTS',points:points.length});continue}const eps=Math.min(2,L*.0002),tests=points.map(c=>{const ss=mod(+c.s,L);return{id:c.id||c.role,role:c.role,sector:c.sector??null,s:ss,forward:crossedTimingLine(mod(ss-eps,L),mod(ss+eps,L),ss,L),reverseRejected:!crossedTimingLine(mod(ss+eps,L),mod(ss-eps,L),ss,L)}}),ok=tests.every(t=>t.forward&&t.reverseRejected)&&points.filter(c=>c.role==='sector-line').length===2&&points.some(c=>c.role==='start-line')&&points.some(c=>c.role==='finish-line');rows.push({id:spec.dir,ok,points:tests.length,tests});}return {ok:rows.length===F1_SEASON_COUNT&&rows.every(r=>r.ok),count:rows.length,model:'POINT_LINE_CROSSING',rows};
}
window.apexTimingSelfTest=apexTimingSelfTest;
const PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS=240;
function recordPracticeLapTrace(sampleS){
 if(currentSession!=='freepractice'||!player||!player.crossedStart||!trackData)return;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,elapsed=Math.max(0,raceT-lapStart),f=apexLapFractionFromFinish(Number.isFinite(sampleS)?sampleS:player.s);
 const bin=clamp(Math.floor(f*PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS),0,PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS-1);
 if(!Array.isArray(player.currentLapTrace))player.currentLapTrace=[];
 // Si estamos justo cruzando meta no machacamos el inicio de la nueva vuelta con el
 // último subpaso de la anterior. updateLapValidation hará el reset de forma limpia.
 if(player.lapTraceLastBin>bin&&player.lapTraceLastBin>PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS*.82)return;
 const last=Math.max(-1,player.lapTraceLastBin|0),lastT=finite(player.lapTraceLastTime,0);
 if(bin>=last){
   const span=Math.max(1,bin-last);
   for(let i=last+1;i<=bin;i++)player.currentLapTrace[i]=lerp(lastT,elapsed,(i-last)/span);
   if(bin===last)player.currentLapTrace[bin]=elapsed;
   player.lapTraceLastBin=bin;player.lapTraceLastTime=elapsed;
 }
}
function practiceLiveDelta(){
 if(currentSession!=='freepractice'||!player||!player.crossedStart||!Array.isArray(player.bestLapTrace))return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,f=apexLapFractionFromFinish(player.s),bin=clamp(Math.floor(f*PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS),0,PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS-1);
 let ref=player.bestLapTrace[bin];
 if(!Number.isFinite(ref)){
   for(let d=1;d<12&&!Number.isFinite(ref);d++){
     if(bin-d>=0&&Number.isFinite(player.bestLapTrace[bin-d]))ref=player.bestLapTrace[bin-d];
     else if(bin+d<PRACTICE_TRACE_BINS&&Number.isFinite(player.bestLapTrace[bin+d]))ref=player.bestLapTrace[bin+d];
   }
 }
 if(!Number.isFinite(ref))return null;
 return Math.max(0,raceT-lapStart)-ref;
}
function practiceDeltaMarkup(delta){
 if(!Number.isFinite(delta))return{txt:'—',cls:'neutral'};
 const a=Math.abs(delta),txt=(delta<0?'−':'+')+(a<60?a.toFixed(3):fmt(a));
 return{txt,cls:delta<-.001?'gain':delta>.001?'loss':'neutral'};
}

function updateLapValidation(prevS,newS,prevTotal,newTotal){
 const L=trackData.km*1000,g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(trackData.spec,player.laps),finishS=g.finish,forwardOk=player.trackDirDot>.08;
 // En salida de parrilla FIA el crono empieza con la señal de salida, no al cruzar START LINE.
 // En sesiones no-race se conserva el armado por cruce de CONTROL/META.
 if(currentSession==='race'&&!player.crossedStart)apexArmRaceGridTimingCar(player,true);
 if(!player.crossedStart){
   if(forwardOk&&crossedTimingLine(prevS,newS,finishS,L)){player.crossedStart=true;player.raceFirstLapPending=false;lapStart=raceT;apexResetDriverTimingState(player,raceT);player.autoLapSeconds=0;player.autoUsedThisLap=false;player.lastLapAutoInvalid=false;player.currentLapTrace=[];player.lapTraceLastBin=-1;player.lapTraceLastTime=0;}
   return false;
 }
 if(forwardOk)apexUpdateIntermediateTiming(player,prevS,newS,L,player.speed);
 const crossedFinish=forwardOk&&crossedTimingLine(prevS,newS,finishS,L);if(!crossedFinish)return false;
 if(currentSession==='race'&&player.raceFirstLapPending&&!apexFirstLapControlCrossingAccepted(player,g))return false;
 const elapsed=Math.max(0,raceT-finite(player.timingLapStart,lapStart)),sectorTimes=apexCompletedSectorTimes(player,elapsed),sectorSplits=Array.isArray(player.timingSectorSplits)?player.timingSectorSplits.slice():[];
 apexCompleteBroadcastLap(player,sectorTimes,elapsed);
 if(currentSession==='race'&&player.raceFirstLapPending){
   player.raceFirstLapPending=false;player.lastCompletedLap=1;player.firstLapElapsed=elapsed;player.lap=1;sessionLapLog.push({lap:1,time:null,firstLap:true,elapsed,shortenedByM:Number(Math.max(0,L-g.firstFinishDistance).toFixed(3)),extendedByM:Number(Math.max(0,g.firstFinishDistance-L).toFixed(3)),controlRelation:g.controlRelation,controlCrossings:g.firstLapControlCrossings,sectors:sectorTimes,sectorSplits,timingGate:'FIA_GRID_START_TO_CONTROL_LINE'});lapStart=raceT;apexResetDriverTimingState(player,raceT);player.autoLapSeconds=0;player.autoUsedThisLap=false;player.currentLapTrace=[];player.lapTraceLastBin=-1;player.lapTraceLastTime=0;if(player.lap>=player.laps){finishRace();return true}return false;
 }
 const lt=elapsed,autoInvalid=!!player.autoUsedThisLap,improved=lt<best,completedTrace=Array.isArray(player.currentLapTrace)?player.currentLapTrace.slice():[];player.lastLapTime=lt;player.lastCompletedLap=player.lap+1;player.lastLapAutoInvalid=autoInvalid;sessionLapLog.push({lap:player.lap+1,time:lt,auto:autoInvalid,sectors:sectorTimes,sectorSplits,speedTrapKph:Number.isFinite(player.timingSpeedTrapKph)?player.timingSpeedTrapKph:null,timingGate:'FINISH_S_LINE_CROSSING'});best=Math.min(best,lt);if(currentSession==='freepractice'&&improved&&completedTrace.length){player.bestLapTrace=completedTrace;player.bestLapTraceTime=lt;}registerFastestLap(0,lt);if(!autoInvalid)recordSessionLap(lt);else{const a=$("raceAlert");if(a){a.textContent=improved?"VUELTA AUTO · MEJOR DE SESIÓN · NO GUARDADA":"VUELTA AUTO · NO GUARDADA";a.style.color="#ffd66b";}}lapStart=raceT;apexResetDriverTimingState(player,raceT);player.autoLapSeconds=0;player.autoUsedThisLap=false;player.lap++;player.currentLapTrace=[];player.lapTraceLastBin=-1;player.lapTraceLastTime=0;if(currentSession==='race'&&player.lap>=player.laps){finishRace();return true}if(sessionExpired&&currentSession!=='race'&&currentSession!=='freepractice'){finishNonRaceSession();return true}if(currentSession!=='race'&&currentSession!=='freepractice'&&player.lap>=player.laps){finishNonRaceSession();return true}return false;
}

// ---------- v4.9 RACECRAFT ----------
// Los rivales siguen usando s/lat (barato en móvil), pero sus decisiones ya son
// tácticas y persistentes. Los rasgos se derivan del UID: un mismo piloto conserva
// carácter entre carreras y temporadas.
function stableTrait(key,salt=0){
 let h=(2166136261 ^ Math.imul((salt+1),374761393))>>>0;
 key=String(key||"RIVAL");
 for(let i=0;i<key.length;i++){h^=key.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)>>>0;}
 h^=h>>>13;h=Math.imul(h,1274126177)>>>0;h^=h>>>16;
 return (h>>>0)/4294967295;
}
function initRivalRacecraft(rv){
 const k=rv.uid||("RIVAL-"+rv.id);
 rv.skill=.885+stableTrait(k,1)*.105;
 rv.aggression=.28+stableTrait(k,2)*.64;
 rv.consistency=.73+stableTrait(k,3)*.265;
 rv.bravery=.80+stableTrait(k,4)*.19;
 rv.defense=.26+stableTrait(k,5)*.68;
 rv.awareness=.78+stableTrait(k,6)*.21;
 rv.launch=.88+stableTrait(k,7)*.18;
 rv.reaction=.075+(1-rv.skill)*.80+stableTrait(k,8)*.075;
 rv.lineBias=(stableTrait(k,9)-.5)*.42;
 rv.paceOffset=(rv.skill-.935)*8.5+(stableTrait(k,10)-.5)*1.1;
 // v5.136: todos comparten el mismo envelope físico/Frenet; lo que cambia es el piloto.
 // Estos rasgos se mantienen estables por UID y gobiernan frenada, tracción, curva y ritmo.
 rv.brakeSkill=.86+stableTrait(k,12)*.14;
 rv.tractionSkill=.86+stableTrait(k,13)*.14;
 rv.cornerSkill=.88+stableTrait(k,14)*.12;
 const skillN=clamp((rv.skill-.885)/.105,0,1),consN=clamp((rv.consistency-.73)/.265,0,1);
 rv.racePaceProfile=clamp(.982+skillN*.024+consN*.006+(stableTrait(k,15)-.5)*.004,.978,1.014);
 rv.simLOD=0;rv.simModel="FAR_KINEMATIC";rv.aiPlanCache=null;rv.aiPlanNext=-99;rv.aiTargetCache=0;rv.aiTargetNext=-99;rv.parityTargetCache=0;rv.hybridGripUse=0;
 rv.intent="RACE";rv.intentTimer=0;rv.moveSide=0;
 rv.attackCooldown=0;rv.defendCooldown=0;rv.mistakeCooldown=7+stableTrait(k,11)*16;
 rv.mistakeTimer=0;rv.mistakeType="";rv.mistakeLat=0;
 rv.impactMode="NONE";rv.impactTimer=0;rv.impactYawRate=0;rv.impactStress=0;rv.impactSource=null;rv.impactOtherId=null;
 rv.cautionTimer=0;rv.contactRiskMemory=0;rv.contactAvoidSide=0;rv.impactContactCooldown=0;rv.rivalContactCooldown=0;rv.pairContactAt={};rv.incidentOfftrack=false;
 rv.lastRaceLine=rv.lat;rv.racecraftNote="RACE";rv.launchCorridorMix=0;rv.launchCorridorHold=currentSession==='race';
}
function rivalTraffic(rv){
 let ahead=null,behind=null;
 const consider=(id,total,lat,speed,team,obj)=>{
   const d=total-rv.total;
   if(d>0&&d<90&&(!ahead||d<ahead.d))ahead={id,d,lat,speed,team,obj};
   else if(d<0&&d>-55&&(!behind||-d<behind.d))behind={id,d:-d,lat,speed,team,obj};
 };
 consider(0,player.total,player.lat,player.speed,player.teamKey,player);
 for(const ov of rivals){if(ov!==rv&&!ov.finished)consider(ov.id,ov.total,ov.lat,ov.speed,ov.team,ov);}
 return {ahead,behind};
}
// Planner geométrico compartido por AUT y rivales. No mueve el coche: sólo decide
// qué curva importa, si forma parte de una enlazada y qué calle de pista conviene.
function driverCornerPlan(s,horizon=300,step=10){
 const threshold=.00135;
 let best={d:999,k:0,safe:98,score:0},first=null,last=null,prevSig=null,signChanges=0,samples=0;
 const groupGap=Math.max(44,step*4.5);
 for(let d=0;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const ss=s+d,k=curvatureAt(ss),safe=cornerSafeSpeed(ss),a=Math.abs(k);
   if(a<threshold&&safe>91)continue;
   const score=(98-safe)*1.42+a*1650-d*.017;
   const pt={d,k,safe,score};
   if(score>best.score)best=pt;
   if(!first){first=pt;last=pt;prevSig=pt;samples=1;continue;}
   // Sólo agrupamos la primera secuencia de curvas que viene por delante.
   if(d-last.d>groupGap)continue;
   if(prevSig&&Math.sign(k)!==Math.sign(prevSig.k)&&Math.abs(k)>.00165&&Math.abs(prevSig.k)>.00165)signChanges++;
   last=pt;prevSig=pt;samples++;
 }
 if(!first)return{...best,first:null,last:null,sequence:false,signChanges:0,samples:0};
 return{...best,first,last,sequence:signChanges>0&&last.d-first.d>24,signChanges,samples};
}
function driverRacingLineTarget(s,plan,skill=.96,bravery=.90,bias=0){
 const env=envProfile(s),limit=clamp(env.half-2.05,1.65,3.15);
 const outer=limit*(.68+.12*bravery),apex=limit*(.62+.16*skill);
 const kNow=curvatureAt(s+7),kPast=curvatureAt(s-24);
 let line=bias;
 if(plan&&plan.d<145&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.00135){
   const sg=Math.sign(plan.k)||1;
   if(plan.d>86){
     line+=sg*outer;
   }else if(plan.d>17){
     const t=clamp((86-plan.d)/69,0,1);
     // Entrada progresiva: exterior -> ápice. Smoothstep evita el latigazo de línea.
     const e=t*t*(3-2*t);
     line+=lerp(sg*outer,-sg*apex,e);
   }else{
     line+=-sg*apex;
   }
   // En una enlazada de sentidos opuestos, sacrificamos parte de la primera salida
   // para quedar colocados para la última curva del conjunto.
   if(plan.sequence&&plan.last&&plan.last.d<150&&Math.sign(plan.last.k)!==sg){
     const nextSg=Math.sign(plan.last.k)||1,w=clamp((150-plan.last.d)/115,.10,.62);
     const nextPrep=nextSg*outer*.92;
     line=lerp(line,nextPrep,w);
   }
 }else if(Math.abs(kPast)>.0019&&Math.abs(kNow)<Math.abs(kPast)*.70){
   // Salida: dejamos correr el coche hacia el exterior de la curva que acaba de pasar.
   line+=Math.sign(kPast)*outer*.88;
 }
 return clamp(line,-limit,limit);
}
function rivalSimulationLOD(rv){
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),near=mobile?120:150,mid=mobile?360:500,maxDynamic=mobile?5:8;
 const d=player?Math.abs((rv.total||0)-(player.total||0)):9999,prev=Number.isFinite(rv&&rv.simLOD)?rv.simLOD:null;
 let closer=0;
 if(d<=near+32)for(const ov of rivals||[]){if(ov!==rv&&Math.abs((ov.total||0)-(player?.total||0))<d)closer++;}
 // Histéresis: una entidad que ya está en un nivel no cambia justo en el umbral.
 // Evita saltos de trayectoria cuando el jugador se acerca/aleja unos pocos metros.
 if(prev===2&&d<=near+28&&closer<maxDynamic+2)return 2;
 if(prev===1&&d<=mid+65){
   if(d<=near-12&&closer<maxDynamic)return 2;
   return 1;
 }
 if(d<=near&&closer<maxDynamic)return 2;
 return d<=mid?1:0;
}
function rivalLODName(lod){return lod===2?"NEAR_DYNAMIC":lod===1?"MID_HYBRID":"FAR_KINEMATIC";}
function rivalLODCounts(){
 const out={nearDynamic:0,midHybrid:0,farKinematic:0};
 for(const rv of rivals||[]){const l=Number.isFinite(rv.simLOD)?rv.simLOD:rivalSimulationLOD(rv);if(l===2)out.nearDynamic++;else if(l===1)out.midHybrid++;else out.farKinematic++;}
 return out;
}
// Envelope de velocidad compartido por el AUT del jugador y los rivales. La geometría,
// la carga aerodinámica, la deceleración prevista y el margen de curva salen del mismo
// modelo; cada piloto sólo introduce sus parámetros propios y, en el jugador, memoria local.
function sharedDriverTrackTargetSpeed(s,opt={}){
 const perf=opt.perf||teamPerformance(opt.teamKey||player?.teamKey),skill=clamp(opt.skill??.96,.75,1),diff=Number.isFinite(opt.diff)?opt.diff:1;
 const wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),pace=Number.isFinite(opt.pace)?opt.pace:1,riskWeight=Number.isFinite(opt.riskWeight)?opt.riskWeight:.14;
 const baseDecel=(wet?9.8:14.0)*(.96+.08*skill)*perf.brake*(Number.isFinite(opt.decelScale)?opt.decelScale:1);
 let target=(wet?88:98)*perf.top;
 if(Number.isFinite(opt.topCap))target=Math.min(target,opt.topCap);
 const horizon=Number.isFinite(opt.horizon)?opt.horizon:(wet?330:370),step=Math.max(6,Number.isFinite(opt.step)?opt.step:8),safeScale=Number.isFinite(opt.safeScale)?opt.safeScale:1;
 for(let d=step;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const ss=s+d,e=envProfile(ss),wallish=apexWallishAtS(ss,e),k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss));
   const diffTrim=clamp(1+(diff-1)*.32,.98,1.018),wallTrim=wallish?.972:1.000;
   const learned=typeof opt.riskFn==="function"?clamp(opt.riskFn(ss)||0,0,1):0;
   let safe=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(ss)*pace*diffTrim*perf.aero*wallTrim*safeScale*(1-learned*riskWeight);
   if(wet)safe*=.90*perf.wet;
   const turnShare=clamp((k-.0025)/.0105,0,1),planDecel=baseDecel*(1-turnShare*.18),settle=d>18?8:3;
   const allowed=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,safe*safe+2*planDecel*Math.max(0,d-settle)));
   if(allowed<target)target=allowed;
 }
 return target;
}
function rivalCornerPlan(s,lod=1){return driverCornerPlan(s,lod===2?300:lod===1?260:220,lod===2?9:lod===1?12:16);}
function rivalRacingLine(rv,plan){return driverRacingLineTarget(rv.s,plan,rv.skill,rv.bravery,rv.lineBias);}
// El envelope físico compartido es una referencia excelente para decidir dónde frenar,
// pero un rival cinemático no paga slip, yaw ni transferencia de carga como el coche real.
// v5.136 lo dejó explotar ese envelope literalmente y el circuito de referencia cayó a un tiempo irreal. Este gobernador
// convierte el mismo plan en una velocidad equivalente a la física, sin añadir física completa.
function rivalKinematicParityTarget(rv,diff,lod=1){
 const perf=rv.teamPerf||teamPerformance(rv.team);
 const diffEq=clamp(.94+(diff-.94)*.18,.90,.975);
 const paceN=clamp(((rv.racePaceProfile||.996)-.978)/.036,0,1);
 const driverPace=.988+paceN*.020;
 let top=(88*diffEq+stableTrait(rv.uid,16)*1.35+(rv.paceOffset||0)*.65)*perf.top*driverPace;
 if(apexWeatherIsWet())top*=.955*perf.wet;
 const decel=(apexWeatherIsWet()?11.2:15.8)*(.91+.13*(rv.brakeSkill||rv.skill))*perf.brake;
 let target=top;
 const horizon=lod===2?250:lod===1?238:222,step=lod===2?12:lod===1?14:18;
 for(let d=step;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   let cs=cornerSafeSpeed(rv.s+d)*diffEq*(.915+.050*(rv.bravery||.9)+.035*(rv.cornerSkill||rv.skill))*perf.aero*driverPace;
   if(apexWeatherIsWet())cs*=.845;
   const allowed=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,cs*cs+2*decel*d));
   if(allowed<target)target=allowed;
 }
 return target;
}
function rivalBaseTargetSpeed(rv,diff,lod=1,plan=null){
 const perf=rv.teamPerf||teamPerformance(rv.team),now=raceT;
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),planGap=lod===2?(mobile?.050:.035):(lod===1?(mobile?.14:.10):(mobile?.30:.22));
 if(!plan||!rv.aiPlanCache||rv.aiPlanLOD!==lod||now>=(rv.aiPlanNext||-99)){
   rv.aiPlanCache=rivalCornerPlan(rv.s,lod);rv.aiPlanLOD=lod;rv.aiPlanNext=now+planGap+(lod===2?0:stableTrait(rv.uid,31)*.025);
 }
 plan=rv.aiPlanCache;
 const targetGap=lod===2?(mobile?.050:.035):(lod===1?(mobile?.14:.10):(mobile?.32:.24));
 if(!Number.isFinite(rv.aiTargetCache)||rv.aiTargetCache<=0||rv.aiTargetLOD!==lod||now>=(rv.aiTargetNext||-99)){
   const topCap=(rv.baseSpeed+rv.paceOffset)*perf.top*(apexWeatherIsWet()?.955*perf.wet:1);
   const safeScale=.982+.018*(rv.cornerSkill||rv.skill);
   const decelScale=.94+.08*(rv.brakeSkill||rv.skill);
   rv.aiTargetCache=sharedDriverTrackTargetSpeed(rv.s,{perf,skill:rv.brakeSkill||rv.skill,diff,pace:rv.racePaceProfile||1,safeScale,decelScale,topCap,horizon:lod===2?(mobile?330:350):lod===1?(mobile?280:310):250,step:lod===2?(mobile?10:8):lod===1?(mobile?14:11):18});
   rv.parityTargetCache=rivalKinematicParityTarget(rv,diff,lod);
   rv.aiTargetLOD=lod;rv.aiTargetNext=now+targetGap+(lod===2?0:stableTrait(rv.uid,32)*.03);
 }
 const traffic=rivalTraffic(rv),ahead=traffic.ahead,draft=!!ahead&&ahead.d<58&&Math.abs(ahead.lat-rv.lat)<1.65;
 let target=Math.min(rv.aiTargetCache,Number.isFinite(rv.parityTargetCache)?rv.parityTargetCache:rv.aiTargetCache);
 rv.sharedEnvelopeTarget=rv.aiTargetCache;rv.parityEnvelopeTarget=rv.parityTargetCache;
 if(draft)target+=.75+1.45*clamp((58-ahead.d)/48,0,1);
 const damage=.76+.24*((rv.damage??100)/100);target*=damage;
 return {target,traffic,draft,plan};
}
function applyRivalNearDynamics(rv,target,plan,dt,lod){
 if(rv.impactMode&&rv.impactMode!=='NONE')return target;
 if(lod!==2){rv.hybridGripUse=(rv.hybridGripUse||0)*Math.exp(-3*dt);return target;}
 const wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(rv.s+clamp(rv.speed*.18,4,13))),M=800,G=9.81,mu=wet?1.24:1.84;
 const down=Math.min(38000,2.72*rv.speed*rv.speed),latCap=mu*(M*G+down)/M,latNeed=rv.speed*rv.speed*k,use=latCap>0?latNeed/latCap:0;
 rv.hybridGripUse=lerp(rv.hybridGripUse||0,use,clamp(dt*7,0,1));
 const threshold=.91+.055*(rv.cornerSkill||rv.skill);
 if(use>threshold)target*=1-clamp((use-threshold)*.18,0,.075);
 // La orientación visible y las colisiones reciben una pequeña dinámica de carrocería.
 const lineErr=(rv.targetLat??rv.lat)-rv.lat,yawDemand=clamp(lineErr*.010+(rv.latVel||0)*.004,-.055,.055);
 rv.yawKick=lerp(rv.yawKick||0,yawDemand,clamp(dt*(2.2+2.8*(rv.awareness||.9)),0,1));
 return target;
}
function chooseAttackSide(rv,ahead,plan){
 const cornerSoon=plan.d<88&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.0018;
 if(cornerSoon&&rv.aggression>.48)return -Math.sign(plan.k||1); // interior
 if(Math.abs(ahead.lat)>.65)return ahead.lat>0?-1:1;
 return stableTrait(rv.uid,20+Math.floor(raceT/7))>.5?1:-1;
}
function beginRivalIntent(rv,intent,side,duration){
 rv.intent=intent;rv.moveSide=side||0;rv.intentTimer=duration;rv.racecraftNote=intent;
 if(sessionTelemetry&&player&&Math.abs((rv.total||0)-(player.total||0))<180)pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_INTENT',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,intent,side:side||0,distanceM:telemetryRound((rv.total||0)-(player.total||0),1)});
 if(intent==="ATTACK")rv.attackCooldown=2.6+(1-rv.aggression)*2.0;
 if(intent==="DEFEND")rv.defendCooldown=3.2+(1-rv.defense)*2.0;
}
function maybeRivalMistake(rv,dt,plan,traffic){
 rv.mistakeCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.mistakeCooldown||0)-dt);
 rv.mistakeTimer=Math.max(0,(rv.mistakeTimer||0)-dt);
 if(rv.mistakeTimer<=0){rv.mistakeType="";rv.mistakeLat=0;}
 if(rv.mistakeCooldown>0||raceT<5||rv.speed<30||plan.d>105)return;
 const pressure=(traffic.ahead&&traffic.ahead.d<11?1:0)+(traffic.behind&&traffic.behind.d<10?1:0);
 const wet=apexWeatherIsWet()?1:0;
 const rate=.00016+(1-rv.consistency)*.0017+pressure*.00034+wet*.00042+rv.aggression*.00008;
 if(Math.random()>=dt*rate)return;
 const roll=Math.random();
 rv.mistakeType=roll<.40?"WIDE":roll<.72?"LOCK":"SNAP";
 rv.mistakeTimer=rv.mistakeType==="WIDE"?(1.00+Math.random()*.32):rv.mistakeType==="LOCK"?.55:.68;
 rv.mistakeCooldown=18+Math.random()*28;
 const sg=Math.sign(plan.k||1),e=envProfile(rv.s);
 if(rv.mistakeType==="WIDE"){
   // v5.115: un error WIDE puede llevar realmente al rival hasta piano/escapatoria.
   // Antes el offset era pequeño y después todo quedaba recortado a ±3,55 m, así que
   // visualmente los rivales eran casi incapaces de salirse de la pista.
   const excursion=Math.min(e.half+e.curb+Math.min(e.runoff*.42,1.15),7.35);
   rv.mistakeLat=sg*excursion;
 }else rv.mistakeLat=(Math.random()<.5?-1:1)*(.45+Math.random()*.65);
 if(rv.mistakeType==="SNAP")rv.yawKick=clamp((rv.yawKick||0)+(Math.random()<.5?-1:1)*(.055+.035*rv.aggression),-.48,.48);
 if(sessionTelemetry&&player&&Math.abs((rv.total||0)-(player.total||0))<260)pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_MISTAKE',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,mistake:rv.mistakeType,distanceM:telemetryRound((rv.total||0)-(player.total||0),1)});
}
function updateRivalRacecraft(rv,dt,L,diff){
 rv.attackCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.attackCooldown||0)-dt);
 rv.defendCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.defendCooldown||0)-dt);
 rv.intentTimer=Math.max(0,(rv.intentTimer||0)-dt);
 updateRivalIncidentTimers(rv,dt);
 const prevLod=Number.isFinite(rv.simLOD)?rv.simLOD:null,lod=rivalSimulationLOD(rv);
 if(prevLod!==null&&prevLod!==lod)rv.lodTransitionTimer=.72;
 rv.lodTransitionTimer=Math.max(0,(rv.lodTransitionTimer||0)-dt);
 rv.simLOD=lod;rv.simModel=rivalLODName(lod);
 const base=rivalBaseTargetSpeed(rv,diff,lod),plan=base.plan,traffic=base.traffic,ahead=traffic.ahead,behind=traffic.behind;
 // Los errores completos se evalúan cerca/medio. Lejos se conserva personalidad pero
 // con una frecuencia menor para no gastar CPU en coches que el jugador ni ve.
 if((rv.impactMode||'NONE')==='NONE'&&(lod>0||raceT>=(rv.nextFarMistakeCheck||0))){maybeRivalMistake(rv,dt,plan,traffic);if(lod===0)rv.nextFarMistakeCheck=raceT+.35;}

 // Un coche que acaba de golpear o ser golpeado deja de atacar durante unos instantes.
 // La duración depende de awareness/consistency/aggression: no todos reaccionan igual.
 const incidentActive=(rv.impactMode||'NONE')!=='NONE'||(rv.cautionTimer||0)>0;
 if(incidentActive){rv.intent='RACE';rv.intentTimer=0;rv.moveSide=0;rv.racecraftNote=(rv.impactMode&&rv.impactMode!=='NONE')?rv.impactMode:'CAUTION';}
 // Estados tácticos. Se mantienen un tiempo mínimo para evitar zigzag artificial.
 else if(rv.intentTimer<=0){
   rv.intent="RACE";rv.moveSide=0;rv.racecraftNote="RACE";
   if(raceT>10.5&&(rv.launchCorridorMix||0)>.58){
     const sameTeam=ahead&&ahead.team===rv.team;
     const closing=ahead?rv.speed-ahead.speed:0;
     const attackWindow=ahead&&ahead.d<27&&Math.abs(ahead.lat-rv.lat)<4.6&&
       (closing>.35||(base.draft&&base.target>ahead.speed+1.8))&&(!sameTeam||rv.aggression>.72)&&
       (plan.d>48||rv.aggression>.66);
     if(attackWindow&&rv.attackCooldown<=0){
       const side=chooseAttackSide(rv,ahead,plan);
       beginRivalIntent(rv,"ATTACK",side,.72+rv.aggression*.78);
     }else if(behind&&behind.d<17&&rv.defendCooldown<=0&&rv.defense>.39){
       const closingB=behind.speed-rv.speed;
       if(closingB>.35||behind.d<10){
         const side=(plan.d<120&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.0016)?-Math.sign(plan.k):(behind.lat>=rv.lat?1:-1);
         beginRivalIntent(rv,"DEFEND",side,1.05+rv.defense*.75);
       }
     }
   }
 }

 let line=rivalRacingLine(rv,plan),target=base.target;
 target=applyRivalNearDynamics(rv,target,plan,dt,lod);
 // v5.149: los rivales también leen los vértices poligonales fuertes. En un circuito importado el
 // jugador ya podía salvar T1, pero el pelotón seguía generando decenas de incidentes
 // alrededor de los nodos de ~101° y ~46°. Los rivales cinemáticos no necesitan un
 // "volante" especial: basta con frenar antes, abandonar ATTACK/DEFEND y centrar la línea.
 const rvKink=geometricKinkAhead(rv.s,104,4),rvKinkAngle=rvKink.angle||0;
 const rvKinkExtreme=rvKinkAngle>Math.PI*.47,rvKinkHard=rvKinkAngle>Math.PI*.31,rvKinkMedium=rvKinkAngle>Math.PI*.20;
 const rvKinkApproach=rvKinkExtreme?96:(rvKinkHard?78:(rvKinkMedium?66:0));
 rv.kinkGuard=false;
 if(rvKink.active&&rvKinkMedium&&rvKink.d<rvKinkApproach){
   const apex=rvKinkExtreme?7.6:(rvKinkHard?10.2:12.4);
   const gain=rvKinkExtreme?.18:(rvKinkHard?.20:.21);
   target=Math.min(target,apex+clamp((rvKink.d-3)*gain,0,rvKinkExtreme?16:(rvKinkHard?14:12)));
   const centerD=rvKinkExtreme?86:(rvKinkHard?64:50);
   if(rvKink.d<centerD&&!(currentSession==='race'&&raceT<10&&rv.launchCorridorHold)){
     line=lerp(line,0,clamp((centerD-rvKink.d)/Math.max(18,centerD-8),0,rvKinkExtreme?.90:(rvKinkHard?.78:.62)));
   }
   if(rvKink.d<72){rv.intent="RACE";rv.intentTimer=0;rv.moveSide=0;rv.racecraftNote="KINK";}
   rv.kinkGuard=true;rv.kinkAngle=rvKinkAngle;rv.kinkD=rvKink.d;
 }else{rv.kinkAngle=rvKinkAngle;rv.kinkD=rvKink.active?rvKink.d:null;}
 // v5.143: corredor real de salida. La v5.142 empezaba a abandonar la calle de parrilla
 // a los 2,6 s; las cinco telemetrías mostraron que las dos columnas convergían hasta dejar
 // sólo ~2,0-2,3 m entre centros y los primeros contactos aparecían a los 3,5-4,0 s.
 // Ahora cada coche conserva SU columna mientras el pelotón siga comprimido y sólo mezcla
 // gradualmente hacia la trazada cuando existe espacio longitudinal real.
 const gridLane=Number.isFinite(rv.gridLat)?rv.gridLat:rv.lat;
 const corridorPacked=!!((ahead&&ahead.d<14.0)||(behind&&behind.d<13.0));
 if(!Number.isFinite(rv.launchCorridorMix))rv.launchCorridorMix=0;
 if(currentSession==='race'&&raceT<14.5){
   const mayRelease=raceT>6.6&&!corridorPacked;
   if(mayRelease)rv.launchCorridorMix=clamp(rv.launchCorridorMix+dt/3.4,0,1);
   line=lerp(gridLane,line,rv.launchCorridorMix);
   rv.launchCorridorHold=rv.launchCorridorMix<.985;
 }else{rv.launchCorridorMix=1;rv.launchCorridorHold=false;}
 const pds=player?player.total-rv.total:99,pLat=player?player.lat-rv.lat:99;
 rv.launchGapGuard=false;
 // v5.139: en parrilla, el coche de dos posiciones atrás comparte la misma calle a ~9.8 m.
 // Las telemetrías v5.137 mostraron impactos traseros a 1.18 s y 1.52 s porque un rival
 // con mejor lanzamiento podía cerrar esos metros antes de que actuara el antiguo límite de 3.8 m.
 // Mantenemos una distancia longitudinal realista durante los primeros 10 s sin teletransportar
 // ni frenar al pelotón entero: sólo actúa cuando el rival viene por la misma calle y detrás.
 const samePlayerCorridor=player&&Math.abs((player.gridLat??player.lat)-(rv.gridLat??rv.lat))<.85;
 if(player&&currentSession==='race'&&raceT<12.5&&pds>0&&pds<11.8&&samePlayerCorridor){
   const desiredGap=7.0,allowedClosing=clamp((pds-desiredGap)*.55,-1.8,1.8);
   target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,player.speed+allowedClosing));
   if(pds<6.15)target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,player.speed-1.25));
   rv.launchGapGuard=true;
 }
 if(player&&Math.abs(pds)<5.2&&Math.abs(pLat)<1.72&&(!rv.launchCorridorHold||raceT>8.5)){
   line+=pLat>=0?-.42:.42;
   if(pds>0&&pds<3.8)target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,player.speed+1.2));
 }
 for(const ov of rivals){
   if(ov===rv||ov.finished)continue;
   const ds=ov.total-rv.total,lg=ov.lat-rv.lat;
   const sameGridCorridor=Math.abs((ov.gridLat??ov.lat)-(rv.gridLat??rv.lat))<.85;
   if(currentSession==='race'&&raceT<12.5&&ds>0&&ds<11.8&&sameGridCorridor){
     const desiredGap=7.0,allowedClosing=clamp((ds-desiredGap)*.55,-1.8,1.8);
     target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,ov.speed+allowedClosing));
     if(ds<6.15)target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,ov.speed-1.25));
     rv.launchGapGuard=true;
   }
   if(Math.abs(ds)<4.0&&Math.abs(lg)<1.58&&(!rv.launchCorridorHold||raceT>8.5))line+=lg>=0?-.24:.24;
 }
 if(rv.intent==="ATTACK"&&ahead){
   // Mantiene una calle lateral suficiente para llegar realmente lado a lado.
   line=clamp(ahead.lat+rv.moveSide*(2.05+.35*rv.aggression),-3.20,3.20);
   if(ahead.d<9.0&&Math.abs(ahead.lat-line)<1.65)target=Math.min(target,ahead.speed+1.1);
   else if(base.draft&&plan.d>55)target+=.7+.9*rv.aggression;
 }else if(rv.intent==="DEFEND"){
   // Un solo movimiento defensivo, después mantiene la posición.
   line=clamp(rv.moveSide*(1.25+.75*rv.defense)+rv.lineBias*.20,-2.75,2.75);
 }

 // v5.144: prudencia individual y memoria del contacto. Un piloto consciente deja más
 // espacio; uno agresivo acepta un margen menor, pero si golpea tampoco queda inmune.
 const safetyScale=rivalAvoidanceScale(rv);
 if(rv.cautionTimer>0&&ahead&&ahead.d<18*safetyScale){
   const allowed=clamp((ahead.d-7.2*safetyScale)*.30,-1.8,1.4);
   target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,ahead.speed+allowed));
 }
 // Evitación de emergencia: márgenes distintos por personalidad y memoria de golpes.
 if(ahead){
   const latGap=Math.abs(ahead.lat-rv.lat),tightD=6.4*safetyScale,softD=11.5*safetyScale;
   const tightLat=1.50+Math.max(-.10,(safetyScale-1)*.30),softLat=1.82+Math.max(-.10,(safetyScale-1)*.34);
   if(ahead.d<tightD&&latGap<tightLat)target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,ahead.speed-(tightD-ahead.d)*(1.72+.30*safetyScale)));
   else if(ahead.d<softD&&latGap<softLat)target=Math.min(target,ahead.speed+clamp((ahead.d-tightD)*.38,.05,2.0));
 }
 for(const ov of rivals){
   if(ov===rv||ov.finished)continue;
   const ds=ov.total-rv.total,latGap=Math.abs(ov.lat-rv.lat),pairD=7.0*safetyScale,pairLat=1.44+Math.max(-.08,(safetyScale-1)*.28);
   if(ds>0&&ds<pairD&&latGap<pairLat){
     target=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,ov.speed-(pairD-ds)*(1.28+.24*safetyScale)));
     line+=rv.lat>=ov.lat?.30*safetyScale:-.30*safetyScale;
   }
 }

 if(rv.mistakeTimer>0){
   if(rv.mistakeType==="WIDE")line=rv.mistakeLat;
   else line+=rv.mistakeLat;
   if(rv.mistakeType==="WIDE")target*=.92;
   else if(rv.mistakeType==="LOCK")target*=.86;
   else if(rv.mistakeType==="SNAP")target*=.90;
   rv.racecraftNote=rv.mistakeType;
 }
 // Si el rival está realmente fuera, la superficie también le cuesta tiempo. No usamos
 // la física completa del jugador, pero ya no conserva el mismo ritmo sobre grava/césped.
 const rivalEnv=envProfile(rv.s),rivalAbsLat=Math.abs(rv.lat);
 if(rivalAbsLat>rivalEnv.half){
   const beyondKerb=rivalAbsLat>rivalEnv.half+rivalEnv.curb;
   let surfFactor=.94;
   if(beyondKerb){
     const surf=rivalEnv.runoffSurface||rivalEnv.offSurface;
     surfFactor=surf==="GRAVEL"?.74:surf==="GRASS"?.79:surf==="SAND"?.70:.90;
   }
   target*=surfFactor;
 }
 const incidentAdj=applyRivalIncidentTargets(rv,target,line);target=incidentAdj.target;line=incidentAdj.line;

 // Reacción y lanzamiento diferenciados.
 if(raceT<rv.reaction)target=0;
 const launchFactor=raceT<3.2?rv.launch:1;
 const perf=rv.teamPerf||teamPerformance(rv.team);
 if((rv.lodTransitionTimer||0)>0){
   const k=clamp(dt*3.4,0,1);
   if(Number.isFinite(rv.liveTargetSpeed))target=lerp(rv.liveTargetSpeed,target,k);
   if(Number.isFinite(rv.liveTargetLat))line=lerp(rv.liveTargetLat,line,k);
 }
 rv.liveTargetSpeed=target;rv.liveTargetLat=line;rv.livePlanD=plan&&Number.isFinite(plan.d)?plan.d:null;rv.livePlanK=plan&&Number.isFinite(plan.k)?plan.k:null;rv.liveDraft=!!base.draft;
 const accel=(8.45+4.05*(rv.tractionSkill||rv.skill))*launchFactor*perf.traction;
 const brake=(apexWeatherIsWet()?17.5:23.5)*(.91+.11*(rv.brakeSkill||rv.skill))*perf.brake;
 rv.speed+=clamp(target-rv.speed,-brake*dt,accel*dt);
 rv.speed=Math.max(0,rv.speed);

 // La línea no cambia instantáneamente. En conducción normal seguimos manteniendo una
 // banda prudente; un WIDE sí puede alcanzar el borde/escapatoria y después vuelve de
 // forma progresiva a la trazada, sin un snap invisible a ±3,55 m.
 const laneEnv=envProfile(rv.s),normalLaneLimit=Math.min(3.55,Math.max(2.75,laneEnv.half-.92));
 const excursionLimit=Math.min(laneEnv.half+laneEnv.curb+Math.min(laneEnv.runoff,1.35),7.65);
 const impactMode=rv.impactMode||'NONE';
 const incidentExcursion=impactMode!=='NONE';
 const targetLaneLimit=((rv.mistakeTimer>0&&rv.mistakeType==="WIDE")||incidentExcursion)?excursionLimit:normalLaneLimit;
 line=clamp(line,-targetLaneLimit,targetLaneLimit);
 rv.targetLat=line;
 const baseLineRate=(rv.intent==="ATTACK"?1.55:rv.intent==="DEFEND"?1.30:1.05)*(.90+.18*rv.awareness);
 const lodLineMul=lod===2?.92:lod===1?1.00:1.08;
 let lineRate=((rv.mistakeTimer>0&&rv.mistakeType==="WIDE")?Math.max(2.25,baseLineRate*1.85):baseLineRate)*lodLineMul;
 const prevLat=rv.lat;
 if(impactMode==='SPIN'){
   rv.latVel=(rv.latVel||0)*Math.exp(-.24*dt);rv.lat+=rv.latVel*dt;
   lineRate*=.035;rv.lat=lerp(rv.lat,rv.targetLat,clamp(dt*lineRate,0,.035));
   rv.yawKick=clamp((rv.yawKick||0)+(rv.impactYawRate||0)*dt,-2.75,2.75);rv.impactYawRate=(rv.impactYawRate||0)*Math.exp(-.30*dt);
 }else if(impactMode==='WOBBLE'){
   rv.latVel=(rv.latVel||0)*Math.exp(-.72*dt);rv.lat+=rv.latVel*dt;
   lineRate*=.24;rv.lat=lerp(rv.lat,rv.targetLat,clamp(dt*lineRate,0,.24));
   rv.yawKick=clamp((rv.yawKick||0)+(rv.impactYawRate||0)*dt,-1.35,1.35);rv.impactYawRate=(rv.impactYawRate||0)*Math.exp(-1.05*dt);
 }else if(impactMode==='RECOVER'){
   rv.latVel=(rv.latVel||0)*Math.exp(-1.35*dt);rv.lat+=rv.latVel*dt;
   lineRate*=.52;rv.lat=lerp(rv.lat,rv.targetLat+Math.sin(rv.phase+raceT*.19)*.035,clamp(dt*lineRate,0,.52));
   rv.impactYawRate=(rv.impactYawRate||0)*Math.exp(-1.65*dt);rv.yawKick=lerp(rv.yawKick||0,0,clamp(dt*.92,0,1));
 }else{
   rv.latVel=(rv.latVel||0)*Math.exp(-(rv.mistakeTimer>0&&rv.mistakeType==="WIDE"?1.75:(lod===2?2.55:3.15))*dt);
   rv.lat+=rv.latVel*dt;rv.lat=lerp(rv.lat,rv.targetLat+Math.sin(rv.phase+raceT*.19)*.055,clamp(dt*lineRate,0,1));
   if(lod===2)rv.latVel=lerp(rv.latVel||0,(rv.lat-prevLat)/Math.max(dt,.001),clamp(dt*3.2,0,1));
   rv.impactYawRate=(rv.impactYawRate||0)*Math.exp(-2.2*dt);rv.yawKick=(rv.yawKick||0)*Math.exp(-2.45*dt);
 }
 rv.lat=clamp(rv.lat,-excursionLimit,excursionLimit);rv.lastRaceLine=rv.lat;
 rv.contactCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.contactCooldown||0)-dt);rv.wallCooldown=Math.max(0,(rv.wallCooldown||0)-dt);
 const prevRivalS=rv.s;rv.total+=rv.speed*dt;rv.s=mod((Number.isFinite(rv.progressOriginS)?rv.progressOriginS:0)+rv.total,L);
 updateRivalLapTiming(rv,prevRivalS,rv.s,L);
 resolveRivalWallCollision(rv);
 const postEnv=envProfile(rv.s),nowIncidentOff=Math.abs(rv.lat)>postEnv.half+Math.min(postEnv.curb*.45,.35);
 if(nowIncidentOff&&!rv.incidentOfftrack&&impactMode!=='NONE'&&sessionTelemetry&&rivalIncidentNearPlayer(rv,320))pushTelemetryEvent('RIVAL_OFFTRACK',{rivalId:rv.id,rivalUid:raceIdentity(rv.id),rivalName:raceName(rv.id),team:rv.team,mode:impactMode,source:rv.impactSource||'CONTACT',lat:telemetryRound(rv.lat,2),speedKph:telemetryRound(rv.speed*3.6,1)});
 rv.incidentOfftrack=nowIncidentOff;
}

function resetAutoDriveState(){
 if(!player)return;
 player.reverse=false;player.reverseHold=0;player.gyroNeutral=false;
 player.autoPlan=null;player.autoPlanStamp=-99;player.autoLineStamp=raceT;player.autoTargetLat=player.lat;player.autoPassSide=0;player.autoPassUntil=0;
 player.autoRecovery=null;player.autoStuckSince=0;player.autoLastWallHit=-99;player.autoLastWallSide=0;player.autoRecoveryProbe=null;
 player.autoPace=currentSession==='race'?1.00:1.000;player.autoStableTime=0;player.autoRiskTime=0;player.autoLastRisk=0;player.autoLowSpeedSince=0;player.autoLaunchAssistUntil=0;player.autoTechnicalActive=false;
 player.autoLastRivalContact=-99;player.autoLastRivalContactId=null;player.autoLastRivalContactClosing=0;player.autoRaceTraffic=null;player.autoRacePackRisk=0;player.autoRaceStartProtect=false;player.autoLaunchCorridorHold=false;player.autoFirstComplexLaneLock=false;player.autoTechnicalRiskPrep=0;player.autoKinkCommitUntil=-99;player.autoKinkCommitHeading=null;player.autoKinkCommitAngle=0;player.autoKinkCommitActive=false;player.autoKinkCommitRemaining=0;player.autoKinkCommitBlend=0;player.autoLaneSwingGuard=false;player.autoLaneSwingLimit=null;player.autoContactGuardUntil=-99;player.autoContactHoldLat=player.lat;player.autoRaceSurvivalActive=false;player.autoRaceSurvivalTarget=Infinity;player.autoRaceSurvivalCornerD=null;player.autoRaceSurvivalSafe=null;player.autoRaceSurvivalCleanRelease=false;player.autoRaceSurvivalRiskAhead=0;player.telemetryLastContainedContact=-99;player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed=0;player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed=0;player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit=null;player.autoTargetSpeedReason="TRACK";player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail=null;player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed=null;player.autoCommittedTargetStamp=raceT;player.autoEmergencyUnstickAt=-99;player.autoRecoveryAssist=false;player.autoRecoveryForcePull=false;player.autoRearThreat=0;player.autoTrafficEscapeLane=false;player.autoLimiterReduction=0;
 if(!player.autoRiskBins)autoEnsureRiskMemory();
}
function applyAutoDriveUI(){
 document.body.classList.toggle("demo-active",demoMode);
 document.body.classList.toggle("assist-active",assistMode);
 const b=$("demo");if(b){b.classList.toggle("on",demoMode);b.classList.toggle("assist",assistMode);b.textContent="AUT";b.title=demoMode?"AUT completo":assistMode?"AUT asistido · tú controlas el volante":"Conducción automática";}
 const m=$("demoModeToggle");if(m&&m.checked!==demoMode)m.checked=demoMode;
 updatePitButtonUI();
}
function setAutoDriveMode(mode,reset=true){
 mode=mode===1?1:mode===2?2:0;
 demoMode=mode===1;assistMode=mode===2;
 if(demoMode){keys.left=false;keys.right=false;keys.gas=false;keys.brake=false;}
 if((demoMode||assistMode)&&reset)resetAutoDriveState();
 if(!demoMode&&!assistMode&&player){player.autoRecovery=null;player.autoStuckSince=0;player.autoRecoveryProbe=null;}
 // Keep BOX request while the driver takes manual control.
 applyAutoDriveUI();updatePitButtonUI();
 const a=$("raceAlert");if(a&&player&&reset){a.textContent=demoMode?"AUT · CONDUCCIÓN COMPLETA":assistMode?"AUT · VELOCIDAD ASISTIDA":"AUT · OFF";a.style.color=assistMode?"#ffad66":demoMode?"#fff1bd":"#d8e2ed";setTimeout(()=>{if(a.textContent.startsWith("AUT ·"))a.textContent=""},1100);}
}
function setDemoMode(on){setAutoDriveMode(on?1:0,true)}
function cycleAutoDriveMode(){setAutoDriveMode(demoMode?2:assistMode?0:1,true)}
function autoSafeLaneLimit(s){
 const e=envProfile(s),wc=apexWallContextAtS(s,e),wallish=wc.wallish;
 // Si el SVG tiene un muro real junto a la pista dejamos una reserva adicional.
 // El límite sigue siendo simétrico para no alterar la convención histórica de lat,
 // pero ahora nace de la misma geometría que puede golpear físicamente al coche.
 let margin=wallish?2.45:1.72;
 if(wc.exact&&Number.isFinite(wc.nearest)){const clearance=wc.nearest-e.half;margin=Math.max(margin,2.65-clamp(clearance,0,2.2)*.18)}
 return clamp(e.half-margin,1.10,4.15);
}
function autoRaceTrafficSnapshot(){
 const active=currentSession==='race'&&rivals&&rivals.length>0;
 const out={active,nearCount:0,packRisk:0,ahead:null,behind:null,left:null,right:null,side:null,launchProtect:false,leftClear:true,rightClear:true};
 if(!active||!player)return out;
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 for(const rv of rivals){
   if(rv.finished)continue;
   let d=rv.total-player.total;
   if(d>L*.5)d-=L;else if(d<-L*.5)d+=L;
   if(d<-42||d>70)continue;
   const latDelta=rv.lat-player.lat,latAbs=Math.abs(latDelta),rel=rv.speed-player.speed;
   if(Math.abs(d)<24)out.nearCount++;
   const item={rv,d,lat:rv.lat,latDelta,latAbs,speed:rv.speed,rel};
   if(d>=0&&(!out.ahead||d<out.ahead.d))out.ahead=item;
   if(d<0&&(!out.behind||-d<-out.behind.d))out.behind=item;
   if(Math.abs(d)<7.2&&latAbs<3.4&&(!out.side||latAbs<out.side.latAbs))out.side=item;
   if(latDelta<-.25&&Math.abs(d)<11&&(!out.left||Math.abs(d)<Math.abs(out.left.d)))out.left=item;
   if(latDelta>.25&&Math.abs(d)<11&&(!out.right||Math.abs(d)<Math.abs(out.right.d)))out.right=item;
   let risk=0;
   if(Math.abs(d)<5.4&&latAbs<2.25)risk=1;
   else if(d>0&&d<13&&latAbs<2.1)risk=Math.max(risk,clamp((13-d)/10,0,1)*.92);
   else if(d<0&&d>-12&&latAbs<2.0&&rel>2.0)risk=Math.max(risk,clamp((12+d)/10,0,1)*clamp(rel/16,0,1));
   out.packRisk=Math.max(out.packRisk,risk);
 }
 out.launchProtect=raceT<14.0&&(out.nearCount>0||raceT<7.5);
 // Amenaza trasera: no obliga a acelerar contra un coche delante, pero sí permite
 // abandonar una calle bloqueada durante la salida si existe un hueco lateral seguro.
 out.rearThreat=out.behind?clamp((8-(-out.behind.d))/6,0,1)*clamp((out.behind.rel-3.0)/12,0,1):0;
 out.leftClear=!out.left||Math.abs(out.left.d)>7.5||out.left.latAbs>2.45;
 out.rightClear=!out.right||Math.abs(out.right.d)>7.5||out.right.latAbs>2.45;
 return out;
}
function autoRaceLaneClear(candidate,trafficSnap){
 if(currentSession!=='race'||!rivals||!rivals.length)return true;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,from=player.lat,to=candidate,lo=Math.min(from,to)-1.35,hi=Math.max(from,to)+1.35;
 for(const rv of rivals){
   if(rv.finished)continue;
   let d=rv.total-player.total;if(d>L*.5)d-=L;else if(d<-L*.5)d+=L;
   if(d<-11||d>17)continue;
   const futureD=d+(rv.speed-player.speed)*.55;
   const nearLong=Math.abs(d)<7.0||Math.abs(futureD)<7.5;
   const destination=Math.abs(rv.lat-candidate)<1.75;
   const crosses=rv.lat>lo&&rv.lat<hi&&Math.abs(d)<6.2;
   if(nearLong&&(destination||crosses))return false;
 }
 return true;
}
function playerAutoTraffic(plan){
 const snap=autoRaceTrafficSnapshot();
 let ahead=null;
 for(const rv of rivals){
   if(rv.finished)continue;
   const d=rv.total-player.total;
   if(d>0&&d<105&&(!ahead||d<ahead.d))ahead={rv,d,lat:rv.lat,speed:rv.speed};
 }
 const env=envProfile(player.s),limit=autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s);
 let lane=null,blocked=false;
 const launchCorridorHold=currentSession==='race'&&raceT<13.5&&(raceT<6.8||snap.nearCount>1||snap.packRisk>.10||!!snap.side);
 player.autoLaunchCorridorHold=!!launchCorridorHold;
 if(launchCorridorHold){
   lane=clamp(Number.isFinite(player.gridLat)?player.gridLat:player.lat,-limit,limit);
   player.autoPassSide=0;player.autoPassUntil=0;
 }else if(ahead){
   const closing=player.speed-ahead.speed,cornerSoon=plan&&plan.d<105&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.00155;
   const wallish=apexWallishAtS(player.s,env),firstLapSurvival=currentSession==='race'&&player.lap<1;
   // En la primera vuelta no inicia un adelantamiento nuevo dentro de una curva próxima.
   // Puede mantener un paralelo ya existente, pero evita el cambio brusco de calle que
   // estaba provocando varias salidas antes de completar una sola vuelta.
   const passAllowed=firstLapSurvival?!cornerSoon:(!wallish||!cornerSoon||plan.d>145);
   if(passAllowed&&((ahead.d<38&&(closing>.35||ahead.d<16))||((player.autoPassUntil||0)>raceT&&ahead.d<62))){
     if((player.autoPassUntil||0)<=raceT||!player.autoPassSide){
       let preferred;
       if(cornerSoon)preferred=-Math.sign(plan.k||1);
       else if(Math.abs(ahead.lat)>.55)preferred=ahead.lat>0?-1:1;
       else preferred=stableTrait('PLAYER-AUTO',31+Math.floor(raceT/8))>.5?1:-1;
       const candA=clamp(ahead.lat+preferred*2.10,-limit,limit),candB=clamp(ahead.lat-preferred*2.10,-limit,limit);
       const clearA=autoRaceLaneClear(candA,snap),clearB=autoRaceLaneClear(candB,snap);
       if(clearA)player.autoPassSide=preferred;
       else if(clearB)player.autoPassSide=-preferred;
       else {player.autoPassSide=0;blocked=true;}
       player.autoPassUntil=player.autoPassSide?raceT+2.15:raceT+.45;
     }
     if(player.autoPassSide){
       const candidate=clamp(ahead.lat+player.autoPassSide*2.05,-limit,limit);
       if(autoRaceLaneClear(candidate,snap))lane=candidate;
       else {player.autoPassSide=0;player.autoPassUntil=raceT+.35;blocked=true;}
     }
   }else if(ahead.d>64||!passAllowed){player.autoPassSide=0;player.autoPassUntil=0;}
 }else{player.autoPassSide=0;player.autoPassUntil=0;}
 // En el pelotón y durante la salida no cruza de golpe de una calle a otra. Si tiene
 // un coche prácticamente al lado, conserva su calle hasta que haya solape suficiente.
 if(!launchCorridorHold&&snap.active&&snap.side&&Math.abs(snap.side.d)<6.0){
   if(Number.isFinite(lane)&&!autoRaceLaneClear(lane,snap)){lane=null;blocked=true;}
   if(!Number.isFinite(lane))lane=clamp(player.lat,-limit,limit);
 }
 const urgentAhead=!!(ahead&&ahead.d<9.0&&(player.speed-ahead.speed)>3.5);
 const rearThreat=(snap.rearThreat||0)>.42;
 const escapeAllowed=!launchCorridorHold&&Number.isFinite(lane)&&autoRaceLaneClear(lane,snap)&&(urgentAhead||rearThreat);
 if(snap.launchProtect&&raceT<10.5&&!launchCorridorHold&&Number.isFinite(lane)&&Math.abs(lane-player.lat)>1.0&&!escapeAllowed)lane=clamp(player.lat,-limit,limit);
 player.autoRaceTraffic={...snap,ahead:ahead||snap.ahead,blocked,lane,escapeAllowed};
 player.autoRacePackRisk=snap.packRisk||0;player.autoRaceStartProtect=!!snap.launchProtect;player.autoRearThreat=snap.rearThreat||0;player.autoTrafficEscapeLane=!!escapeAllowed;
 return{ahead,lane,limit,blocked,snap};
}
// v5.123: límite de curva derivado del MISMO modelo físico que mueve el coche.
// La fórmula anterior era deliberadamente defensiva y acababa tratando un F1 con
// carga aerodinámica como un turismo. Usamos una envolvente robusta de curvatura y
// resolvemos la velocidad máxima que puede sostener el agarre lateral disponible.
function autoCurvatureEnvelope(s){
 const a=[-6,-3,0,3,6].map(d=>Math.abs(curvatureAt(s+d))).sort((x,y)=>x-y);
 // percentil alto: ignora un único pico de geometría/GPX pero no una curva sostenida.
 return Math.max(a[3]||0,(a[2]||0)*1.04);
}
function autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(s){
 const k=autoCurvatureEnvelope(s);
 if(k<.00055)return 99;
 const wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),mu=wet?1.24:1.86,M=800,G=9.81;
 const safety=wet?.90:.960;
 let lo=12,hi=99;
 for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
   const v=(lo+hi)*.5;
   const down=Math.min(38000,2.72*v*v);
   const latAccel=mu*(M*G+down)/M*safety;
   // Incluye también la autoridad geométrica de dirección a esa velocidad.
   const maxSteer=.285/(1+v*.0105),needSteer=Math.atan(3.60*k);
   const safe=(v*v*k<=latAccel)&&(needSteer<=maxSteer*.96);
   if(safe)lo=v;else hi=v;
 }
 return clamp(lo,14,99);
}
function autoEnsureRiskMemory(){
 if(!player||!trackData)return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,bin=Math.max(14,Number(player.autoRiskBinSize)||20),n=Math.max(32,Math.ceil(L/bin));
