 if(!(player.autoRiskBins instanceof Float32Array)||player.autoRiskBins.length!==n)player.autoRiskBins=new Float32Array(n);
 player.autoRiskBinSize=bin;return player.autoRiskBins;
}
function autoRiskIndex(s){
 const bins=autoEnsureRiskMemory();if(!bins)return 0;
 const L=trackData.km*1000;return clamp(Math.floor(mod(s,L)/L*bins.length),0,bins.length-1);
}
function autoRiskAt(s){
 const bins=autoEnsureRiskMemory();return bins?bins[autoRiskIndex(s)]||0:0;
}
function autoRiskAhead(s,horizon=70,step=10){
 let risk=0,at=999;
 for(let d=0;d<=horizon;d+=step){const r=autoRiskAt(s+d)*(1-d/(horizon*1.55));if(r>risk){risk=r;at=d}}
 return{risk:clamp(risk,0,1),d:at};
}
function autoMarkLocalRisk(s,severity=1){
 const bins=autoEnsureRiskMemory();if(!bins)return;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,sev=clamp(severity,.18,1);
 // El aprendizaje se propaga sobre todo hacia ATRÁS para que en la siguiente vuelta
 // empiece a levantar/frenar antes de llegar al lugar del trompo o salida.
 for(let back=0;back<=135;back+=10){
   const w=sev*lerp(1,.30,back/135),i=autoRiskIndex(s-back);bins[i]=Math.max(bins[i],w);
 }
 for(let ahead=10;ahead<=30;ahead+=10){const i=autoRiskIndex(s+ahead);bins[i]=Math.max(bins[i],sev*.52);}
 player.autoRiskLastMark=raceT;
}
function autoCoolLocalRisk(s,strength=.024){
 const bins=autoEnsureRiskMemory();if(!bins)return;
 const i=autoRiskIndex(s),st=clamp(strength,0,.055);
 // En una pasada limpia enfriamos también los dos bins vecinos. Así una salida real
 // sigue dejando memoria, pero no convierte media vuelta en una zona lenta permanente.
 for(let o=-1;o<=1;o++){
   const j=mod(i+o,bins.length),w=o===0?1:.42,keep=1-st*w;
   bins[j]*=keep;if(bins[j]<.032)bins[j]=0;
 }
}
function autoLearnLocalRisk(stability,sp,env){
 if(!player||raceT-(player.autoRiskLastUpdate||-99)<.075)return;
 player.autoRiskLastUpdate=raceT;
 const ps=playerSurfaceState(),body=Math.abs(finite(player.slipAngle,0)),rear=Math.abs(finite(player.slipR,0)),dir=finite(player.trackDirDot,1);
 const recentRival=raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<2.8;
 const launchNoise=currentSession==='race'&&raceT<12&&ps.zone==='TRACK';
 const trafficNoise=currentSession==='race'&&(recentRival||(player.autoRacePackRisk||0)>.45)&&ps.zone==='TRACK';
 // v5.146: la memoria persistente aprende INCIDENTES, no simplemente una curva tomada
 // con mucha carga. La telemetría V13 acababa marcando como peligrosas zonas normales
 // por picos de slip/tyreUse y el riesgo crecía vuelta tras vuelta aun sin trompo.
 let sev=0;
 const trulyOff=ps.zone!=='TRACK'||Math.abs(player.lat)>env.half+Math.max(.12,env.curb*.10);
 if(trulyOff)sev=Math.max(sev,.80);
 if(dir<.48&&sp>6&&!trafficNoise)sev=Math.max(sev,.90);
 if(raceT-(player.autoLastWallHit||-99)<.30&&!recentRival)sev=1;
 if(sev>.48&&raceT-(player.autoRiskLastMark||-99)>.28&&!recentRival)autoMarkLocalRisk(player.s,sev);
 else if(ps.zone==='TRACK'&&!trafficNoise&&!launchNoise&&dir>.88&&body<.075&&rear<.105&&(!stability||stability.risk<.46))autoCoolLocalRisk(player.s,.028);
}


// ============================================================================
// v5.215 · PLAYER AUT LEGACY PROFILE v5.149
// Sólo el AUT del jugador usa estas funciones. Rivales, circuitos, física, render,
// HUD y el resto del proyecto continúan siendo los de la base v5.214.1.
// Se recupera la lógica real de conducción de v5.149 para comparar el circuito de referencia sin que
// la referencia continua/pure-pursuit v5.213+ intervenga en el resultado.
// ============================================================================
function playerDriverCornerPlan149(s,horizon=300,step=10){
 const threshold=.00135;
 let best={d:999,k:0,safe:98,score:0},first=null,last=null,prevSig=null,signChanges=0,samples=0;
 const groupGap=Math.max(44,step*4.5);
 for(let d=0;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const ss=s+d,k=curvatureAt(ss),safe=cornerSafeSpeed(ss),a=Math.abs(k);
   if(a<threshold&&safe>91)continue;
   const score=(98-safe)*1.42+a*1650-d*.017;
   const pt={d,k,safe,score};
   if(score>best.score)best=pt;
   if(!first){first=pt;last=pt;prevSig=pt;samples=1;continue;}
   if(d-last.d>groupGap)continue;
   if(prevSig&&Math.sign(k)!==Math.sign(prevSig.k)&&Math.abs(k)>.00165&&Math.abs(prevSig.k)>.00165)signChanges++;
   last=pt;prevSig=pt;samples++;
 }
 if(!first)return{...best,first:null,last:null,sequence:false,signChanges:0,samples:0};
 return{...best,first,last,sequence:signChanges>0&&last.d-first.d>24,signChanges,samples};
}
function playerRacingLineTarget149(s,plan,skill=.96,bravery=.90,bias=0){
 const env=envProfile(s),limit=clamp(env.half-2.05,1.65,3.15);
 const outer=limit*(.68+.12*bravery),apex=limit*(.62+.16*skill);
 const kNow=curvatureAt(s+7),kPast=curvatureAt(s-24);
 let line=bias;
 if(plan&&plan.d<145&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.00135){
   const sg=Math.sign(plan.k)||1;
   if(plan.d>86)line+=sg*outer;
   else if(plan.d>17){const t=clamp((86-plan.d)/69,0,1),e=t*t*(3-2*t);line+=lerp(sg*outer,-sg*apex,e);}
   else line+=-sg*apex;
   if(plan.sequence&&plan.last&&plan.last.d<150&&Math.sign(plan.last.k)!==sg){
     const nextSg=Math.sign(plan.last.k)||1,w=clamp((150-plan.last.d)/115,.10,.62),nextPrep=nextSg*outer*.92;
     line=lerp(line,nextPrep,w);
   }
 }else if(Math.abs(kPast)>.0019&&Math.abs(kNow)<Math.abs(kPast)*.70)line+=Math.sign(kPast)*outer*.88;
 return clamp(line,-limit,limit);
}
function playerTrackTargetSpeed149(s,opt={}){
 const perf=opt.perf||teamPerformance(player?.teamKey),skill=clamp(opt.skill??.96,.75,1),diff=Number.isFinite(opt.diff)?opt.diff:1;
 const wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),pace=Number.isFinite(opt.pace)?opt.pace:1,riskWeight=Number.isFinite(opt.riskWeight)?opt.riskWeight:.14;
 const baseDecel=(wet?9.8:14.0)*(.96+.08*skill)*perf.brake*(Number.isFinite(opt.decelScale)?opt.decelScale:1);
 let target=(wet?88:98)*perf.top;
 const horizon=Number.isFinite(opt.horizon)?opt.horizon:(wet?330:370),step=Math.max(6,Number.isFinite(opt.step)?opt.step:8),safeScale=Number.isFinite(opt.safeScale)?opt.safeScale:1;
 for(let d=step;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const ss=s+d,e=envProfile(ss),wallish=apexWallishAtS(ss,e),k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss));
   const diffTrim=clamp(1+(diff-1)*.32,.98,1.018),wallTrim=wallish?.972:1.000;
   const learned=typeof opt.riskFn==="function"?clamp(opt.riskFn(ss)||0,0,1):0;
   let safe=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(ss)*pace*diffTrim*perf.aero*wallTrim*safeScale*(1-learned*riskWeight);
   if(wet)safe*=.90*perf.wet;
   const turnShare=clamp((k-.0025)/.0105,0,1),planDecel=baseDecel*(1-turnShare*.18),settle=d>18?8:3;
   const allowed=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,safe*safe+2*planDecel*Math.max(0,d-settle)));
   if(allowed<target)target=allowed;
 }
 return target;
}
function playerAutoCompoundEntryProfile149(s,horizon=118,step=5){
 let first=null,peak=null,sign=0,oppositeBeforePeak=false,significant=0;
 for(let d=5;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const k=curvatureAt(s+d),a=Math.abs(k);if(a<.00145)continue;significant++;
   const sg=Math.sign(k)||sign||1;
   if(!first){first={d,k,a};sign=sg;peak={d,k,a};continue;}
   if(sg!==sign&&a>.00225){oppositeBeforePeak=true;break;}
   if(sg===sign&&a>(peak?peak.a:0))peak={d,k,a};
 }
 if(!first||!peak||significant<3)return{active:false,firstD:999,peakD:999,severity:0,peakK:0};
 const growth=peak.a/Math.max(.00145,first.a),span=peak.d-first.d;
 const active=!oppositeBeforePeak&&first.d<88&&peak.d<112&&span>=10&&peak.a>.00305&&growth>1.24;
 const severity=active?clamp((growth-1.20)/1.15+(peak.a-.0030)*62,0,1):0;
 return{active,firstD:first.d,peakD:peak.d,severity,peakK:peak.a,growth,sign};
}
function playerAutoTechnicalProfile149(s,horizon=112,step=5){
 let minSafe=98,maxK=0,firstTightD=999,lastSig=0,lastSigD=-999,signChanges=0,changeD=999,activeSamples=0;
 for(let d=4;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const k=curvatureAt(s+d),a=Math.abs(k),safe=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(s+d);
   minSafe=Math.min(minSafe,safe);maxK=Math.max(maxK,a);
   if((safe<36||a>.0090)&&firstTightD===999)firstTightD=d;
   if(a>.0028){const sig=Math.sign(k)||lastSig;activeSamples++;if(lastSig&&sig!==lastSig&&d-lastSigD<=52){signChanges++;changeD=Math.min(changeD,d);}lastSig=sig;lastSigD=d;}
 }
 const chicane=signChanges>0&&maxK>.0031&&changeD<78,hairpin=minSafe<29||maxK>.0115,veryTight=minSafe<33&&firstTightD<55;
 const active=chicane||hairpin||veryTight,severity=clamp(Math.max((36-minSafe)/20,maxK*70,chicane?.72:0,hairpin?.88:0),0,1);
 return{active,chicane,hairpin,minSafe,maxK,firstTightD,signChanges,changeD,severity,activeSamples};
}
function playerAutoTechnicalDriveNow149(s,profile,sp){
 if(!profile||!profile.active)return false;
 const kNow=Math.abs(curvatureAt(s+6)),safeNow=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(s+6),near=clamp(25+sp*.36,30,52);
 const swapNear=profile.chicane&&Number.isFinite(profile.changeD)&&profile.changeD<Math.min(34,near*.72);
 return profile.firstTightD<near||swapNear||kNow>.0047||safeNow<42;
}function playerAutoTechnicalLane149(s,profile,skill=.96){
 if(!profile||!profile.active)return null;
 const k0=curvatureAt(s+6),k1=curvatureAt(s+14),k2=curvatureAt(s+24),a=Math.abs(k0);
 if(a<.0020)return 0;
 if(profile.chicane){
   const sg0=Math.sign(k0)||1,sg1=Math.sign(k1)||sg0,sg2=Math.sign(k2)||sg1;
   const swapAhead=(Math.abs(k1)>.0022&&sg1!==sg0)||(Math.abs(k2)>.0025&&sg2!==sg0);
   if(swapAhead)return 0;
   const amp=.22+.15*skill;return clamp(-sg0*amp,-.52,.52);
 }
 const amp=.40+.20*skill;return clamp(-Math.sign(k0)*amp,-.68,.68);
}
function playerAutoLaneAt149(s,skill,bravery,trafficLane=null){
 const p=playerDriverCornerPlan149(s,220,9),e=envProfile(s),wallish=apexWallishAtS(s,e);
 let lat=playerRacingLineTarget149(s,p,skill,bravery,0);
 if(!wallish&&!Number.isFinite(trafficLane))lat*=1.18;
 else if(wallish&&!Number.isFinite(trafficLane))lat*=1.06;
 if(Number.isFinite(trafficLane))lat=trafficLane;
 return clamp(lat,-autoSafeLaneLimit(s),autoSafeLaneLimit(s));
}

function autoTargetSpeed(plan,traffic,skill,bravery,diff=1){
 const perf=teamPerformance(player.teamKey),wet=apexWeatherIsWet();
 const paceFloor=currentSession==='race'?.995:.955,paceCeil=currentSession==='race'?1.055:1.05;
 const pace=clamp(Number.isFinite(player.autoPace)?player.autoPace:(currentSession==='race'?1.00:.985),paceFloor,paceCeil);
 // v5.136: exactamente el mismo envelope geométrico/físico que usan los rivales.
 // El jugador añade su memoria de riesgo local porque su coche sí integra la física completa.
 return sharedDriverTrackTargetSpeed(player.s,{perf,skill,bravery,diff,pace,riskFn:autoRiskAt,riskWeight:.095,horizon:wet?330:370,step:8,safeScale:1,decelScale:1});
}

// v5.145: segundo cinturón de seguridad para AUT de carrera. El envelope físico puro
// funciona bien en pista libre, pero las telemetrías V12 muestran varias salidas sin
// contacto reciente en curvas de radio decreciente. Este gobernador mira la velocidad
// geométrica conservadora por delante y calcula una frenada alcanzable ANTES del giro.
function autoRaceSurvivalGovernor(s,sp,raceTraffic){
 if(currentSession!=='race'||!(demoMode||assistMode)||!player)return{active:false,target:Infinity,d:999,safe:99,firstLap:false};
 const firstLap=player.lap<1,recentContact=raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<2.4,postImpact=(player.autoContactGuardUntil||-99)>raceT;
 const pack=clamp((raceTraffic&&raceTraffic.packRisk)||0,0,1),wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),perf=teamPerformance(player.teamKey);
 const riskAhead=autoRiskAhead(s,92,10).risk,nearCount=(raceTraffic&&raceTraffic.nearCount)||0;
 const cleanRelease=!firstLap&&!recentContact&&!postImpact&&pack<.16&&nearCount<2&&riskAhead<.28;
 let horizon=firstLap?172:150,step=6;
 let scale=wet?(firstLap?1.11:1.16):(firstLap?1.23:1.30);
 // v5.147: en aire limpio y después de la primera vuelta dejamos de aplicar todo el
 // margen pensado para sobrevivir al pelotón. No se libera si hay memoria de riesgo
 // por delante, tráfico, lluvia o un contacto reciente.
 if(cleanRelease){horizon-=12;scale+=wet?.018:.035;}
 if(pack>.45)scale-=.025;if(recentContact)scale-=.035;if(postImpact)scale-=.035;
 scale=clamp(scale,wet?1.05:1.14,wet?1.22:1.36);
 const decel=(wet?9.0:12.95)*perf.brake*(firstLap?.97:(cleanRelease?1.035:1));
 let best=Infinity,bestD=999,bestSafe=99,bestK=0;
 for(let d=12;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const ss=s+d,k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss)),legacy=cornerSafeSpeed(ss);
   if(k<.00215&&legacy>61)continue;
   let curveTarget=legacy*scale+(firstLap?.35:1.1);
   if(k>.0090)curveTarget*=.975;
   const settle=d>22?10:4,allowed=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,curveTarget*curveTarget+2*decel*Math.max(0,d-settle)));
   if(allowed<best){best=allowed;bestD=d;bestSafe=curveTarget;bestK=k;}
 }
 const active=Number.isFinite(best)&&best<98&&bestD<999;
 return{active,target:best,d:bestD,safe:bestSafe,k:bestK,firstLap,pack,recentContact,postImpact,cleanRelease,riskAhead};
}

function autoCompoundEntryProfile(s,horizon=118,step=5){
 // Curvas de radio decreciente / entrada muy rápida: la curvatura aumenta de forma
 // sostenida en la MISMA dirección. El caso típico es una secuencia T1→T2 de radio decreciente: no es una
 // horquilla ni una chicane, por lo que no debe entrar en TÉCNICA, pero sí necesita
 // conservar algo de margen para el segundo vértice.
 let first=null,peak=null,sign=0,oppositeBeforePeak=false,significant=0;
 for(let d=5;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const k=curvatureAt(s+d),a=Math.abs(k);
   if(a<.00145)continue;
   significant++;
   const sg=Math.sign(k)||sign||1;
   if(!first){first={d,k,a};sign=sg;peak={d,k,a};continue;}
   if(sg!==sign&&a>.00225){oppositeBeforePeak=true;break;}
   if(sg===sign&&a>(peak?peak.a:0))peak={d,k,a};
 }
 if(!first||!peak||significant<3)return{active:false,firstD:999,peakD:999,severity:0,peakK:0};
 const growth=peak.a/Math.max(.00145,first.a),span=peak.d-first.d;
 const active=!oppositeBeforePeak&&first.d<88&&peak.d<112&&span>=10&&peak.a>.00305&&growth>1.24;
 const severity=active?clamp((growth-1.20)/1.15+(peak.a-.0030)*62,0,1):0;
 return{active,firstD:first.d,peakD:peak.d,severity,peakK:peak.a,growth,sign};
}
function autoTechnicalProfile(s,horizon=112,step=5){
 // v5.120: TÉCNICA se reserva a geometrías que realmente lo necesitan. En v5.119
 // el umbral minSafe<56 activaba este modo también en curvas rápidas/medias (p.ej.
 // podía alcanzar Degner), reducía demasiado el look-ahead y volvía brusco el volante.
 let minSafe=98,maxK=0,firstTightD=999,lastSig=0,lastSigD=-999,signChanges=0,changeD=999,activeSamples=0;
 for(let d=4;d<=horizon;d+=step){
   const k=curvatureAt(s+d),a=Math.abs(k),safe=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(s+d);
   minSafe=Math.min(minSafe,safe);maxK=Math.max(maxK,a);
   if((safe<36||a>.0090)&&firstTightD===999)firstTightD=d;
   if(a>.0028){
     const sig=Math.sign(k)||lastSig;activeSamples++;
     if(lastSig&&sig!==lastSig&&d-lastSigD<=52){signChanges++;changeD=Math.min(changeD,d);}
     lastSig=sig;lastSigD=d;
   }
 }
 const chicane=signChanges>0&&maxK>.0031&&changeD<78;
 const hairpin=minSafe<29||maxK>.0115;
 const veryTight=minSafe<33&&firstTightD<55;
 const active=chicane||hairpin||veryTight;
 const severity=clamp(Math.max((36-minSafe)/20,maxK*70,chicane?.72:0,hairpin?.88:0),0,1);
 return{active,chicane,hairpin,minSafe,maxK,firstTightD,signChanges,changeD,severity,activeSamples};
}
function autoTechnicalDriveNow(s,profile,sp){
 // v5.127: el perfil técnico sigue mirando lejos para FRENAR a tiempo, pero no debe
 // convertir 100+ m de aproximación en volante de chicane/horquilla. La telemetría
 // completa del circuito de referencia v5.125 mostró TÉCNICA activa durante gran parte de la vuelta.
 if(!profile||!profile.active)return false;
 const kNow=Math.abs(curvatureAt(s+6)),safeNow=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(s+6);
 const near=clamp(25+sp*.36,30,52);
 const swapNear=profile.chicane&&Number.isFinite(profile.changeD)&&profile.changeD<Math.min(34,near*.72);
 return profile.firstTightD<near||swapNear||kNow>.0047||safeNow<42;
}
function autoTechnicalLane(s,profile,skill=.96){
 // v5.147: una chicane no cambia de ápice como un interruptor. V14 alternaba
 // -0.476 / +0.476 m en unos pocos metros y, con el coche todavía cargado del giro
 // anterior, podía provocar una oscilación creciente. Cerca del cambio de signo
 // priorizamos el centro y usamos un ápice algo menor.
 if(!profile||!profile.active)return null;
 const k0=curvatureAt(s+6),k1=curvatureAt(s+14),k2=curvatureAt(s+24),a=Math.abs(k0);
 if(a<.0020)return 0;
 if(profile.chicane){
   const sg0=Math.sign(k0)||1,sg1=Math.sign(k1)||sg0,sg2=Math.sign(k2)||sg1;
   const swapAhead=(Math.abs(k1)>.0022&&sg1!==sg0)||(Math.abs(k2)>.0025&&sg2!==sg0);
   if(swapAhead)return 0;
   const amp=.22+.15*skill;
   return clamp(-sg0*amp,-.52,.52);
 }
 const amp=.40+.20*skill;
 return clamp(-Math.sign(k0)*amp,-.68,.68);
}
function autoLaneAt(s,skill,bravery,trafficLane=null){
 const p=driverCornerPlan(s,220,9),e=envProfile(s),wallish=apexWallishAtS(s,e);
 let lat=driverRacingLineTarget(s,p,skill,bravery,0);
 // Aprovecha más pista en curvas abiertas: aumenta el radio sin acercarse de forma
 // temeraria a un muro. Las zonas técnicas siguen usando autoTechnicalLane().
 if(!wallish&&!Number.isFinite(trafficLane))lat*=1.18;
 else if(wallish&&!Number.isFinite(trafficLane))lat*=1.06;
 if(Number.isFinite(trafficLane))lat=trafficLane;
 return clamp(lat,-autoSafeLaneLimit(s),autoSafeLaneLimit(s));
}
function autoRecoveryTrafficRisk(targetS,targetLat,eta=1){
 const L=trackData.km*1000,d=forwardDistanceS(player.s,targetS,L),targetTotal=player.total+d;
 let risk=0,blocking=false,nearestBehind=Infinity,nearestAhead=Infinity;
 for(const rv of rivals){
   if(rv.finished)continue;
   const future=rv.total+Math.max(0,rv.speed)*Math.max(.15,eta),gap=future-targetTotal,latGap=Math.abs(rv.lat-targetLat);
   if(latGap>2.75)continue;
   if(gap<0)nearestBehind=Math.min(nearestBehind,-gap);else nearestAhead=Math.min(nearestAhead,gap);
   let r=0;
   if(gap>-7&&gap<13)r=1.45-clamp(Math.abs(gap-2)/18,0,.40);
   else if(gap<=-7&&gap>-42)r=clamp((42+gap)/35,0,1)*1.08;
   else if(gap>=13&&gap<27)r=clamp((27-gap)/14,0,1)*.58;
   const closing=Math.max(0,rv.speed-Math.max(5,player.speed));
   if(gap<0&&closing>4)r+=clamp(closing/28,0,.42);
   risk=Math.max(risk,r);
 }
 blocking=risk>.86;
 return{risk,blocking,nearestBehind,nearestAhead};
}
function autoRecoveryGateClear(targetLat){
 // Comprueba únicamente la puerta lateral de vuelta a la cinta. No prueba una cuerda
 // hasta un punto lejano (eso volvería a penalizar horquillas y curvas cerradas).
 const q=poseAt(player.s+2.5),tx=q.x+q.nx*targetLat,tz=q.z+q.nz*targetLat,dx=tx-player.x,dz=tz-player.z;
 const dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(dist<1.0)return true;
 const h=Math.atan2(dx,dz),steps=Math.max(3,Math.min(8,Math.ceil(dist/1.25)));let hint=player.trackIndex;
 for(let i=2;i<=steps;i++){
   const t=i/steps,x=player.x+dx*t,z=player.z+dz*t;
   const st=nearestTrackState(x,z,hint,{radius:28,allowGlobal:false});hint=st.index;
   const c=wallContactAtPose(x,z,h,hint);if(c)return false;
 }
 return true;
}
function autoStraightRecoveryCorridor(tx,tz){
 // Comprueba la cuerda física real que pretende usar el coche. Se permite pisar
 // escapatoria/cesped por una emergencia, pero jamás atravesar una barrera o el
 // límite físico de contención del escenario.
 const dx=tx-player.x,dz=tz-player.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(dist<2)return{clear:true,dist,minMargin:99};
 const heading=Math.atan2(dx,dz),steps=Math.max(7,Math.min(70,Math.ceil(dist/1.15)));let hint=player.trackIndex,minMargin=99;
 for(let i=1;i<=steps;i++){
   const t=i/steps,x=player.x+dx*t,z=player.z+dz*t;
   const st=nearestTrackState(x,z,hint,{radius:28,allowGlobal:false});hint=st.index;
   const e=envProfile(st.s),wgL=wallGeometryAtS(st.s,1),wgR=wallGeometryAtS(st.s,-1);
   const margin=st.lat>=0?(wgL.edge-st.lat):(wgR.edge+st.lat);minMargin=Math.min(minMargin,margin);
   // Margen para el ancho del F1; no esperamos al contacto matemático de la carrocería.
   if(margin<1.18||wallContactAtPose(x,z,heading,hint))return{clear:false,dist,minMargin,blockedS:st.s};
 }
 return{clear:true,dist,minMargin};
}
function autoChicaneEscapeAheadPlan(sp,state,recentHit=false,stuck=false){
 // Sólo existe tras un error real. En conducción normal AUT debe completar la chicane.
 const technical=autoTechnicalProfile(player.s,118,4);
 if(!(technical.chicane||technical.signChanges>0||technical.active&&technical.maxK>.010))return null;
 if(!(state.outsideTrack||state.deepOff||state.badlyCrossed||recentHit||stuck))return null;
 // Nunca intentamos cortar hacia la salida de la chicane con el coche casi perpendicular.
 // Primero hay que recuperar orientación: el JSON v5.125 mostró ESCAPE_AHEAD con ~100° de error.
 const tr0=player.track||poseAt(player.s),trackH0=Math.atan2(tr0.tx,tr0.tz),headingAbs=Math.abs(angleDiff(trackH0,player.heading));
 if(headingAbs>1.12||state.align<.43)return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,side=Math.sign(player.lat)||player.autoLastWallSide||1;
 // Localizamos la primera zona razonablemente recta una vez terminada TODA la secuencia.
 let exitD=0,quiet=0,sawTight=false;
 for(let d=10;d<=125;d+=5){
   const k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+d)),safe=cornerSafeSpeed(player.s+d),p=autoTechnicalProfile(player.s+d,28,4);
   const tight=p.active||k>.0042||safe<61;
   if(tight){sawTight=true;quiet=0}else if(sawTight){quiet+=5;if(quiet>=15){exitD=d+5;break}}
 }
 if(!exitD)exitD=clamp((Number.isFinite(technical.changeD)&&technical.changeD<900?technical.changeD+38:62),38,105);
 const dsCandidates=[exitD,Math.min(118,exitD+10),Math.min(124,exitD+20)].filter((v,i,a)=>v>=34&&a.indexOf(v)===i);
 let best=null;
 for(const d of dsCandidates){
   const ss=mod(player.s+d,L),limit=autoSafeLaneLimit(ss),q=poseAt(ss);
   // Primero intentamos salir por el mismo lado y después acercarnos al centro. Así
   // la cuerda suele atravesar la escapatoria, no toda la anchura de la pista.
   const lanes=[side*Math.min(1.45,limit*.38),side*Math.min(.70,limit*.18),0];
   for(const lane0 of lanes){
     const lane=clamp(lane0,-limit,limit),tx=q.x+q.nx*lane,tz=q.z+q.nz*lane;
     const corridor=autoStraightRecoveryCorridor(tx,tz);if(!corridor.clear)continue;
     const pathHeading=Math.atan2(tx-player.x,tz-player.z),turn=Math.abs(angleDiff(pathHeading,player.heading)),traffic=autoRecoveryTrafficRisk(ss,lane,clamp(corridor.dist/Math.max(8,sp+7),.5,4));
     if(turn>1.08)continue;
     let score=corridor.dist*.18+turn*4.8+traffic.risk*22-Math.min(3,corridor.minMargin)*.8+d*.025;
     if(Math.sign(lane)!==side&&Math.abs(lane)>.15)score+=8;
     const c={kind:'ESCAPE_AHEAD',s:ss,d,lane,x:tx,z:tz,heading:Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz),pathHeading,eta:clamp(corridor.dist/Math.max(8,sp+6),.5,4),trafficRisk:traffic.risk,trafficBlocking:traffic.blocking,gateClear:true,reverseNeeded:false,score,created:raceT,corridorMargin:corridor.minMargin};
     if(!best||c.score<best.score)best=c;
   }
 }
 return best;
}
function autoRecoverySteerToPoint(plan,sp){
 const dx=plan.x-player.x,dz=plan.z-player.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz),desired=Math.atan2(dx,dz),err=angleDiff(desired,player.heading);
 const damping=(player.yawRate||0)*.095+(player.slipAngle||0)*.28;
 let steer=clamp(err*(sp<8?2.65:2.15)-damping,-1,1);
 // A alta velocidad no intentamos cerrar de golpe hacia el punto: la recuperación
 // tiene que parecer una escapatoria recta, no otro trompo.
 if(sp>18)steer=clamp(steer,-.72,.72);
 return{steer,err,dist,desired};
}

function autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,recentHit=false,stuck=false){
 const L=trackData.km*1000,side=Math.sign(player.lat)||player.autoLastWallSide||1;
 const outside=state.outsideTrack,deep=state.deepOff,aligned=state.align,technical=autoTechnicalProfile(player.s,74,5);
 // En chicanes/horquillas no intentamos reincorporarnos 40-60 m por delante: ese punto
 // puede estar ya al otro lado del segundo giro. Se recupera una puerta cada vez.
 const dsList=technical.active?(outside?[4,6,9,12,16,21,27]:[3,5,8,11,15,20]):(outside?[8,12,17,23,30,38,48,58]:[5,9,14,20,28,38]);
 let best=null;
 for(const d of dsList){
   const ss=mod(player.s+d,L),e=envProfile(ss),limit=autoSafeLaneLimit(ss);
   const edgeLane=side*Math.min(limit,Math.max(1.0,limit*(technical.active?.66:.82)));
   const currentLane=clamp(player.lat,-limit,limit);
   const lanes=technical.active?(outside?[edgeLane,side*limit*.30,0]:[currentLane*.55,0]):(outside?[edgeLane,side*limit*.58,side*limit*.30]:[currentLane,side*limit*.55,0]);
   const uniq=[];for(const ln of lanes){const v=clamp(ln,-limit,limit);if(!uniq.some(x=>Math.abs(x-v)<.18))uniq.push(v)}
   for(const lane of uniq){
     const q=poseAt(ss),tx=q.x+q.nx*lane,tz=q.z+q.nz*lane,dx=tx-player.x,dz=tz-player.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
     const pathH=Math.atan2(dx,dz),trackH=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz),turn=Math.abs(angleDiff(pathH,player.heading)),approach=Math.abs(angleDiff(trackH,pathH));
     const eta=clamp(dist/Math.max(7,Math.min(18,sp+7))+turn*.26,.45,4.0),traffic=autoRecoveryTrafficRisk(ss,lane,eta);
     const wallish=apexWallishAtS(ss,e),curve=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss));
     const laneMove=Math.abs(player.lat-lane),gateClear=autoRecoveryGateClear(lane);
     let score=dist*.36+turn*5.8+approach*8.2+laneMove*.58+curve*820+traffic.risk*25+Math.abs(d-23)*.075;
     if(wallish)score+=2.2+Math.abs(lane)/Math.max(limit,1)*1.3;
     if(!gateClear)score+=85;
     // Reincorporarse por el mismo lado es normalmente más rápido y evita atravesar
     // la línea de carrera. Sólo se acepta el centro si aporta una ventaja clara.
     if(outside&&Math.sign(lane)!==side&&Math.abs(lane)>.2)score+=18;
     if(outside&&Math.abs(lane)<.35)score+=3.4;
     const reverseNeeded=(stuck&&sp<3.0)||(recentHit&&stuck&&sp<4.0)||turn>2.05||aligned<-.34;
     const candidate={s:ss,d,lane,x:tx,z:tz,heading:trackH,pathHeading:pathH,eta,trafficRisk:traffic.risk,trafficBlocking:traffic.blocking,gateClear,reverseNeeded,score,created:raceT};
     if(!best||candidate.score<best.score)best=candidate;
   }
 }
 if(!best){
   const ss=mod(player.s+10,L),q=poseAt(ss),limit=autoSafeLaneLimit(ss),lane=clamp(side*limit*.72,-limit,limit);
   best={s:ss,d:10,lane,x:q.x+q.nx*lane,z:q.z+q.nz*lane,heading:Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz),pathHeading:Math.atan2(q.x-player.x,q.z-player.z),eta:1.2,trafficRisk:0,trafficBlocking:false,gateClear:true,reverseNeeded:recentHit||stuck,score:999,created:raceT};
 }
 return best;
}
function autoRecoverySteerToLane(targetLat,sp,mode){
 const technical=autoTechnicalProfile(player.s,70,5),look=technical.active?(mode==='REALIGN'?clamp(3.8+sp*.18,4,7):clamp(4.8+sp*.22,5,10)):(mode==='REALIGN'?clamp(5+sp*.32,5,10):clamp(7+sp*.43,8,19)),q=poseAt(player.s+look);
 const futureHeading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz),crossErr=player.lat-targetLat;
 const lateralHeading=Math.atan(clamp(crossErr/(2.8+sp*.20),-.72,.72));
 const desired=futureHeading+lateralHeading,err=angleDiff(desired,player.heading);
 let steer=clamp(err*2.65+crossErr*.085-(player.yawRate||0)*.11,-1,1);
 const e=envProfile(player.s),wallish=apexWallishAtS(player.s,e),guard=wallish?Math.max(.85,e.half-1.55):Math.max(1.25,e.half-.85);
 if(Math.abs(player.lat)>guard){const inward=Math.sign(player.lat)||1;steer=lerp(steer,inward*(wallish?.96:.84),.58)}
 return{steer,err,crossErr};
}
function autoRecoveryController(sp,env){
 const tr=player.track||nearestTrackState(player.x,player.z,player.trackIndex,{radius:28,allowGlobal:false});
 const ps=playerSurfaceState(),trackHeading=Math.atan2(tr.tx,tr.tz),headingErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),align=Math.cos(headingErr);
 const outsideTrack=Math.abs(player.lat)>env.half+Math.max(.15,env.curb*.25),deepOff=Math.abs(player.lat)>env.half+env.curb+Math.max(.4,env.runoff*.28);
 const recentHit=raceT-(player.autoLastWallHit||-99)<1.25,badlyCrossed=align<.20;
 const spinOnTrack=!outsideTrack&&align<.52&&sp<24&&(Math.abs(player.yawRate||0)>.32||Math.abs(player.slipAngle||0)>.10);
 const trouble=outsideTrack||recentHit||spinOnTrack||(badlyCrossed&&sp<12.5);

 // Detección de atasco por movimiento real, no sólo por velocímetro. Detecta coches
 // que reptan contra un muro a 2–4 m/s pero apenas avanzan.
 if(trouble){
   let p=player.autoRecoveryProbe;
   if(!p)player.autoRecoveryProbe={t:raceT,x:player.x,z:player.z};
   else if(raceT-p.t>.62){
     const moved=Math.hypot(player.x-p.x,player.z-p.z);
     if(moved<.72&&sp<5.2)player.autoStuckSince=player.autoStuckSince||raceT;
     else if(moved>1.25)player.autoStuckSince=0;
     player.autoRecoveryProbe={t:raceT,x:player.x,z:player.z};
   }
 }else{player.autoRecoveryProbe=null;player.autoStuckSince=0;}
 if(trouble&&sp<1.45)player.autoStuckSince=player.autoStuckSince||raceT;
 const stuck=!!(player.autoStuckSince&&raceT-player.autoStuckSince>.32);

 if(!player.autoRecovery&&trouble){
   const rivalCaused=currentSession==='race'&&raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<3.2;
   const launchCaused=currentSession==='race'&&raceT<12;
   if(!rivalCaused&&!launchCaused){
     autoMarkLocalRisk(player.s,recentHit?1:(outsideTrack?.86:.72));
     const incidentDrop=spinOnTrack?.009:deepOff?.008:outsideTrack?.0055:.004;
     player.autoPace=clamp((Number.isFinite(player.autoPace)?player.autoPace:1)-incidentDrop,currentSession==='race'?.985:.965,currentSession==='race'?1.055:1.05);
   }
   const state={outsideTrack,deepOff,align,badlyCrossed};
   const straightPlan=autoChicaneEscapeAheadPlan(sp,state,recentHit,stuck),plan=straightPlan||autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,recentHit,stuck);
   let mode=spinOnTrack?'SPIN_RECOVER':straightPlan?'ESCAPE_AHEAD':plan.trafficBlocking&&outsideTrack?'YIELD':plan.reverseNeeded?'ESCAPE':badlyCrossed?'REALIGN':'REJOIN';
   player.autoRecovery={mode,since:raceT,episodeStart:raceT,startX:player.x,startZ:player.z,startLat:player.lat,startS:player.s,stableSince:0,attempts:0,plan,lastProgressAt:raceT,lastProgressX:player.x,lastProgressZ:player.z,lastCorridorCheck:raceT,bestAbsLat:Math.abs(player.lat),lastLatImproveAt:raceT,bestSAdvance:0};
   player.autoPassSide=0;player.autoPassUntil=0;
 }
 const R=player.autoRecovery;if(!R)return null;
 let elapsed=raceT-R.since,moved=Math.hypot(player.x-R.startX,player.z-R.startZ);
 const episodeElapsed=raceT-(R.episodeStart||R.since);
 const state={outsideTrack,deepOff,align,badlyCrossed};
 if(!R.plan)R.plan=autoChicaneEscapeAheadPlan(sp,state,recentHit,stuck)||autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,recentHit,stuck);

 // Cuenta impactos dentro del episodio. Si el mismo coche empieza a golpear repetidamente
 // una barrera, dejamos de alternar estrategias y entramos en una maniobra bloqueada.
 if(recentHit&&(R.lastWallSeen||-99)<(player.autoLastWallHit||-99)-.001){R.wallHits=(R.wallHits||0)+1;R.lastWallSeen=player.autoLastWallHit||raceT;}
 const emergencyTrigger=(episodeElapsed>4.8&&(R.attempts||0)>=2)||(R.attempts||0)>=5||(R.wallHits||0)>=4||episodeElapsed>7.5;
 if(emergencyTrigger&&!R.emergency){
   R.emergency=true;R.emergencyStage=(recentHit&&env.wall)?'BACKOUT':'PULL_IN';R.emergencySince=raceT;R.since=raceT;R.emergencyPlan=null;R.emergencyReverse=null;R.emergencyBestLat=Math.abs(player.lat);R.emergencyLastImprove=raceT;
   // v5.133: el lado por el que se salió queda BLOQUEADO durante todo el rescate.
   // Antes se recalculaba con sign(lat) y podía cambiar al cruzar la pista, haciendo
   // que el controlador persiguiera el borde opuesto.
   R.emergencySide=Math.sign(player.lat)||player.autoLastWallSide||1;R.emergencyEdgeLane=null;R.alignStableSince=0;R.lastStageTelemetry=null;R.lateralGuardTrips=0;
   R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.startLat=player.lat;R.startS=player.s;R.attempts=Math.max(R.attempts||0,1);
   pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_EMERGENCY',{stage:R.emergencyStage,attempts:R.attempts,wallHits:R.wallHits||0});
 }
 if(R.emergency){
   const eSide=R.emergencySide||Math.sign(player.lat)||player.autoLastWallSide||1,eLimit=autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s);
   const contact=wallContactAtPose(player.x,player.z,player.heading,player.trackIndex);
   const absLat=Math.abs(player.lat),stageElapsed=raceT-(R.emergencySince||raceT);const alignedE=Math.cos(angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading));

   // Objetivo de rescate BLOQUEADO: primero vuelve al borde del mismo lado por el que
   // salió. No persigue puntos 10-60 m por delante mientras está enterrado en grava.
   if(!R.emergencyPlan){
     const q0=poseAt(player.s),edgeLane=clamp(eSide*Math.min(eLimit,Math.max(.75,env.half-1.15)),-eLimit,eLimit);
     R.emergencyPlan={s:player.s,lane:edgeLane,x:q0.x+q0.nx*edgeLane,z:q0.z+q0.nz*edgeLane,created:raceT};
     R.emergencyEdgeLane=edgeLane;R.emergencyBestLat=absLat;R.emergencyLastImprove=raceT;R.emergencyAssistAnnounced=false;
   }
   if(absLat<(R.emergencyBestLat??absLat)-.18){R.emergencyBestLat=absLat;R.emergencyLastImprove=raceT;}
   const noLatProgress=raceT-(R.emergencyLastImprove||raceT)>2.2;
   const hardPull=((episodeElapsed>6.5&&noLatProgress)||episodeElapsed>11.5)&&!contact;
   if(hardPull&&!R.emergencyAssistAnnounced){R.emergencyAssistAnnounced=true;pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_TRACTION_ASSIST',{stage:R.emergencyStage||'PULL_IN',absLat:telemetryRound(absLat,2)});}

   // v5.133: si ya habíamos ganado terreno hacia la pista y empezamos a perderlo de
   // nuevo, aborta inmediatamente la fase actual. Esto evita el patrón visto en la
   // telemetría: tocar TRACK con ~90° de error y atravesar la cinta hasta el runoff
   // contrario. Mientras está sobre/asomado al asfalto vuelve a ALIGN; fuera, PULL_IN.
   const lateralWorsening=absLat>(R.emergencyBestLat??absLat)+.55&&raceT-(R.emergencyLastImprove||raceT)>.45;
   if(lateralWorsening&&R.emergencyStage!=='BACKOUT'){
     const nextStage=(ps.zone==='TRACK'||absLat<env.half+env.curb+.65)?'ALIGN':'PULL_IN';
     if(R.emergencyStage!==nextStage){pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_LATERAL_GUARD',{from:R.emergencyStage,to:nextStage,absLat:telemetryRound(absLat,2),bestAbsLat:telemetryRound(R.emergencyBestLat,2)});}
     R.emergencyStage=nextStage;R.emergencySince=raceT;R.emergencyReverse=false;R.alignStableSince=0;R.lateralGuardTrips=(R.lateralGuardTrips||0)+1;
     R.emergencyBestLat=absLat;R.emergencyLastImprove=raceT;
     const qg=poseAt(player.s),edgeLane=R.emergencyEdgeLane??clamp(eSide*Math.min(eLimit,Math.max(.75,env.half-1.15)),-eLimit,eLimit);
     R.emergencyEdgeLane=edgeLane;R.emergencyPlan={s:player.s,lane:edgeLane,x:qg.x+qg.nx*edgeLane,z:qg.z+qg.nz*edgeLane,created:raceT};
   }

   // Sólo se hace BACKOUT si existe contacto físico con barrera. En grava libre, dar
   // marcha atrás por sistema podía alejarnos aún más de la pista durante minutos.
   if(contact&&R.emergencyStage!=='BACKOUT'&&stageElapsed>.12){R.emergencyStage='BACKOUT';R.emergencySince=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;}
   if(R.emergencyStage==='BACKOUT'){
     const nx=contact?contact.nx:(player.autoLastWallNx||0),nz=contact?contact.nz:(player.autoLastWallNz||0);
     const escapeH=Math.atan2(-nx,-nz),vehicleTarget=escapeH+Math.PI,err=angleDiff(vehicleTarget,player.heading);
     const steer=clamp(-err*1.55,-.92,.92),movedE=Math.hypot(player.x-R.startX,player.z-R.startZ);
     if((!contact&&movedE>1.0)||movedE>2.6||stageElapsed>1.55){R.emergencyStage='PULL_IN';R.emergencySince=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;}
     return{steer,throttle:.62,brake:0,target:6.5,boost:false,reverse:true,phase:'RECUP · LIBERA MURO',recoveryAssist:true,recoveryHeading:vehicleTarget,recoveryForcePull:false};
   }

   if(R.emergencyStage==='TURN_IN')R.emergencyStage='PULL_IN';
   if(R.emergencyStage==='PULL_IN'){
     const p=R.emergencyPlan,dx=p.x-player.x,dz=p.z-player.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz),travelH=Math.atan2(dx,dz);
     const fErr=angleDiff(travelH,player.heading),rTarget=travelH+Math.PI,rErr=angleDiff(rTarget,player.heading);
     // Elegimos una sola dirección y la bloqueamos durante la maniobra para evitar
     // avance/marcha atrás alternos. Preferimos avance salvo que requiera girar >100°.
     if(R.emergencyReverse==null)R.emergencyReverse=Math.abs(rErr)+.20<Math.abs(fErr)&&Math.abs(fErr)>1.75;
     const rev=!!R.emergencyReverse,vehicleTarget=rev?rTarget:travelH,err=angleDiff(vehicleTarget,player.heading);
     let steer=clamp((rev?-1:1)*err*1.72-(player.yawRate||0)*.16,-.94,.94);
     const aerr=Math.abs(err),target=aerr>1.20?5.5:aerr>.70?9.5:hardPull?14:12;
     let throttle=sp<target-1?clamp(.42+(target-sp)/18,.38,.82):.10,brake=sp>target+1.5?clamp((sp-target)/8,.10,.48):0;
     if(aerr>1.35&&sp>6.5){throttle=.10;brake=Math.max(brake,.18);}
     const nearRibbon=absLat<env.half+env.curb+.85||dist<2.6;
     if(nearRibbon){R.emergencyStage='ALIGN';R.emergencySince=raceT;R.emergencyReverse=false;R.alignStableSince=0;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.emergencyEdgeLane=R.emergencyPlan.lane;}
     return{steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:rev,phase:'RECUP · VUELVE A PISTA',recoveryAssist:true,recoveryHeading:vehicleTarget,recoveryForcePull:hardPull};
   }

   // ALIGN v5.133: tocar asfalto NO significa reincorporarse. Primero se queda
   // pegado al mismo borde, casi parado si está cruzado, y orienta el chasis. No existe
   // ya el timeout de 2.2 s que podía mandar un coche a 90° a REJOIN_LOCK.
   if(R.emergencyStage==='ALIGN'){
     const edgeLane=R.emergencyEdgeLane??R.emergencyPlan.lane,edgeErr=player.lat-edgeLane;
     const rawTrackErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),rawAbs=Math.abs(rawTrackErr);
     // Sólo añadimos corrección de carril cuando la orientación ya es razonable; con
     // >50° el único objetivo es apuntar paralelo a la pista sin cruzarla.
     const laneHeading=rawAbs<.88?clamp(edgeErr*.085,-.18,.18):0;
     const desiredHeading=trackHeading+laneHeading,err=angleDiff(desiredHeading,player.heading),aerr=Math.abs(err);
     let steer=clamp(err*2.05-(player.yawRate||0)*.28,-.88,.88);
     let target=aerr>.78?1.6:aerr>.48?2.6:aerr>.28?4.0:5.2;
     let throttle=sp<target-.45?clamp(.20+(target-sp)/14,.16,.52):0,brake=sp>target+.45?clamp((sp-target)/5.5,.16,.68):0;
     if(aerr>.78&&sp>2.2){throttle=0;brake=Math.max(brake,.34);}
     if(Math.abs(edgeErr)>.80&&rawAbs<.70)steer=lerp(steer,clamp(edgeErr*.22,-.55,.55),.28);
     // Debe estar realmente alineado (<15°), junto al borde bloqueado y estable durante
     // 0.45 s. Mientras no se cumpla, permanece en ALIGN indefinidamente.
     const alignedGate=rawAbs<.26&&Math.abs(edgeErr)<.82&&sp<6.2;
     if(alignedGate){R.alignStableSince=R.alignStableSince||raceT;}else R.alignStableSince=0;
     if(R.alignStableSince&&raceT-R.alignStableSince>.45){
       R.emergencyStage='REJOIN_LOCK';R.emergencySince=raceT;R.alignStableSince=0;
       const q1=poseAt(player.s+10),ln=clamp(eSide*Math.min(eLimit*.68,Math.max(.65,env.half-1.65)),-eLimit,eLimit);
       R.emergencyPlan={s:mod(player.s+10,trackData.km*1000),lane:ln,x:q1.x+q1.nx*ln,z:q1.z+q1.nz*ln,created:raceT};
       pushTelemetryEvent('RECOVERY_ALIGN_LOCKED',{headingErrDeg:telemetryRound(rawTrackErr*180/Math.PI,1),edgeErrM:telemetryRound(edgeErr,2),speedKph:telemetryRound(sp*3.6,1)});
     }
     return{steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'RECUP · ALINEA EN BORDE',recoveryAssist:true,recoveryHeading:trackHeading,recoveryForcePull:false};
   }

   // REJOIN_LOCK v5.133: sólo puede existir tras la compuerta de alineación. Si por
   // cualquier perturbación vuelve a cruzarse >28° o empieza a escapar lateralmente,
   // regresa inmediatamente a ALIGN antes de seguir avanzando.
   const ctl=autoRecoverySteerToLane(R.emergencyPlan.lane,sp,'REJOIN'),trackErrNow=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),aerr=Math.abs(trackErrNow);
   const edgeLaneNow=R.emergencyEdgeLane??R.emergencyPlan.lane,edgeEscape=Math.abs(player.lat-edgeLaneNow);
   if(aerr>.49||(ps.zone==='TRACK'&&edgeEscape>1.65)){
     R.emergencyStage='ALIGN';R.emergencySince=raceT;R.alignStableSince=0;R.emergencyReverse=false;
     const qa=poseAt(player.s),ln=edgeLaneNow;R.emergencyPlan={s:player.s,lane:ln,x:qa.x+qa.nx*ln,z:qa.z+qa.nz*ln,created:raceT};
     return{steer:clamp(trackErrNow*2.05-(player.yawRate||0)*.28,-.88,.88),throttle:0,brake:sp>2?.34:.08,target:1.8,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'RECUP · RECUPERA ALINEACIÓN',recoveryAssist:true,recoveryHeading:trackHeading,recoveryForcePull:false};
   }
   let target=aerr>.32?6:aerr>.20?10:16,throttle=sp<target-1?clamp(.38+(target-sp)/22,.34,.76):.08,brake=sp>target+1.5?clamp((sp-target)/8,.10,.52):0;
   const inside=ps.zone==='TRACK'&&absLat<Math.max(1.0,env.half-.70),alignedNow=Math.abs(trackErrNow)<.21;
   if(inside&&alignedNow){player.autoRecovery=null;player.autoStuckSince=0;player.autoRecoveryProbe=null;player.autoPlan=null;player.autoPlanStamp=-99;return{steer:ctl.steer,throttle:Math.max(throttle,.52),brake:0,target:22,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'RECUP · LISTO',recoveryAssist:false,recoveryForcePull:false};}
   return{steer:ctl.steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'RECUP · REINCORPORA',recoveryAssist:true,recoveryHeading:trackHeading,recoveryForcePull:hardPull&&ps.zone!=='TRACK'};
 }
 // Si golpea de nuevo o no hace progreso, se replantea la maniobra completa en vez
 // de insistir indefinidamente contra el mismo obstáculo.
 const sinceProgress=raceT-(R.lastProgressAt||raceT),progressMove=Math.hypot(player.x-(R.lastProgressX||player.x),player.z-(R.lastProgressZ||player.z));
 if(progressMove>1.0){R.lastProgressAt=raceT;R.lastProgressX=player.x;R.lastProgressZ=player.z;}
 const absLatNow=Math.abs(player.lat),sAdvance=forwardDistanceS(R.startS,player.s,trackData.km*1000);
 if(absLatNow<(R.bestAbsLat??absLatNow)-.12){R.bestAbsLat=absLatNow;R.lastLatImproveAt=raceT;}
 R.bestSAdvance=Math.max(R.bestSAdvance||0,sAdvance);
 // Si el plan nos está alejando lateralmente de la pista y tampoco avanzamos por ella,
 // se considera una estrategia divergente y queda prohibido ESCAPE_AHEAD en el reintento.
 const diverging=outsideTrack&&absLatNow>(R.bestAbsLat??absLatNow)+.42&&raceT-(R.lastLatImproveAt||raceT)>.42&&(R.bestSAdvance||0)<3.2;
 const stalled=(sinceProgress>.82&&sp<4.2&&R.mode!=='YIELD'&&R.mode!=='ESCAPE');
 // El registro de 3 vueltas mostró recuperaciones de 90–130 s. A partir de 6 s sin
 // resolver, deja de perseguir salidas lejanas y fuerza una maniobra local al borde
 // más próximo; a los 10 s nunca se vuelve a ESCAPE_AHEAD en ese episodio.
 const recoveryLong=episodeElapsed>5.0,escapeAheadForbidden=episodeElapsed>8.5||diverging;
 if(recoveryLong&&R.mode==='ESCAPE_AHEAD'){R.mode='REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.plan=autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,true,stuck);R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.startLat=player.lat;R.startS=player.s;elapsed=0;}
 if(diverging||(R.mode!=='ESCAPE'&&recentHit&&stuck&&sp<3.6&&elapsed>.12)||stalled){
   R.attempts++;
   const allowFast=!escapeAheadForbidden&&!deepOff&&align>.48&&!badlyCrossed;
   const fast=allowFast?autoChicaneEscapeAheadPlan(sp,state,true,true):null;
   R.plan=fast||autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,true,true);R.mode=fast?'ESCAPE_AHEAD':R.plan.reverseNeeded?'ESCAPE':'REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.startLat=player.lat;R.startS=player.s;R.stableSince=0;R.lastProgressAt=raceT;R.lastProgressX=player.x;R.lastProgressZ=player.z;R.lastCorridorCheck=raceT;R.bestAbsLat=Math.abs(player.lat);R.lastLatImproveAt=raceT;R.bestSAdvance=0;elapsed=0;moved=0;
 }
 const plan=R.plan,targetLat=clamp(plan.lane,-autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s),autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s));

 // Comprueba el hueco mientras la maniobra está en curso. Si un rival va a ocupar
 // nuestra calle de entrada, espera fuera antes de cruzar la línea de carrera.
 const remaining=forwardDistanceS(player.s,plan.s,trackData.km*1000),etaNow=clamp(remaining/Math.max(7,sp+5),.25,3.2),trafficNow=autoRecoveryTrafficRisk(plan.s,plan.lane,etaNow);
 if(R.mode!=='ESCAPE'&&outsideTrack&&trafficNow.risk>1.00&&sp<8.5){R.mode='YIELD';R.since=raceT;R.stableSince=0;elapsed=0;}

 if(R.mode==='SPIN_RECOVER'){
   // Trompo dentro o junto al asfalto: no inicia una reincorporación larga. Primero
   // recupera orientación y sale acelerando en cuanto el coche vuelve a apuntar a pista.
   const err=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),absErr=Math.abs(err),alignedNow=Math.cos(err);
   let steer=clamp(err*1.55-(player.yawRate||0)*.48,-1,1);
   let target=absErr>1.55?5.5:absErr>1.05?9:absErr>.62?15:28;
   let throttle=sp<target-1?clamp(.32+(target-sp)/24,.26,.74):.06,brake=sp>target+2?clamp((sp-target)/12,.08,.46):0;
   if(absErr>1.25&&sp>8){throttle=0;brake=Math.max(brake,.22)}
   if(alignedNow>.82&&absErr<.62){R.mode=outsideTrack?'REJOIN':'REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.stableSince=0;}
   if(!outsideTrack&&alignedNow>.92&&absErr<.40){player.autoRecovery=null;player.autoStuckSince=0;player.autoRecoveryProbe=null;player.autoPlan=null;return {steer,throttle:Math.max(throttle,.45),brake:0,target:34,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'SALVA TROMPO'};}
   // Si casi se ha parado muy cruzado, permitimos una maniobra corta de liberación,
   // pero no saltamos inmediatamente a ESCAPE_AHEAD.
   if(elapsed>1.25&&sp<2.6&&absErr>1.18){R.plan=autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,false,true);R.mode=R.plan.reverseNeeded?'ESCAPE':'REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.bestAbsLat=Math.abs(player.lat);R.lastLatImproveAt=raceT;}
   return {steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'SALVA TROMPO'};
 }

 if(R.mode==='ESCAPE_AHEAD'){
   // Tras un error en una chicane abierta, no intentamos rehacerla: apuntamos a la
   // salida de toda la secuencia. Si aparece una barrera en la cuerda, se cancela
   // inmediatamente y volvemos a la maniobra convencional con marcha atrás si hace falta.
   const p=R.plan,ctl=autoRecoverySteerToPoint(p,sp),remaining=ctl.dist;
   if(raceT-(R.lastCorridorCheck||0)>.34){
     R.lastCorridorCheck=raceT;
     if(!autoStraightRecoveryCorridor(p.x,p.z).clear){R.plan=autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,true,stuck);R.mode=R.plan.reverseNeeded?'ESCAPE':'REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;return {steer:0,throttle:0,brake:.38,target:0,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'BUSCA SALIDA'};}
   }
   const traffic=autoRecoveryTrafficRisk(p.s,p.lane,clamp(remaining/Math.max(8,sp+6),.35,3.5));
   if(traffic.risk>1.05&&outsideTrack&&sp<7.5){R.mode='YIELD';R.since=raceT;return {steer:ctl.steer,throttle:0,brake:.65,target:0,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'ESPERA HUECO'};}
   const turn=Math.abs(ctl.err),psNow=playerSurfaceState();
   let target=psNow.kind==='GRAVEL'||psNow.kind==='SAND'?12:psNow.kind==='GRASS'?18:turn>.70?14:turn>.38?21:31;
   if(remaining<13)target=Math.min(target,21);
   let throttle=sp<target-1?clamp(.46+(target-sp)/24,.40,.88):.08,brake=sp>target+1?clamp((sp-target)/9,.10,.74):0;
   if(turn>.82){throttle*=.38;brake=Math.max(brake,.18)}
   // Alcanzada la escapatoria de salida: a partir de aquí ya vuelve a orientar el
   // coche con la pista, pero sin regresar a las puertas anteriores de la chicane.
   const passed=remaining<4.8||forwardDistanceS(p.s,player.s,trackData.km*1000)<12;
   if(passed){R.mode='REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.startLat=player.lat;R.startS=player.s;R.plan={...p,lane:clamp(p.lane,-autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s),autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s))};return {steer:ctl.steer,throttle:.18,brake:sp>11?.35:0,target:11,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'SALIDA CHICANE'};}
   return{steer:ctl.steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'RECTO ESCAPATORIA'};
 }

 if(R.mode==='YIELD'){
   const ctl=autoRecoverySteerToLane(targetLat,sp,'REALIGN');
   const safe=trafficNow.risk<.62;
   if(safe&&elapsed>.16){R.plan=autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,false,false);R.mode=R.plan.reverseNeeded?'ESCAPE':badlyCrossed?'REALIGN':'REJOIN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.startLat=player.lat;R.startS=player.s;return {steer:ctl.steer,throttle:0,brake:.45,target:0,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'HUECO'};}
   if(elapsed>2.4){R.plan=autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,false,false);R.since=raceT;}
   return {steer:Math.abs(headingErr)<.25?0:ctl.steer,throttle:0,brake:clamp(.55+sp/18,.55,1),target:0,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'ESPERA HUECO'};
 }

 if(R.mode==='ESCAPE'){
   // Marcha atrás corta para crear espacio y ángulo. En reversa el signo del volante
   // respecto al yaw se invierte; por eso el error angular entra con signo contrario.
   const side=Math.sign(player.lat)||player.autoLastWallSide||1,desired=plan.heading;
   const revErr=angleDiff(desired,player.heading);
   let steer=clamp(-revErr*1.45+side*.42,-.94,.94);
   if(align<-.28)steer=clamp(-revErr*1.85+side*.28,-.96,.96);
   const lateralGain=Math.max(0,Math.abs(R.startLat)-Math.abs(player.lat));
   const enough=moved>3.1||lateralGain>.58||elapsed>.88||(Math.cos(angleDiff(plan.heading,player.heading))>.62&&moved>1.6);
   if(enough){R.mode='REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.stableSince=0;return {steer:0,throttle:0,brake:.42,target:0,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'REALINEA'};}
   return {steer,throttle:deepOff?.76:.64,brake:0,target:7.5,boost:false,reverse:true,phase:'MARCHA ATRÁS'};
 }

 const ctl=autoRecoverySteerToLane(targetLat,sp,R.mode);
 if(R.mode==='REALIGN'){
   // v5.133: REALIGN ya no puede expirar por tiempo. El bug de la v5.132 saltaba a
   // REJOIN tras 0.72 s aunque el coche siguiera casi perpendicular a la pista.
   const hErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),aErr=Math.abs(hErr),aligned=Math.cos(hErr);
   const target=aErr>1.05?2.0:aErr>.78?3.0:aErr>.52?5.0:aErr>.36?7.5:10.5;
   let throttle=sp<target-.6?clamp(.22+(target-sp)/18,.18,.58):0,brake=sp>target+.6?clamp((sp-target)/6,.14,.68):0;
   if(aErr>.78&&sp>2.4){throttle=0;brake=Math.max(brake,.32);}
   const steer=clamp(hErr*1.95-(player.yawRate||0)*.26,-.92,.92);
   if(aErr<.42&&aligned>.91){R.mode='REJOIN';R.since=raceT;R.stableSince=0;}
   return {steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'REALINEA'};
 }

 // REJOIN: entra por la calle elegida, normalmente la más próxima al lado por el que
 // salió. Si vuelve a cruzarse, regresa a REALIGN antes de poder atravesar la pista.
 const rejoinHeadingErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),rejoinAbsErr=Math.abs(rejoinHeadingErr);
 if(rejoinAbsErr>.70){
   R.mode='REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.stableSince=0;
   return {steer:clamp(rejoinHeadingErr*1.95-(player.yawRate||0)*.26,-.92,.92),throttle:0,brake:sp>2.5?.34:.08,target:2.2,boost:false,reverse:false,phase:'REALINEA'};
 }
 const inside=ps.zone==='TRACK'&&Math.abs(player.lat)<Math.max(1.0,env.half-.62),aligned=Math.cos(rejoinHeadingErr)>.96;
 const surfNow=playerSurfaceState(),surfCap=(surfNow.kind==='GRAVEL'||surfNow.kind==='SAND')?11:surfNow.kind==='GRASS'?16:28;
 let target=deepOff?surfCap:outsideTrack?Math.max(16,surfCap):inside?34:24;
 if(trafficNow.risk>.55)target=Math.min(target,18);
 if(rejoinAbsErr>.48)target=Math.min(target,8);else if(rejoinAbsErr>.32)target=Math.min(target,13);
 let throttle=sp<target-1?clamp(.42+(target-sp)/28,.34,.82):sp<target?.20:0,brake=sp>target+1.3?clamp((sp-target)/9,.10,.68):0;
 if(rejoinAbsErr>.55){throttle*=.28;brake=Math.max(brake,.20)}
 if(inside&&aligned&&trafficNow.risk<.92){if(!R.stableSince)R.stableSince=raceT;}else R.stableSince=0;
 if(R.stableSince&&raceT-R.stableSince>.22){
   player.autoRecovery=null;player.autoStuckSince=0;player.autoRecoveryProbe=null;player.autoPlan=null;player.autoPlanStamp=-99;player.autoTargetLat=player.lat;
   return null;
 }
 if(elapsed>1.65&&sp<5.0){R.attempts++;const fast=(episodeElapsed<8.0&&!deepOff&&align>.50&&!badlyCrossed)?autoChicaneEscapeAheadPlan(sp,state,true,true):null;R.plan=fast||autoChooseRecoveryPlan(sp,env,state,true,true);R.mode=fast?'ESCAPE_AHEAD':R.plan.reverseNeeded?'ESCAPE':'REALIGN';R.since=raceT;R.startX=player.x;R.startZ=player.z;R.startLat=player.lat;R.startS=player.s;R.lastCorridorCheck=raceT;R.bestAbsLat=Math.abs(player.lat);R.lastLatImproveAt=raceT;R.bestSAdvance=0;}
 return {steer:ctl.steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,targetLat,phase:'REINCORPORA'};
}

function autoDynamicStability(sp,env,steer,target){
 // v5.116: supervisor predictivo. La prioridad es detectar una salida ANTES de que
 // llegue el coche al piano, no cuando ya estamos intentando salvarla.
 const tr=player.track||poseAt(player.s),wallish=apexWallishAtS(player.s,env);
 const latVel=(player.vx||0)*tr.nx+(player.vz||0)*tr.nz;
 const side=Math.sign(player.lat)||1,outward=side*latVel;
 const guard=wallish?Math.max(1.00,env.half-2.30):Math.max(1.35,env.half-1.55);
 const predictT=wallish?.95:.78,predicted=player.lat+latVel*predictT;
 const edgeNow=clamp((Math.abs(player.lat)-guard)/(wallish?1.25:1.65),0,1);
 const edgeFuture=clamp((Math.abs(predicted)-guard)/(wallish?1.15:1.55),0,1);
 const edgeRisk=Math.max(edgeNow,edgeFuture,clamp((outward-.05)/(wallish?4.2:5.6),0,1));

 const tyreUse=Math.max(finite(player.frontUse,0),finite(player.rearUse,0));
 const tyreRisk=clamp((tyreUse-.87)/.13,0,1);
 const rearSlip=Math.abs(finite(player.slipR,0)),frontSlip=Math.abs(finite(player.slipF,0)),bodySlip=Math.abs(finite(player.slipAngle,0));
 const slipRisk=Math.max(clamp((rearSlip-.062)/.115,0,1),clamp((frontSlip-.082)/.155,0,1)*.72,clamp((bodySlip-.040)/.115,0,1));
 const k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+clamp(8+sp*.11,9,20))),expectedYaw=sp*k;
 const yawExcess=Math.max(0,Math.abs(finite(player.yawRate,0))-expectedYaw*1.30-.055);
 const yawRisk=clamp(yawExcess/.52,0,1);
 const rearCritical=Math.max(clamp((finite(player.rearUse,0)-.93)/.07,0,1),clamp((rearSlip-.105)/.16,0,1));
 const risk=Math.max(edgeRisk,tyreRisk*.90,slipRisk,yawRisk,rearCritical*.94);

 let outSteer=steer;
 if(risk>.015){
   const yaw=finite(player.yawRate,0),slip=finite(player.slipAngle,0);
   outSteer=clamp(outSteer-(yaw*.20+slip*.96)*risk,-1,1);
   // Cuando el eje trasero ya está saturado, no seguimos pidiendo más cambio de yaw.
   // La prioridad es descargar el coche y conservar la dirección de giro actual.
   if(rearCritical>.08){
     const hold=Number.isFinite(player.autoSteerCmd)?player.autoSteerCmd:outSteer;
     outSteer=lerp(outSteer,hold,clamp(.18+rearCritical*.52,0,.68));
   }
   if(edgeRisk>.08){
     const inward=Math.sign(player.lat)||1;
     outSteer=lerp(outSteer,inward*(wallish?.98:.88),clamp(.20+edgeRisk*.65,0,.86));
   }
 }
 const speedCut=1-risk*(wallish?.115:.058);
 const stableTarget=Math.min(target,target*speedCut);
 const throttleScale=1-clamp(risk*.56+rearCritical*.18,0,.82);
 // Frenar más cuando el eje trasero ya está girando puede terminar el trompo. El freno
 // correctivo queda reservado al riesgo de borde con el coche todavía direccional.
 const directional=rearCritical<.24&&slipRisk<.48&&yawRisk<.48;
 const brakeFloor=(directional&&edgeRisk>.58&&sp>22)?clamp((edgeRisk-.58)*.72,0,.28):0;
 const brakeRelease=clamp(Math.max(rearCritical*.78,yawRisk*.55,slipRisk*.42)-.12,0,.78);
 return{steer:outSteer,target:stableTarget,risk,edgeRisk,tyreRisk,slipRisk,yawRisk,rearCritical,throttleScale,brakeFloor,brakeRelease};
}

function updateAutoAdaptivePace(stability,sp,env){
 if(!player)return;
 const race=currentSession==='race',paceFloor=race?.995:.955,paceCeil=race?1.055:1.05;
 let pace=clamp(Number.isFinite(player.autoPace)?player.autoPace:(race?1.00:.985),paceFloor,paceCeil);
 const risk=stability?stability.risk:0,latFrac=Math.abs(player.lat)/Math.max(.5,autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s));
 const clean=risk<.26&&latFrac<.90&&Math.abs(player.slipAngle||0)<.065&&Math.abs(player.slipR||0)<.095;
 if(clean&&sp>18){
   player.autoStableTime=(player.autoStableTime||0)+1/60;player.autoRiskTime=Math.max(0,(player.autoRiskTime||0)-1/28);
   if(player.autoStableTime>.85)pace+=race?.00022:.00012;
   if(race&&pace<1.008&&player.autoStableTime>3.0)pace+=.00010;
 }else{
   player.autoStableTime=Math.max(0,(player.autoStableTime||0)-.040);player.autoRiskTime=(player.autoRiskTime||0)+1/60;
   if(risk>.60)pace-=.00016*(1+.75*risk);if(risk>.86)pace-=.00030;
 }
 if(Math.abs(player.lat)>env.half||playerSurfaceState().zone!=="TRACK")pace-=race?.0015:.0022;
 player.autoPace=clamp(pace,paceFloor,paceCeil);player.autoLastRisk=risk;
}

// v5.230.13 · BOX AUT. Entrada por la puerta exacta del pitline y soporte de subpaths que cruzan s=0.
// La geometría del pit sigue siendo la red PIT real (SVG o fallback); el controlador no
// inventa una segunda trayectoria ni mueve el coche de forma artificial.
function apexPitSpeedLimitKph(){
 const pn=trackData&&trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;
 if(pn&&Number.isFinite(+pn.speedKph)&&+pn.speedKph>0)return clamp(+pn.speedKph,30,120);
 const pit=pitLaneSpec();if(pit&&Number.isFinite(+pit.speedKph)&&+pit.speedKph>0)return clamp(+pit.speedKph,30,120);
 return 80;
}
function apexPitPointAtS(pitS){
 const pn=trackData&&trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;if(!pn||!Array.isArray(pn.runs)||!pn.runs.length)return null;
 const total=Math.max(0,+pn.totalLength||0),ss=clamp(+pitS||0,0,total);
 let run=pn.runs[pn.runs.length-1];
 for(const r of pn.runs){if(ss<=r.startS+r.length+.001){run=r;break}}
 const pts=run&&run.points;if(!Array.isArray(pts)||pts.length<2)return null;
 let lo=0,hi=pts.length-1;
 while(lo+1<hi){const m=(lo+hi)>>1;if(pts[m].pitS<=ss)lo=m;else hi=m}
 const a=pts[lo],b=pts[Math.min(lo+1,pts.length-1)],den=(b.pitS-a.pitS)||1,t=clamp((ss-a.pitS)/den,0,1),dx=b.x-a.x,dz=b.z-a.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;
 const aw=Number.isFinite(+a.width)?+a.width:(run.width||5.9),bw=Number.isFinite(+b.width)?+b.width:(run.width||5.9),al=Number.isFinite(+a.leftWidth)?+a.leftWidth:aw*.5,bl=Number.isFinite(+b.leftWidth)?+b.leftWidth:bw*.5,ar=Number.isFinite(+a.rightWidth)?+a.rightWidth:aw*.5,br=Number.isFinite(+b.rightWidth)?+b.rightWidth:bw*.5;
 return{x:lerp(a.x,b.x,t),z:lerp(a.z,b.z,t),y:lerp(a.y,b.y,t),tx:dx/len,tz:dz/len,nx:-dz/len,nz:dx/len,pitS:ss,width:lerp(aw,bw,t),leftWidth:lerp(al,bl,t),rightWidth:lerp(ar,br,t),runId:run.id};
}
function apexPitRoutePlan(){
 if(!trackData)return null;const pn=trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;if(!pn||!Array.isArray(pn.runs)||!pn.runs.length)return null;
 // Sólo el componente principal encadenado forma la ruta de parada. Así un segundo
 // path auxiliar o un template no puede convertirse accidentalmente en la entrada.
 const primary=pn.runs.filter(r=>(r.chainId||0)===(pn.primaryChainId||0)&&!r.detached);if(!primary.length)return null;
 const first=primary[0],last=primary[primary.length-1],A=first.points&&first.points[0],B=last.points&&last.points[last.points.length-1];if(!A||!B)return null;
 const primaryStart=first.startS||0,primaryEnd=(last.startS||0)+(last.length||0),routeLength=Math.max(1,primaryEnd-primaryStart);if(routeLength<=25)return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,aTrack=nearestTrackState(A.x,A.z,null,{radius:140,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:120}),bTrack=nearestTrackState(B.x,B.z,null,{radius:140,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:120}),meta=pitLaneSpec();
 let dir=1;
 if(meta&&Number.isFinite(+meta.entry)){
   const expectedE=mod(+meta.entry,L),expectedX=Number.isFinite(+meta.exit)?mod(+meta.exit,L):null,
         costPlus=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(aTrack.s,expectedE,L))+(expectedX==null?0:Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(bTrack.s,expectedX,L))),
         costMinus=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(bTrack.s,expectedE,L))+(expectedX==null?0:Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(aTrack.s,expectedX,L)));
   dir=costPlus<=costMinus?1:-1;
 }else dir=mod(bTrack.s-aTrack.s,L)<=L*.5?1:-1;
 const entry=dir>0?A:B,exit=dir>0?B:A,entryTrack=dir>0?aTrack:bTrack,exitTrack=dir>0?bTrack:aTrack,
       entryPitS=dir>0?primaryStart:primaryEnd,exitPitS=dir>0?primaryEnd:primaryStart,boxFrac=.57,boxPitS=entryPitS+dir*routeLength*boxFrac,
       entryInsideS=clamp(entryPitS+dir*Math.min(8,routeLength*.03),primaryStart,primaryEnd),entryInside=apexPitPointAtS(entryInsideS)||entry;
 const etx=entryInside.x-entry.x,etz=entryInside.z-entry.z,elen=Math.hypot(etx,etz)||1,entryTx=etx/elen,entryTz=etz/elen,
       entryNx=-entryTz,entryNz=entryTx,entryAngle=Math.abs(angleDiff(Math.atan2(entryTx,entryTz),Math.atan2(entryTrack.tx,entryTrack.tz))),
       exitInsideS=clamp(exitPitS-dir*Math.min(8,routeLength*.03),primaryStart,primaryEnd),exitInside=apexPitPointAtS(exitInsideS)||exit,
       xtx=exit.x-exitInside.x,xtz=exit.z-exitInside.z,xlen=Math.hypot(xtx,xtz)||1,exitTx=xtx/xlen,exitTz=xtz/xlen,exitNx=-exitTz,exitNz=exitTx;
 return{pn,primary,primaryStart,primaryEnd,dir,entry,exit,entryTrackS:entryTrack.s,exitTrackS:exitTrack.s,entryPitS,exitPitS,routeLength,boxPitS,boxFrac,
        entryTx,entryTz,entryNx,entryNz,entryAngle,exitTx,exitTz,exitNx,exitNz,speedKph:apexPitSpeedLimitKph(),speedMps:apexPitSpeedLimitKph()/3.6};
}
function apexPitPathControl(plan,pitS,sp,targetSpeed,phase,look=13){
 const aimS=clamp(pitS+plan.dir*look,0,plan.pn.totalLength),aim=apexPitPointAtS(aimS)||apexPitPointAtS(pitS),near=nearestPitState(player.x,player.z,player.pitIndex);if(!aim)return null;
 const desired=Math.atan2(aim.x-player.x,aim.z-player.z),hErr=angleDiff(desired,player.heading),latEff=near?near.lat*plan.dir:0;
 let steer=clamp(hErr*2.05+latEff*.075-(player.yawRate||0)*.20,-.92,.92),err=targetSpeed-sp,throttle=0,brake=0;
 if(err>2.2)throttle=clamp(.34+err*.075,.34,.78);else if(err>.35)throttle=.22;else if(err<-.25)brake=clamp((-err)/7.2,.08,1);
 if(Math.abs(hErr)>.48){throttle*=.48;brake=Math.max(brake,sp>targetSpeed+1?.16:0)}
 player.autoTargetSpeed=targetSpeed;player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed=targetSpeed;player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed=targetSpeed;player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit=targetSpeed;player.autoTargetSpeedReason='PIT';player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail=phase;player.autoPhase=phase;player.autoTargetLat=player.lat;player.autoRawTargetLat=player.lat;player.autoDesiredHeading=desired;player.autoLimiterReduction=0;
 return{steer,throttle,brake,target:targetSpeed,boost:false,reverse:false,targetLat:player.lat,phase};
}
function apexPitStopController(sp){
 if(!demoMode)return null; // Manual/assisted requests do not take over steering or service.
 const st=player&&player.pitStop;if(!st||!st.active)return null;const plan=apexPitRoutePlan();if(!plan){st.active=false;st.phase='FAILED';st.hud='BOX · PIT NO DISPONIBLE';updatePitButtonUI();return null}
 st.speedKph=plan.speedKph;const L=trackData.km*1000,near=nearestPitState(player.x,player.z,player.pitIndex),entryD=mod(plan.entryTrackS-player.s,L),worldEntry=Math.hypot(player.x-plan.entry.x,player.z-plan.entry.z);
 const along=(player.x-plan.entry.x)*plan.entryTx+(player.z-plan.entry.z)*plan.entryTz,across=Math.abs((player.x-plan.entry.x)*plan.entryNx+(player.z-plan.entry.z)*plan.entryNz),gatePassed=along>.45&&across<Math.max(5.5,(near?near.width:5.9)*.95);
 st.entryDistance=entryD;st.entryWorldDistance=worldEntry;st.entryAlong=along;st.entryAcross=across;st.pitSpeedKph=plan.speedKph;
 if(st.phase==='REQUESTED'){
   st.hud=`BOX · SOLICITADO · LÍMITE ${Math.round(plan.speedKph)} km/h`;
   if(entryD<145||worldEntry<115){st.phase='APPROACH';st.approachAt=raceT;st.entryGateCrossed=false}else return null;
 }
 if(st.phase==='APPROACH'){
   // v5.230.13: la entrada es una PUERTA real. Mientras no se cruce el comienzo exacto
   // del pitlane, AUT apunta al endpoint del SVG, nunca a un punto 20-30 m más adentro.
   // Eso impide cortar el muro y "aparecer" a mitad del carril de boxes.
   if(gatePassed||worldEntry<2.2)st.entryGateCrossed=true;
   const insideDist=st.entryGateCrossed?Math.min(11,Math.max(5,sp*.22)):Math.min(1.2,Math.max(.15,worldEntry*.05)),
         aim=apexPitPointAtS(clamp(plan.entryPitS+plan.dir*insideDist,plan.primaryStart,plan.primaryEnd))||plan.entry,
         desired=Math.atan2(aim.x-player.x,aim.z-player.z),hErr=angleDiff(desired,player.heading),turnFactor=clamp(plan.entryAngle/.75,0,1),
         gateSpeed=Math.min(plan.speedMps*1.08,lerp(29,22,turnFactor)),farSpeed=gateSpeed+clamp(entryD,0,130)*.055,
         target=Math.min(31,farSpeed),steer=clamp(hErr*2.15-(player.yawRate||0)*.20,-.94,.94),err=target-sp;
   let throttle=err>2?.62:err>.3?.18:0,brake=err<-.2?clamp((-err)/7.4,.08,1):0;
   if(worldEntry<24&&!st.entryGateCrossed){throttle*=.72;if(sp>gateSpeed)brake=Math.max(brake,clamp((sp-gateSpeed)/6,.12,.82))}
   st.hud=st.entryGateCrossed?`BOX · ENTRANDO EN PIT`:`BOX · PUERTA PIT · ${Math.max(0,Math.round(worldEntry))} m`;
   // No consideramos que ya está "dentro" por proximidad lateral: primero hay que
   // atravesar el extremo inicial del pitline. Después sí puede tomar el control pitS.
   if(st.entryGateCrossed&&near&&near.dist<=near.width*.66+.65){st.phase='IN_LANE';st.enteredAt=raceT;player.driveNetwork='PIT';}
   else{
     const passedTrack=wrapDeltaS(player.s,plan.entryTrackS,L)>18;
     if(passedTrack&&raceT-(st.approachAt||raceT)>8.0&&!st.entryGateCrossed&&worldEntry>38){st.phase='REQUESTED';st.hud='BOX · ENTRADA PERDIDA · PRÓXIMA VUELTA';return null}
   }
   player.autoTargetSpeed=target;player.autoTargetSpeedReason='PIT';player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail='PIT_ENTRY_GATE';player.autoPhase='BOX ENTRADA';
   return{steer,throttle,brake,target,boost:false,reverse:false,targetLat:player.lat,phase:'BOX ENTRADA'};
 }
 const pit=near||player.pitState;if(!pit){st.hud='BOX · BUSCANDO PIT';return null}
 player.pitIndex=pit.index;player.pitS=pit.pitS;player.pitState=pit;
 const progress=plan.dir*(pit.pitS-plan.entryPitS),boxAhead=plan.dir*(plan.boxPitS-pit.pitS),exitAhead=plan.dir*(plan.exitPitS-pit.pitS);
 st.progress=progress;st.boxAhead=boxAhead;st.exitAhead=exitAhead;
 if(!st.serviced){
   if(st.phase!=='SERVICE')st.phase='IN_LANE';
   let target=plan.speedMps;
   if(boxAhead<70){target=Math.min(target,Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,2*5.4*Math.max(0,boxAhead-.8))));if(boxAhead<2.0)target=0;}
   st.hud=target<3?`BOX · DETENIÉNDOSE`:`PIT · ${Math.round(plan.speedKph)} km/h · BOX ${Math.max(0,Math.round(boxAhead))} m`;
   const ctl=apexPitPathControl(plan,pit.pitS,sp,target,'PIT BOX',Math.min(12,Math.max(4,sp*.42)));
   if((boxAhead<=1.8||boxAhead<0)&&sp<.65){st.phase='SERVICE';st.serviceStarted=raceT;st.serviceUntil=raceT+2.8;player.vx*=.25;player.vz*=.25;}
   if(st.phase!=='SERVICE')return ctl;
 }
 if(st.phase==='SERVICE'){
   player.vx*=.35;player.vz*=.35;if(Math.hypot(player.vx,player.vz)<.16){player.vx=0;player.vz=0;player.speed=0}
   const remain=Math.max(0,(st.serviceUntil||raceT)-raceT);st.hud=`BOX · CAMBIO DE NEUMÁTICOS · ${remain.toFixed(1)} s`;
   player.autoTargetSpeed=0;player.autoTargetSpeedReason='PIT';player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail='TYRE_CHANGE';player.autoPhase='BOX PARADA';
   if(remain<=0){st.serviced=true;st.phase='EXITING';st.serviceCompletedAt=raceT;st.exitStartedAt=raceT;st.exitGateCrossed=false;player.driveNetwork='PIT';player.brakeInput=0;player.throttle=0;player.tyreSet=(player.tyreSet||1)+1;player.tyreAge=0;player.pitStops=(player.pitStops||0)+1;st.hud=`BOX · NEUMÁTICOS NUEVOS · JUEGO ${player.tyreSet}`;const a=$('raceAlert');if(a){a.textContent=st.hud;a.style.color='#8dffb6'}}
   return{steer:0,throttle:0,brake:1,target:0,boost:false,reverse:false,targetLat:player.lat,phase:'BOX PARADA'};
 }
 if(st.phase==='EXITING'){
   const worldExit=Math.hypot(player.x-plan.exit.x,player.z-plan.exit.z),exitAlong=(player.x-plan.exit.x)*plan.exitTx+(player.z-plan.exit.z)*plan.exitTz,
         exitAcross=Math.abs((player.x-plan.exit.x)*plan.exitNx+(player.z-plan.exit.z)*plan.exitNz),exitGatePassed=exitAlong>.35&&exitAcross<Math.max(5.5,(pit.width||5.9)*.98),elapsed=Math.max(0,raceT-(st.exitStartedAt||raceT));
   // Salida progresiva: libera freno y sube desde ~14 km/h hasta el límite de pit.
   const target=Math.min(plan.speedMps,3.8+elapsed*5.0);let ctl;
   if(exitAhead>5.0&&!exitGatePassed){ctl=apexPitPathControl(plan,pit.pitS,sp,target,'PIT SALIDA',Math.min(15,Math.max(5,sp*.50)))}
   else{
     const aim={x:plan.exit.x+plan.exitTx*9,z:plan.exit.z+plan.exitTz*9},desired=Math.atan2(aim.x-player.x,aim.z-player.z),hErr=angleDiff(desired,player.heading),err=target-sp;
     ctl={steer:clamp(hErr*2.0-(player.yawRate||0)*.18,-.90,.90),throttle:err>1.0?clamp(.30+err*.08,.30,.72):err>.2?.18:0,brake:err<-.3?clamp((-err)/8,.06,.75):0,target,boost:false,reverse:false,targetLat:player.lat,phase:'PIT SALIDA'};
   }
   st.exitWorldDistance=worldExit;st.exitAlong=exitAlong;st.exitAcross=exitAcross;st.hud=`PIT · SALIDA · ${Math.max(0,Math.round(Math.max(exitAhead,worldExit)))} m`;
   if(exitGatePassed||(worldExit<1.6&&exitAhead<=1.8)){st.exitGateCrossed=true;st.phase='MERGE_OUT';st.mergeStartedAt=raceT;player.driveNetwork='TRACK';}
   return ctl;
 }
 if(st.phase==='MERGE_OUT'){
   player.driveNetwork='TRACK';const elapsed=Math.max(0,raceT-(st.mergeStartedAt||raceT)),ahead=clamp(24+elapsed*8,24,48),tq=poseAt(plan.exitTrackS+ahead),desired=Math.atan2(tq.x-player.x,tq.z-player.z),hErr=angleDiff(desired,player.heading),target=Math.min(36,plan.speedMps+elapsed*4.2),err=target-sp,
         ctl={steer:clamp(hErr*1.85-(player.yawRate||0)*.17,-.86,.86),throttle:err>1?.62:err>.2?.20:0,brake:err<-.35?clamp((-err)/9,.06,.70):0,target,boost:false,reverse:false,targetLat:player.lat,phase:'PIT REINCORPORACIÓN'};
   const tr=nearestTrackState(player.x,player.z,player.trackIndex),e=envProfile(tr.s);st.hud='PIT · REINCORPORACIÓN';
   if((Math.abs(tr.lat)<Math.max(2.2,e.half*.68)&&elapsed>.8)||elapsed>4.5){st.phase='DONE';st.doneUntil=raceT+2.0;st.hud=`BOX · PARADA COMPLETADA · JUEGO ${player.tyreSet||2}`;}
   return ctl;
 }
 if(st.phase==='DONE'){
   st.hud=`BOX · PARADA COMPLETADA · JUEGO ${player.tyreSet||2}`;
   if(raceT>(st.doneUntil||raceT)){player.pitStop=null;updatePitButtonUI();return null}
   return null;
 }
 return null;
}
function pitStopHudStatus(){return player&&player.pitStop&&player.pitStop.active?player.pitStop.hud||'BOX · PARADA SOLICITADA':''}
function updatePitButtonUI(){
 const b=$('box');if(!b)return;const hasPit=!!apexPitRoutePlan(),st=player&&player.pitStop&&player.pitStop.active?player.pitStop:null,usable=!!(player&&hasPit);
 b.disabled=!usable;b.classList.toggle('on',!!st&&st.phase!=='SERVICE');b.classList.toggle('service',!!st&&st.phase==='SERVICE');b.textContent='BOX';
 b.title=!player?'Parada en boxes':!hasPit?'Este circuito no tiene pit lane':'Solicitar parada en boxes · límite '+Math.round(apexPitSpeedLimitKph())+' km/h';
}
function requestPitStop(){
 if(!player)return;const plan=apexPitRoutePlan();if(!plan){const a=$('raceAlert');if(a){a.textContent='BOX · PIT NO DISPONIBLE';a.style.color='#ff8b8b'}return}
 const st=player.pitStop;
 if(st&&st.active&&(st.phase==='REQUESTED'||st.phase==='APPROACH')){player.pitStop=null;const a=$('raceAlert');if(a){a.textContent='BOX · SOLICITUD CANCELADA';a.style.color='#d8e2ed'}updatePitButtonUI();return}
 if(st&&st.active)return;
 player.pitStop={active:true,phase:player.driveNetwork==='PIT'?'IN_LANE':'REQUESTED',requestedAt:raceT,serviced:false,hud:`BOX · SOLICITADO · LÍMITE ${Math.round(plan.speedKph)} km/h`};
 const a=$('raceAlert');if(a){a.textContent=player.pitStop.hud;a.style.color='#8dffb6'}updatePitButtonUI();
}

function demoDriverControl(){
 if(!player||!trackData)return {steer:0,throttle:0,brake:0,target:0,boost:false,reverse:false};
 const sp=Math.max(0,finite(player.speed,0)),env=envProfile(player.s),diff=Number.isFinite(sessionSettings.diff)?sessionSettings.diff:1;
 autoEnsureRiskMemory();
 const pitCtl=apexPitStopController(sp);if(pitCtl)return pitCtl;
 const skill=clamp(.982+(diff-1)*.10,.965,.995),bravery=clamp(.952+(diff-1)*.10,.925,.985);
 const recovery=autoRecoveryController(sp,env);
 if(recovery){
   const recoveryTarget=Math.max(0,finite(recovery.target,0));
   player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed=Math.max(recoveryTarget,autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(player.s)*.97);
   player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed=recoveryTarget;player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit=null;
   player.autoTargetSpeedReason="RECOVERY";player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail=recovery.phase||"RECOVERY";
   player.autoTargetSpeed=recoveryTarget;player.autoLimiterReduction=Math.max(0,player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed-recoveryTarget);
   player.autoDecisionAfterGeometry=recoveryTarget;player.autoDecisionBeforeStability=recoveryTarget;player.autoDecisionAfterStability=recoveryTarget;player.autoRawTargetLat=player.autoTargetLat;player.autoDesiredHeading=player.heading;player.autoLaneRate=0;
   player.autoRecoveryAssist=!!recovery.recoveryAssist;player.autoRecoveryForcePull=!!recovery.recoveryForcePull;
   return recovery;
 }
 player.autoRecoveryAssist=false;player.autoRecoveryForcePull=false;
 if(!player.autoPlan||raceT-(player.autoPlanStamp||-99)>.055){
   player.autoPlan=driverCornerPlan(player.s,360,8);player.autoPlanStamp=raceT;
 }
 const plan=player.autoPlan,traffic=playerAutoTraffic(plan),raceTraffic=traffic.snap||autoRaceTrafficSnapshot(),wallCtx=apexWallContextAtS(player.s,env),wallish=wallCtx.wallish,technical=autoTechnicalProfile(player.s,118,5),compound=autoCompoundEntryProfile(player.s,118,5);
 const techDrive=autoTechnicalDriveNow(player.s,technical,sp);
 player.autoTechnicalAhead=!!technical.active;player.autoTechnicalActive=!!techDrive;
 const trackEdge=env.half,offTrack=Math.abs(player.lat)>trackEdge+env.curb*.25;
 const learnedZone=autoRiskAhead(player.s,82,8),learnedLong=autoRiskAhead(player.s,155,10);
 const geometryKink=geometricKinkAhead(player.s,112,3);
 player.autoGeometryKinkAngle=geometryKink.angle||0;
 player.autoGeometryKinkD=geometryKink.active?geometryKink.d:null;
 player.autoGeometryKinkGuard=false;
 // v5.149: un vértice fuerte ya no es sólo una limitación de velocidad "antes" del nodo.
 // Guardamos también la dirección de SALIDA y la mantenemos durante unos instantes después
 // de cruzarlo. Esto evita el fallo visto en un circuito importado: el guard desaparecía justo al pasar
 // el nodo de 101°/46° y el coche aún estaba 30-50° desalineado con la nueva recta.
 const kinkAngle=geometryKink.angle||0;
 const kinkExtreme=kinkAngle>Math.PI*.47;   // >~85°
 const kinkHard=kinkAngle>Math.PI*.31;      // >~56°
 const kinkMedium=kinkAngle>Math.PI*.20;    // >~36°
 const kinkApproachD=kinkExtreme?96:(kinkHard?78:(kinkMedium?66:0));
 const kinkCenterD=kinkExtreme?88:(kinkHard?66:(kinkMedium?52:0));
 const kinkCommitD=kinkExtreme?24:(kinkHard?20:(kinkMedium?16:0));
 if(geometryKink.active&&kinkMedium&&geometryKink.d<=kinkCommitD){
   player.autoKinkCommitUntil=raceT+(kinkExtreme?2.15:(kinkHard?1.65:1.25));
   player.autoKinkCommitHeading=geometryKink.to;
   player.autoKinkCommitAngle=kinkAngle;
 }
 const kinkCommitActive=Number.isFinite(player.autoKinkCommitUntil)&&raceT<player.autoKinkCommitUntil&&Number.isFinite(player.autoKinkCommitHeading);
 player.autoKinkCommitActive=!!kinkCommitActive;
 player.autoKinkCommitRemaining=kinkCommitActive?Math.max(0,player.autoKinkCommitUntil-raceT):0;
 player.autoKinkCommitBlend=0;
 player.autoLaneSwingGuard=false;
 player.autoLaneSwingLimit=null;

 let rawTarget=techDrive?autoTechnicalLane(player.s,technical,skill):autoLaneAt(player.s,skill,bravery,traffic.lane);
 const launchLaneLock=currentSession==='race'&&!!player.autoLaunchCorridorHold&&Number.isFinite(traffic.lane);
 player.autoFirstComplexLaneLock=!!launchLaneLock;
 player.autoTechnicalRiskPrep=0;
 // v5.147: el corredor de parrilla manda también dentro de TÉCNICA. En v5.146
 // playerAutoTraffic() seguía diciendo "hold", pero autoTechnicalLane() lo sobrescribía:
 // en un circuito importado el coche salió de +1.95 m, cruzó hacia -0.63 m justo al llegar a T1 y quedó
 // expuesto a un toque lateral. Frenet permite conservar esa calle paralela a la pista.
 if(launchLaneLock){
   rawTarget=clamp(traffic.lane,-traffic.limit,traffic.limit);
   // El corredor de parrilla sólo debe proteger la salida, no obligar al coche a
   // mantener ±1.95 m hasta dentro de un vértice poligonal. Al acercarnos a una
   // discontinuidad fuerte del trazado, reducimos progresivamente la excentricidad.
   if(geometryKink.active&&geometryKink.angle>Math.PI*.20&&geometryKink.d<42){
     const release=clamp((42-geometryKink.d)/30,0,.84);
     rawTarget=lerp(rawTarget,clamp(rawTarget,-.65,.65),release);
     player.autoFirstChicaneRelease=release;
   }else player.autoFirstChicaneRelease=0;
 }else{
   player.autoFirstChicaneRelease=0;
   if(!techDrive&&compound.active&&!Number.isFinite(traffic.lane))rawTarget*=1-clamp(.10+compound.severity*.16,.10,.24);
   if(!techDrive&&learnedZone.risk>.15&&!Number.isFinite(traffic.lane))rawTarget*=1-clamp(learnedZone.risk*.52,0,.48);
   // La memoria de una salida debe preparar también LA LÍNEA antes de volver a una
   // secuencia técnica. V14 frenaba demasiado en el circuito técnico, pero repetía la salida en s≈5465
   // porque el coche seguía llegando desde el exterior y el riesgo sólo recortaba km/h.
   if(technical.active&&!Number.isFinite(traffic.lane)&&learnedLong.risk>.24){
     const prep=clamp((learnedLong.risk-.24)/.62,0,.78);
     rawTarget=lerp(rawTarget,0,prep);
     player.autoTechnicalRiskPrep=prep;
   }
   if(techDrive&&!Number.isFinite(traffic.lane)&&learnedZone.risk>.32){
     const localPrep=clamp((learnedZone.risk-.30)/.55,0,.58);
     rawTarget=lerp(rawTarget,0,localPrep);
     player.autoTechnicalRiskPrep=Math.max(player.autoTechnicalRiskPrep||0,localPrep);
   }
   // En zona técnica sólo aceptamos una calle de adelantamiento si estamos claramente
   // antes de la secuencia. Dentro de la chicane se sigue la puerta actual, no al rival.
   if(techDrive&&Number.isFinite(traffic.lane)&&technical.firstTightD>70)rawTarget=clamp(traffic.lane,-.85,.85);
   // Los nodos duros del circuito importado necesitan llegar centrados. Fuera de la salida de parrilla
   // dejamos de perseguir una trazada de ±3 m cuando faltan pocos metros para un cambio
   // de dirección de 36-100°. En la primera chicane, mientras siga el corredor de salida,
   // manda el bloque específico de arriba para no cruzar coches de una columna a otra.
   if(geometryKink.active&&kinkMedium&&geometryKink.d<kinkCenterD){
     const centerBlend=clamp((kinkCenterD-geometryKink.d)/Math.max(18,kinkCenterD-8),0,kinkExtreme?.94:(kinkHard?.84:.70));
     rawTarget=lerp(rawTarget,0,centerBlend);
   }
   if(kinkCommitActive)rawTarget=lerp(rawTarget,0,kinkExtreme?.96:(kinkHard?.90:.82));
 }
 if(offTrack)rawTarget=0;
 const safeLimit=autoSafeLaneLimit(player.s),contactGuardActive=currentSession==='race'&&(player.autoContactGuardUntil||-99)>raceT;
 // v5.149: en circuitos urbanos el AUT no debe pedir de golpe una trazada de ±3 m a
 // 110-150 km/h. La telemetría del circuito importado libre pasó de lat -0.85 a un objetivo +2.95 m
 // antes de s≈1560 y provocó un giro de 63°. Limitamos sólo la magnitud de la trazada
 // a alta velocidad; no tocamos una calle de tráfico lateral real ni la salida de parrilla.
 if(wallish&&!offTrack&&!launchLaneLock&&sp>24&&!raceTraffic.side){
   const speedBlend=clamp((sp-24)/22,0,1);
   const bend=Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+18)),bendBlend=clamp((bend-.003)/.020,0,1);
   const cityLaneLimit=Math.max(1.15,lerp(2.30,1.42,speedBlend)-bendBlend*.30);
   player.autoLaneSwingLimit=cityLaneLimit;
   if(Math.abs(rawTarget)>cityLaneLimit){
     rawTarget=clamp(rawTarget,-cityLaneLimit,cityLaneLimit);
     player.autoLaneSwingGuard=true;
   }
 }
 if(contactGuardActive&&!offTrack){
   const hold=clamp(Number.isFinite(player.autoContactHoldLat)?player.autoContactHoldLat:player.lat,-safeLimit,safeLimit);
   rawTarget=lerp(rawTarget,hold,.78);
 }
 rawTarget=clamp(rawTarget,-safeLimit,safeLimit);
 if(!Number.isFinite(player.autoTargetLat))player.autoTargetLat=player.lat;
 const lastStamp=Number.isFinite(player.autoLineStamp)?player.autoLineStamp:raceT,adt=clamp(raceT-lastStamp,.004,.060);
 player.autoLineStamp=raceT;
 const normalLaneRate=wallish?2.75:lerp(3.55,2.65,clamp((sp-34)/46,0,1));
 // La calle técnica ya no puede saltar lateralmente a 4.55 m/s a 180–220 km/h.
 const techLaneRate=lerp(3.05,1.75,clamp((sp-18)/45,0,1));
 let laneRate=offTrack?6.9:(techDrive?techLaneRate:(compound.active?Math.min(normalLaneRate,2.70):normalLaneRate));
 if(!techDrive&&learnedLong.risk>.35)laneRate=Math.min(laneRate,lerp(2.25,1.55,clamp(learnedLong.risk,0,1)));
 if(raceTraffic.active&&(raceTraffic.launchProtect||raceTraffic.packRisk>.35||raceTraffic.side))laneRate=Math.min(laneRate,raceTraffic.launchProtect?1.55:1.95);
