 if(wallish&&!offTrack&&!launchLaneLock&&sp>25&&!raceTraffic.side){
   laneRate=Math.min(laneRate,lerp(2.10,1.22,clamp((sp-25)/24,0,1)));
 }
 if(geometryKink.active&&kinkMedium)laneRate=Math.min(laneRate,kinkExtreme?1.12:(kinkHard?1.32:1.48));
 if(kinkCommitActive)laneRate=Math.min(laneRate,kinkExtreme?1.02:1.18);
 if(contactGuardActive)laneRate=Math.min(laneRate,1.20);
 player.autoRawTargetLat=rawTarget;player.autoLaneRate=laneRate;
 player.autoTargetLat+=clamp(rawTarget-player.autoTargetLat,-laneRate*adt,laneRate*adt);
 player.autoTargetLat=clamp(player.autoTargetLat,-safeLimit,safeLimit);

 // Controlador basado en geometría del monoplaza. El término feed-forward calcula el
 // ángulo de volante que necesita aproximadamente la batalla de 3,60 m para seguir la
 // curvatura, en lugar del antiguo multiplicador empírico ffK*64.
 let look=techDrive?clamp(5.8+sp*.13,6.5,13):clamp(8+sp*.26,10,34);
 if(!techDrive&&compound.active)look=Math.min(38,Math.max(look,clamp(16+compound.firstD*.18,17,31)));
 if(!techDrive&&plan&&plan.d<80)look=Math.min(look,clamp(9+plan.d*.24,10,25));
 if(offTrack)look=Math.min(look,techDrive?7.5:13);
 // Puerta secuencial: en una chicane miramos sólo al siguiente pequeño tramo de pista.
 // Así no existe una cuerda geométrica que pueda atravesar el segundo vértice.
 const q=poseAt(player.s+look),futureTrafficLane=(!techDrive&&traffic.ahead&&traffic.ahead.d<55&&Number.isFinite(traffic.lane))?traffic.lane:null;
 const futureLat=offTrack?0:(techDrive?(autoTechnicalLane(player.s+look,autoTechnicalProfile(player.s+look,70,5),skill)||0):autoLaneAt(player.s+look,skill,bravery,futureTrafficLane));
 const laneSlope=clamp((futureLat-player.autoTargetLat)/Math.max(look,1),techDrive?-.075:-.16,techDrive?.075:.16);
 let tangentHeading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);
 // Un nodo bruto no puede pedir 60-100° de cambio de dirección en un único instante.
 // Primero limitamos el salto del look-ahead; después, al entrar en la "puerta" del
 // vértice, empezamos a comprometer el coche con la tangente de SALIDA. El segundo
 // término es clave: en v5.148 el guard desaparecía al cruzar el nodo y el coche quedaba
 // orientado hacia la rama antigua.
 if(geometryKink.active&&kinkMedium&&geometryKink.d<look+36){
   const nowQ=poseAt(player.s),nowHeading=Math.atan2(nowQ.tx,nowQ.tz);
   const maxAheadTurn=clamp(.22+look*.030,.28,.64);
   tangentHeading=nowHeading+clamp(angleDiff(tangentHeading,nowHeading),-maxAheadTurn,maxAheadTurn);
   player.autoGeometryKinkGuard=true;
 }
 if(geometryKink.active&&kinkMedium&&geometryKink.d<kinkCommitD+8){
   const commitBlend=clamp((kinkCommitD+8-geometryKink.d)/Math.max(10,kinkCommitD+3),0,kinkExtreme?.90:(kinkHard?.82:.70));
   tangentHeading+=angleDiff(geometryKink.to,tangentHeading)*commitBlend;
   player.autoKinkCommitBlend=Math.max(player.autoKinkCommitBlend||0,commitBlend);
   player.autoGeometryKinkGuard=true;
 }else if(kinkCommitActive){
   const postBlend=kinkExtreme?.78:(kinkHard?.70:.60);
   tangentHeading+=angleDiff(player.autoKinkCommitHeading,tangentHeading)*postBlend;
   player.autoKinkCommitBlend=Math.max(player.autoKinkCommitBlend||0,postBlend);
 }
 const desiredHeading=tangentHeading+Math.atan(-laneSlope);
 player.autoDesiredHeading=desiredHeading;
 const headingErr=angleDiff(desiredHeading,player.heading),crossErr=player.lat-player.autoTargetLat;
 const kRef=curvatureAt(player.s+(techDrive?clamp(5+sp*.06,5.5,10):clamp(7+sp*.09,8,17)));
 const maxSteerModel=finiteClamp(.285/(1+sp*.0105),.058,.285,.18);
 const deltaFF=Math.atan(3.60*kRef),ff=clamp(deltaFF/Math.max(.055,maxSteerModel),-1,1);
 const crossGain=techDrive?clamp(.175-sp*.0009,.090,.175):clamp(.145-sp*.0008,.072,.145);
 let steer=ff+headingErr*(techDrive?2.85:2.55)+crossErr*crossGain-(player.yawRate||0)*(techDrive?.105:.105)-(player.slipAngle||0)*(techDrive?.44:.44);
 // En curvas rápidas la corrección no puede saltar muy lejos del feed-forward físico:
 // evita un latigazo de volante por un error transversal momentáneo (causa típica de trompo).
 if(!techDrive&&sp>32&&!offTrack){
   let corrBand=lerp(.40,.22,clamp((sp-32)/42,0,1));
   if(compound.active)corrBand=Math.min(corrBand,lerp(.24,.17,compound.severity));
   steer=clamp(steer,ff-corrBand,ff+corrBand);
 }

 const trNow=player.track||poseAt(player.s),latVel=(player.vx||0)*trNow.nx+(player.vz||0)*trNow.nz;
 const outward=(Math.sign(player.lat)||1)*latVel;
 const edgeGuard=wallish?Math.max(1.05,trackEdge-2.45):Math.max(1.40,trackEdge-1.70);
 const predictedLat=player.lat+latVel*(wallish?.92:.72);
 let edgeRisk=0;
 if(Math.abs(player.lat)>edgeGuard)edgeRisk=Math.max(edgeRisk,clamp((Math.abs(player.lat)-edgeGuard)/1.35,0,1));
 if(Math.abs(predictedLat)>edgeGuard)edgeRisk=Math.max(edgeRisk,clamp((Math.abs(predictedLat)-edgeGuard)/1.45,0,1));
 if(outward>.05)edgeRisk=Math.max(edgeRisk,clamp(outward/(wallish?4.8:6.0),0,1));
 if(offTrack)edgeRisk=1;
 if(edgeRisk>0){
   const inward=Math.sign(player.lat)||Math.sign(crossErr)||1;
   steer=lerp(steer,inward*(wallish?.98:.88),clamp(.22+edgeRisk*.68,0,.92));
 }
 // Dentro y justo después de un vértice poligonal, la prioridad es terminar el giro.
 // A baja/media velocidad damos autoridad directa a la tangente de salida; desaparece
 // al completar la rotación y no afecta a trazados de referencia ni a curvas suaves.
 if(kinkCommitActive&&sp<30&&edgeRisk<.88){
   const commitErr=angleDiff(player.autoKinkCommitHeading,player.heading);
   if(Math.abs(commitErr)>.08){
     const commitSteer=clamp(commitErr*1.95,-1,1);
     const commitAuthority=clamp(.24+(Math.abs(commitErr)-.08)*.72,0,kinkExtreme?.78:(kinkHard?.68:.56));
     steer=lerp(steer,commitSteer,commitAuthority);
   } }
 const trackHeading=Math.atan2(trNow.tx,trNow.tz),trackHeadingErr=angleDiff(trackHeading,player.heading),align=Math.cos(trackHeadingErr);
 if(align<.62){steer=clamp(trackHeadingErr*2.55,-1,1);}
 steer=clamp(steer,-1,1);

 let target=autoTargetSpeed(plan,null,skill,bravery,diff);
 const baseTrackTarget=target;
 let targetReason="TRACK",targetReasonDetail=null,trafficSpeedLimit=Infinity;
 if(technical.active){
   const targetBeforeTechnical=target;
   // En v5.125 la reducción fuerte aparecía demasiado tarde al cruzar un umbral fijo.
   // Ahora la puerta lenta genera una curva de frenada continua desde bastante antes.
   const techFactor=technical.hairpin?.95:(technical.chicane?.975:.985);
   const techCap=Math.max(14.5,technical.minSafe*techFactor);
   if(Number.isFinite(technical.firstTightD)&&technical.firstTightD<150){
     const dTech=Math.max(0,technical.firstTightD-(technical.chicane?10:7));
     const techDecel=(apexWeatherIsWet()?8.7:11.8)*teamPerformance(player.teamKey).brake;
     const allowedTech=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,techCap*techCap+2*techDecel*dTech));
     target=Math.min(target,allowedTech);
   }
   if(technical.firstTightD<16)target=Math.min(target,techCap*1.02);
   if(technical.changeD<30)target=Math.min(target,techCap*.99);
   if(target<targetBeforeTechnical-.02){targetReason="TECHNICAL";targetReasonDetail=technical.chicane?"CHICANE":technical.hairpin?"HAIRPIN":"TIGHT_CURVE";}
 }
 // Guard geométrico independiente de tráfico y de la memoria aprendida.
 // En un circuito importado T1 el SVG daba un salto bruto de ~72°. La IA llegaba a ~62 km/h y el
 // marco de pista cambiaba de dirección antes de que el monoplaza pudiera rotar.
 // Una discontinuidad >36° recibe una aproximación progresiva y un vértice mucho
 // más lento; no afecta a curvas suaves ni a rectas.
 if(geometryKink.active&&kinkMedium&&geometryKink.d<kinkApproachD){
   // Política por severidad. El nodo de 101° del circuito importado necesita llegar mucho más lento
   // que la primera chicane de 72°, mientras que uno de ~46° sólo requiere reserva.
   const apex=kinkExtreme?7.2:(kinkHard?9.8:12.0); // 26 / 35 / 43 km/h
   const gain=kinkExtreme?.17:(kinkHard?.19:.20);
   const approach=apex+clamp((geometryKink.d-3)*gain,0,kinkExtreme?16:(kinkHard?14:12));
   const beforeKink=target;
   target=Math.min(target,approach);
   if(target<beforeKink-.02){
     targetReason="GEOMETRY_KINK";
     targetReasonDetail=kinkExtreme?"EXTREME_POLYLINE":"POLYLINE_TANGENT";
     player.autoGeometryKinkGuard=true;
   }
 }
 if(kinkCommitActive){
   const a=player.autoKinkCommitAngle||0;
   const exitCap=a>Math.PI*.47?9.0:(a>Math.PI*.31?12.2:15.0); // 32 / 44 / 54 km/h
   const beforeCommit=target;
   target=Math.min(target,exitCap);
   if(target<beforeCommit-.02){
     targetReason="KINK_COMMIT";
     targetReasonDetail="EXIT_TANGENT";
     player.autoGeometryKinkGuard=true;
   }
 }
 if(!technical.active&&compound.active){
   const targetBeforeCompound=target;
   // No hacemos lento todo el circuito: sólo reservamos unos puntos porcentuales cuando
   // la curva se cierra después de una entrada rápida. Una salida cuesta mucho más que
   // ceder 3-6 km/h en este único tipo de curva.
   const proximity=1-clamp(compound.firstD/88,0,1);
   const trim=clamp((.020+.045*compound.severity)*(.45+.55*proximity),.012,.062);
   target*=1-trim;
   if(compound.peakD<34){
     const peakSafe=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(player.s+compound.peakD)*(0.925+.018*skill);
     target=Math.min(target,peakSafe+clamp(compound.peakD*.11,0,3.5));
   }
   if(target<targetBeforeCompound-.02){targetReason="COMPOUND_ENTRY";targetReasonDetail="TIGHTENING_CURVE";}
 }
 const survival=autoRaceSurvivalGovernor(player.s,sp,raceTraffic);
 player.autoRaceSurvivalActive=!!survival.active;player.autoRaceSurvivalTarget=survival.target;player.autoRaceSurvivalCornerD=survival.active?survival.d:null;player.autoRaceSurvivalSafe=survival.active?survival.safe:null;player.autoRaceSurvivalCleanRelease=!!survival.cleanRelease;player.autoRaceSurvivalRiskAhead=survival.riskAhead||0;
 if(survival.active&&survival.target<target-.20){
   target=survival.target;targetReason="RACE_SURVIVAL";targetReasonDetail=survival.firstLap?"FIRST_LAP_CORNER":"CORNER_MARGIN";
 }
 const raceLearningSuppressed=currentSession==='race'&&(raceT<12||raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<3.0||(raceTraffic.packRisk||0)>.55);
 const targetBeforeLearning=target;
 if(!raceLearningSuppressed&&learnedLong.risk>.28)target*=1-clamp((learnedLong.risk-.22)*.045,0,.032);
 if(!raceLearningSuppressed&&learnedZone.risk>.16)target*=1-clamp(learnedZone.risk*.046,0,.044);
 if(target<targetBeforeLearning-.02){targetReason="LOCAL_RISK";targetReasonDetail="LEARNED_ZONE";}
 if(edgeRisk>.16){const er=clamp((edgeRisk-.16)/.84,0,1),cap=lerp(56,18,er);if(cap<target-.02){targetReason="EDGE";targetReasonDetail="TRACK_EDGE";}target=Math.min(target,cap);}
 if(offTrack){if(18<target-.02){targetReason="OFF_TRACK";targetReasonDetail="SURFACE";}target=Math.min(target,18);}
 if(align<.62){if(14<target-.02){targetReason="ALIGN";targetReasonDetail="TRACK_HEADING";}target=Math.min(target,14);}
 if(Math.abs(headingErr)>.48){if(22<target-.02){targetReason="ALIGN";targetReasonDetail="DESIRED_HEADING";}target=Math.min(target,22);}
 const targetBeforeStability=target;
 const stability=autoDynamicStability(sp,env,steer,target);
 player.autoStabilityRisk=stability.risk;player.autoRearCritical=stability.rearCritical;player.autoBrakeRelease=stability.brakeRelease||0;
 steer=stability.steer;target=stability.target;
 const dirStart=techDrive?.11:.16,dirSpan=techDrive?.31:.42;
 const dirLoss=clamp((Math.abs(trackHeadingErr)-dirStart)/dirSpan,0,1);
 if(sp>18&&dirLoss>.02){
   const tangentSteer=clamp(trackHeadingErr*(techDrive?1.58:1.75),techDrive?-.58:-.68,techDrive?.58:.68);
   const dirBlend=techDrive?clamp(.16+dirLoss*.68,0,.82):clamp(dirLoss*.62,0,.62);
   steer=lerp(steer,tangentSteer,dirBlend);
   target=Math.min(target,sp*(1-(techDrive?.25:.18)*dirLoss));
   if(dirLoss>(techDrive?.25:.34)){targetReason="STABILITY";targetReasonDetail="DIRECTION_GUARD";}
 }
 if(target<targetBeforeStability-.42&&targetReason!=="STABILITY"){targetReason="STABILITY";targetReasonDetail=stability.rearCritical>.25?"REAR_CRITICAL":"DYNAMIC_STABILITY";}
 // La telemetría de una primera salida de referencia mostró alternancias rápidas de signo
 // en TÉCNICA. Limitamos la velocidad del comando, no el ángulo final disponible.
 const steerStamp=Number.isFinite(player.autoSteerStamp)?player.autoSteerStamp:raceT;
 const steerDt=clamp(raceT-steerStamp,.004,.060);player.autoSteerStamp=raceT;
 if(!Number.isFinite(player.autoSteerCmd))player.autoSteerCmd=steer;
 const highSpeed=clamp((sp-24)/50,0,1);
 let steerRate=techDrive?lerp(4.1,2.15,highSpeed):lerp(5.8,3.25,highSpeed);
 if(stability.rearCritical>.20)steerRate*=lerp(1,.58,stability.rearCritical);
 player.autoSteerCmd+=clamp(steer-player.autoSteerCmd,-steerRate*steerDt,steerRate*steerDt);
 steer=clamp(player.autoSteerCmd,-1,1);
 autoLearnLocalRisk(stability,sp,env);
 const raceNoisy=currentSession==='race'&&(raceT<12||raceT-(player.autoLastRivalContact||-99)<2.5||(raceTraffic.packRisk||0)>.58);
 if(!raceNoisy)updateAutoAdaptivePace(stability,sp,env);
 // Racecraft: velocidad relativa y calle estable cuando hay coches muy cerca. No cambia
 // el ritmo de pista libre que ya dio dos vueltas consecutivas estables.
 const preTrafficTarget=target;
 if(raceTraffic.active){
   const a=raceTraffic.ahead;
   if(a&&a.d<12&&a.latAbs<2.15){
     const passCommitted=Number.isFinite(traffic.lane)&&Math.abs(traffic.lane-a.lat)>1.72&&autoRaceLaneClear(traffic.lane,raceTraffic);
     const sepProgress=passCommitted?clamp((a.latAbs-1.05)/1.05,0,1):0;
     let buffer=a.d<5.3?Math.max(0,a.speed-.65):a.speed+clamp((a.d-5.3)*.52,.6,3.6);
     // En cuanto la calle de adelantamiento queda claramente separada, deja de copiar
     // la velocidad del coche lento. Mientras aún hay solape conserva el colchón.
     if(passCommitted)buffer=lerp(buffer,target,sepProgress*.94);
     trafficSpeedLimit=Math.min(trafficSpeedLimit,buffer);
     if(buffer<target-.28){targetReason="TRAFFIC";targetReasonDetail=passCommitted?"PASS_ESCAPE":"FOLLOW_AHEAD";}
     target=Math.min(target,buffer);
   }
   const side=raceTraffic.side;
   if(side&&Math.abs(side.d)<4.4&&side.latAbs<2.45){
     const rivalSide=Math.sign(side.latDelta)||1;
     const steeringToward=(steer*rivalSide)>0;
     if(steeringToward)steer*=.22;
     // SIDE_BY_SIDE ya no frena por un rival situado a casi 3,4 m lateralmente. Sólo
     // limita velocidad cuando existe solape real de carrocerías/calle.
     if(side.latAbs<1.78||Math.abs(side.d)<1.65){
       const sideCap=Math.max(20,side.speed+3.0);
       trafficSpeedLimit=Math.min(trafficSpeedLimit,sideCap);
       if(sideCap<target-.28){targetReason="TRAFFIC";targetReasonDetail="SIDE_BY_SIDE";}
       target=Math.min(target,sideCap);
     }
   }
 }

 // v5.230.14 · Compromiso continuo de frenada. La telemetría de La telemetría mostró en la
 // misma curva una orden que saltaba ~102 -> 299 km/h en unas décimas y volvía a caer
 // después a ~61 km/h. El coche ya estaba cargado lateralmente y ese "release" hacía
 // imposible estabilizarlo. Las reducciones siguen siendo inmediatas; las subidas del
 // objetivo se liberan despacio mientras exista curvatura relevante delante.
 const commitStamp=Number.isFinite(player.autoCommittedTargetStamp)?player.autoCommittedTargetStamp:raceT,commitDt=clamp(raceT-commitStamp,.004,.080);player.autoCommittedTargetStamp=raceT;
 const curvePressure=Math.max(Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+8)),Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+28)),Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+52)));
 const brakeComplex=technical.active||compound.active||curvePressure>.00115||(plan&&plan.d<135&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.00125);
 if(!Number.isFinite(player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed))player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed=target;
 if(target<=player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed){player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed=target;}
 else{const rise=brakeComplex?1.75:8.5;player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed=Math.min(target,player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed+rise*commitDt);}
 if(brakeComplex&&player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed<target-.04){target=player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed;if(targetReason==='TRACK'){targetReason='BRAKE_COMMIT';targetReasonDetail='CONTINUOUS_CORNER';}}
 else if(!brakeComplex&&target-player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed>7)player.autoCommittedTargetSpeed=target-7;

 const err=target-sp,curveLoad=clamp(Math.abs(kRef)*190,0,1),steerLoad=clamp(Math.abs(steer),0,1);
 let throttle=0,brake=0;
 if(err<-0.15){
   brake=clamp((-err)/10.8,.06,1);
   // No hacemos trail-braking agresivo: primero buscamos robustez y después ritmo.
   const turnRelease=clamp(Math.abs(headingErr)*.74+curveLoad*.12,0,.46);
   brake*=1-turnRelease*.40;
 }else if(err>3.4)throttle=1;
 else if(err>1.0)throttle=clamp(.30+err*.16,.30,.82);
 else if(err>0)throttle=.18;
 if(throttle>0){
   const slip=Math.max(Math.abs(player.slipF||0),Math.abs(player.slipR||0));
   const reserve=1-clamp(steerLoad*.24+slip*1.45+curveLoad*.055,0,.58);
   throttle*=clamp(reserve,.42,1);
   if(plan&&plan.d<24&&Math.abs(plan.k)>.0024)throttle=Math.min(throttle,.76+.16*skill);
   if(techDrive){
     const turnSwap=technical.changeD<24;
     throttle=Math.min(throttle,turnSwap?.46:(technical.chicane?.66:.74));
   }else if(compound.active&&compound.firstD<42){
     throttle=Math.min(throttle,lerp(.82,.68,compound.severity));
   }
 }
 throttle*=stability.throttleScale;
 brake=Math.max(brake,stability.brakeFloor);
 if(raceTraffic.active){
   if(raceTraffic.launchProtect&&!raceTraffic.escapeAllowed)throttle=Math.min(throttle,raceT<3.0?.82:raceT<6.5?.91:.96);
   if(raceTraffic.packRisk>.55&&!raceTraffic.escapeAllowed)throttle=Math.min(throttle,.84);
   if(raceTraffic.ahead&&raceTraffic.ahead.d<6.2&&raceTraffic.ahead.latAbs<1.9)brake=Math.max(brake,clamp((6.2-raceTraffic.ahead.d)/7*.42,0,.42));
 }
 // Si ya aparece sobreviraje/yaw crítico, descarga el eje trasero en vez de mantener
 // una frenada que lo haga rotar aún más.
 if(stability.brakeRelease>0&&Math.abs(player.yawRate||0)>.18)brake*=1-stability.brakeRelease;
 if(edgeRisk>.03){
   throttle*=1-clamp(edgeRisk*.90,0,.90);
   // v5.117: la protección de borde NO puede mantener el coche bloqueado a baja
   // velocidad. El freno de seguridad sólo interviene cuando todavía hay energía
   // suficiente para que realmente ayude a estabilizar el monoplaza.
   if(sp>8.0&&stability.rearCritical<.25&&stability.yawRisk<.50)brake=Math.max(brake,edgeRisk*.72);
 }

 // Anti-stall de conducción normal. Si AUT tiene un objetivo de velocidad válido pero
 // se queda prácticamente parado sin estar en una maniobra de recuperación, libera
 // completamente el freno y vuelve a lanzar el coche. Esto evita el bucle
 // seguridad→freno→0 km/h→seguridad que podía aparecer en v5.116.
 if(sp<1.35&&target>5.5&&!offTrack){
   if(!player.autoLowSpeedSince)player.autoLowSpeedSince=raceT;
   if(raceT-player.autoLowSpeedSince>.28){
     player.autoLaunchAssistUntil=raceT+.70;
   }
 }else if(sp>3.0){
   player.autoLowSpeedSince=0;
 }
 if((player.autoLaunchAssistUntil||0)>raceT&&!offTrack){
   const th=Math.atan2(trNow.tx,trNow.tz),e0=angleDiff(th,player.heading);
   steer=clamp(e0*2.35+(player.lat-player.autoTargetLat)*.10,-.78,.78);
   throttle=Math.max(throttle,techDrive?(Math.abs(e0)<.62?.38:.22):(Math.abs(e0)<.75?.72:.42));
   brake=0;
   const launchFloor=techDrive?7.5:12;
   if(launchFloor>target+.02){targetReason="LAUNCH_ASSIST";targetReasonDetail="ANTI_STALL";}
   target=Math.max(target,launchFloor);
   player.autoPhase='ARRANCA';
 }
 const straight=plan&&plan.d>145&&Math.abs(curvatureAt(player.s+60))<.0012&&!wallish;
 const attacking=traffic.ahead&&traffic.ahead.d<58;
 const boost=currentSession==='race'&&player.ers>25&&throttle>.84&&((straight&&(attacking||player.ers>66))||(attacking&&plan&&plan.d>80&&player.ers>42));
 if(player.autoPhase!=='ARRANCA'||(player.autoLaunchAssistUntil||0)<=raceT)player.autoPhase=offTrack?'RECUP':stability.risk>.38?'ESTABILIZA':edgeRisk>.16?'SEGURIDAD':techDrive?'TÉCNICA':plan&&plan.d<22?'APEX':plan&&plan.d<95?'FRENO':plan&&plan.d<155?'ENTRADA':'RECTA';
 player.autoBaseTrackTargetSpeed=baseTrackTarget;player.autoPreTrafficTargetSpeed=preTrafficTarget;player.autoTrafficSpeedLimit=Number.isFinite(trafficSpeedLimit)?trafficSpeedLimit:null;player.autoTargetSpeedReason=targetReason;player.autoTargetSpeedReasonDetail=targetReasonDetail;
 player.autoDecisionAfterGeometry=targetBeforeLearning;player.autoDecisionBeforeStability=targetBeforeStability;player.autoDecisionAfterStability=preTrafficTarget;
 player.autoTargetSpeed=target;player.autoLimiterReduction=Math.max(0,baseTrackTarget-target);player.autoRecoveryAssist=false;player.autoRecoveryForcePull=false;player.autoPhysicalNow=autoPhysicalCornerSpeed(player.s);player.autoStabilityRisk=stability.risk;player.autoRearCritical=stability.rearCritical||0;
 return {steer,throttle,brake,target,boost,reverse:false,targetLat:player.autoTargetLat,phase:player.autoPhase};
}

function update(dt,runHeavy=true,heavyDt=dt){
 if(paused||!player)return;
 dt=finiteClamp(dt,.001,.04,.016);
 updateImpactFX(dt);
 if(!physicsFinite())recoverPhysics("PRE");
 player.lastGood=snapshotPhysics();
 if(mode==="countdown"){
   countdown-=dt;
   const ci=countdown>2.8?3:countdown>1.8?2:countdown>.8?1:countdown>0?0:-1;
   $("msg").textContent=ci===3?"3":ci===2?"2":ci===1?"1":ci===0?"¡YA!":"";
   if(ci!==audio.lastCountdown&&ci>=0){soundBeep(ci);audio.lastCountdown=ci}
   updateAudio(dt);if(countdown<=0){mode="race";if(currentSession==='race')apexArmRaceGridTiming();}return;
 }
 if(mode!=="race")return;
 raceT+=dt;apexWeatherUpdate(dt);
 if(player.crossedStart&&(demoMode||assistMode)){player.autoLapSeconds=(player.autoLapSeconds||0)+dt;player.autoUsedThisLap=true;}
 if(currentSession!=='race'&&Number.isFinite(sessionLimit)&&!sessionExpired&&raceT>=sessionLimit){
   sessionExpired=true;
   $("raceAlert").textContent="BANDERA A CUADROS · TERMINA ESTA VUELTA";
   $("raceAlert").style.color="#f4f5f6";
 }

 const rawGasKey=keys.ArrowUp||keys.KeyW||keys.gas,rawBrKey=keys.ArrowDown||keys.KeyS||keys.brake,
       rawLeft=keys.ArrowLeft||keys.left,rawRight=keys.ArrowRight||keys.right;
 const gamepad=!demoMode?apexPollGamepad():null,gamepadActive=!!(gamepad&&gamepad.active);
 const autoSpeedControl=demoMode||assistMode,autoCtl=autoSpeedControl?demoDriverControl():null;
 player.autoRecoveryAssist=!!(autoCtl&&autoCtl.recoveryAssist);player.autoRecoveryForcePull=!!(autoCtl&&autoCtl.recoveryForcePull);player.autoRecoveryHeading=autoCtl&&Number.isFinite(autoCtl.recoveryHeading)?autoCtl.recoveryHeading:null;
 const gasKey=autoSpeedControl?false:rawGasKey,brKey=autoSpeedControl?false:rawBrKey,left=demoMode?false:rawLeft,right=demoMode?false:rawRight;
 const gyroFresh=!demoMode&&gyro.enabled&&performance.now()-gyro.last<1200;if(gyroFresh)apexInputDevice='gyro';
 const gyroGas=gyroFresh?clamp(gyro.throttle||0,0,1):0,gyroBrake=gyroFresh?clamp(gyro.brake||0,0,1):0,gpGas=gamepadActive?gamepad.gas:0,gpBrake=gamepadActive?gamepad.brake:0;
 const analogGas=Math.max(gyroGas,gpGas),analogBrake=Math.max(gyroBrake,gpBrake);
 const gas=autoSpeedControl?(autoCtl&&autoCtl.throttle>.02):(!!gasKey||analogGas>.02),br=autoSpeedControl?(autoCtl&&autoCtl.brake>.02):(!!brKey||analogBrake>.02);
 const inputSpeed=finite(player.speed,0),assistRecovery=!!(assistMode&&player.autoRecovery);

 if(demoMode||assistRecovery){
   player.reverse=!!(autoCtl&&autoCtl.reverse);player.reverseHold=0;player.gyroNeutral=false;
 }else if(assistMode){
   player.reverse=false;player.reverseHold=0;player.gyroNeutral=false;
 }else if((gyroFresh||gamepadActive)&&!gasKey&&!brKey){
   const analogReversePress=analogBrake>.22&&analogGas<.06&&inputSpeed<1.20;
   player.gyroNeutral=!player.reverse&&analogBrake>.08;
   if(!player.reverse){
     if(analogReversePress){player.reverseHold=(player.reverseHold||0)+dt;if(player.reverseHold>=.38){player.reverse=true;player.reverseHold=.38;player.gyroNeutral=false;}}
     else player.reverseHold=Math.max(0,(player.reverseHold||0)-dt*2.6);
   }else if(analogGas>.16&&inputSpeed<1.10){player.reverse=false;player.reverseHold=0;}
 }else{
   player.gyroNeutral=false;
   if(!player.reverse){
     if(brKey&&!gasKey&&inputSpeed<1.65){player.reverseHold=(player.reverseHold||0)+dt;if(player.reverseHold>=.42){player.reverse=true;player.reverseHold=.42;}}
     else player.reverseHold=Math.max(0,(player.reverseHold||0)-dt*2.6);
   }else if(gasKey&&inputSpeed<1.15){player.reverse=false;player.reverseHold=0;}
 }
 const boost=(demoMode?!!(autoCtl&&autoCtl.boost):(apexControls.ersActive||keys.ers))&&player.ers>0&&!player.reverse;
 let steer=demoMode?(autoCtl?autoCtl.steer:0):((left?-1:0)+(right?1:0));
 if(!demoMode&&!left&&!right&&gamepadActive)steer=gamepad.steer;
 else if(!demoMode&&!left&&!right&&gyroFresh)steer=gyro.steer;
 // En modo naranja el jugador conserva el volante. Sólo una recuperación extrema
 // puede tomar temporalmente la dirección para liberar el coche.
 if(assistRecovery&&autoCtl)steer=autoCtl.steer;
 const speedKph=inputSpeed*3.6,speedNorm=clamp((speedKph-65)/200,0,1),autoSteerActive=demoMode||assistRecovery,inputDevice=gamepadActive?'gamepad':(gyroFresh?'gyro':apexInputDevice),driveProfile=apexDriveProfile();
 const steerCfg=autoSteerActive?null:apexDriveSteeringConfig(speedNorm,inputDevice),steerAuthority=autoSteerActive?1:steerCfg.authority;
 let steerCmd=clamp(steer,-1,1)*steerAuthority;
 // Contravolante de estabilidad: corrige sólo el inicio de un sobreviraje. No busca la trazada.
 if(!autoSteerActive&&driveProfile.stability&&driveProfile.counterAssist&&inputSpeed>12){
   const slip=Math.abs(player.slipR||0),yaw=Math.abs(player.yawRate||0),risk=clamp((slip-.035)/.16+(yaw-.20)/1.2,0,1);
   if(risk>0){const corr=clamp(-((player.slipAngle||0)*1.75+(player.yawRate||0)*.18),-.24,.24)*risk;steerCmd=clamp(steerCmd+corr,-steerAuthority,steerAuthority);}
 }
 const correcting=steerCmd!==0&&((player.yawRate||0)*steerCmd<0||(player.slipAngle||0)*steerCmd<0);
 const autoTech=autoSteerActive&&!!player.autoTechnicalActive;
 const steerRate=autoSteerActive?(steerCmd===0?(autoTech?13.5:10.5):(autoTech?(correcting?14.0:12.2):(correcting?10.8:lerp(8.9,5.15,speedNorm)))):(steerCmd===0?steerCfg.center:(correcting?steerCfg.counter:steerCfg.rise));
 player.steer+=clamp(steerCmd-player.steer,-steerRate*dt,steerRate*dt);player.steer=clamp(player.steer,-1,1);
 let throttleTarget,brakeTarget
